tathā anye svān svān prabhāvān sakala amara indrāḥ tadā svayam vṛtraripuḥ sura indraiḥ sureśvaraḥ sarva samṛddhi hetuḥ sureśvaram yakṣam uvāca kaḥ vā bhavān iti ittham sa kutūhala ātmā tadā hi adṛśyam gataḥ eva yakṣaḥ tadā aṃbikā haimavatī śubha āsyā umā śubhaiḥ ābharaṇaiḥ anekaiḥ su śobhamānā tu anu ca āvirāsīt tām śakra mukhyāḥ bahu śobhamānām umām ajām haimavatīm apṛcchan kim etat īśe bahu śobhamāne vā aṃbīke yakṣa vapuḥ cakāsti niśamya tat yakṣam umā ambikā āha tu agocaraḥ ca iti surāḥ sa śakraāḥ praṇemuḥ enām mṛgarāja gāminīm umām ajām lohita śukla kṛṣṇām saṃbhāvitā sā sakala amara indraiḥ sarva pravṛttiḥ tu sura asurāṇām aham purā āsam prakṛtiḥ ca puṃsaḥ yakṣasya ca ājñā vaśa gā iti atha āha tasmāt dvijāḥ sarvam ajasya tasya niyogataḥ ca aṇḍam abhūt ajāt vai ajaḥ ca aṇḍāt akhilam ca tasmāt jyotiḥ gaṇaiḥ lokam aja ātmakam tat jyotiḥ gaṇa pracāram vai saṃkṣipya aṇḍe bravīmi aham dakṣiṇe bhānu putrasya varuṇasya ca vāruṇī saumye somasya vipulā tāsu digdevatāḥ sthitāḥ sarvaiḥ sa āyamanaiḥ sauraḥ hi udayaḥ dṛśyate dvijāḥ saḥ eva sukhavatyām tu niśāta sthaḥ pradṛśyate astam eti punar sūryaḥ vibhāyām viśva dṛś vibhuḥ mayā proktā umarāvatyām yathā asau vāritaskaraḥ tathā saṃyamanīm prāpya sukhām ca eva vibhām khagaḥ yadā aparāhṇaḥ tu āgnoyyām pūrvāhṇaḥ naiṛte dvijāḥ tadā tu apararātraḥ ca vāyu bhāge su dāruṇaḥ īśānyām pūrvarātraḥ tu gatiḥ eṣā ca sarvatas mānasottara śaile tu mahā tejāḥ vibhāvasuḥ bāhyam ca abhyantaram proktam uttarāyaṇa dakṣiṇe pradīpayan sahasrāṃśuḥ agratas pṛṣṭhatas api adhas ūrdhvatas ca karam tyaktvā sabhām brāhmīm anuttamām aṃbhobhiḥ munibhiḥ tyaktaiḥ saṃdhyāyām tu niśācarān hatvā hatvā tu saṃprāptān brahmaṇaiḥ carate raviḥ traya uśa ardham ṛkṣāṇi naktam dvādaśabhiḥ raviḥ auttānapādaḥ bhramati grahaiḥ sārdham adhiṣṭhitaḥ punar tena bhānuḥ tu ādāya tiṣṭhati kiraṇaiḥ sarvatas toyam devaḥ vai sa samīraṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ auttānapādena ca āptam tātasya hetunā āpaḥ pītāḥ tu sūryeṇa kramante śaśinaḥ kramāt niśākarāt nisnavante jīmūtān prati apaḥ kramāt vṛndam jalamucām ca eva śvasanena abhitāḍitam kṣmāyām sṛṣṭim visṛjate abhāsayat tena bhāskaraḥ hitāya sarva jantūnām gatiḥ sarva ima nirmitā bhūḥ bhuvaḥ svar tathā hi apaḥ hi annam ca amṛtam eva ca prāṇāḥ vai jagatām āpaḥ bhūtāni bhuvanāni ca apām śivasya bhagavān ādhipatye vyavasthitaḥ nārāyaṇa tvam devasya hareḥ ca adbhiḥ kṛtam vibhoḥ jagatām ālayaḥ viṣṇuḥ tu āpaḥ tasya ālayāni tu yāḥ yāḥ ūrdhvam mārutena īritāḥ vai tāḥ tāḥ tu abhrāṇi agninā vāyunā ca atas dhūma agni vātānām saṃyogaḥ tu abhram ucyate vārīṇi varṣati iti abhram abhrasya īśaḥ sahasradṛś yajña dhūma udbhavam ca api dvijānām hita kṛt sadā dāva agni dhūma saṃbhūtam abhram vana hitam smṛtam abhicāra agni dhūma uttham bhūtanāsāya vai dvijāḥ evam dhūma viśeṣeṇa jāgatām vai hita ahitam tasmāt ācchādayet dhūmam abhicāra kṛtam naraḥ an ācchādya dvijaḥ kuryāt dhūmam yaḥ ca ābhicārikam apām nidhānam jīmūtāḥ ṣaṣ māsān iha suvratāḥ varṣayanti eva jagatām hitāya pavana ājñayā tridhā teṣām iha utpattiḥ abhrāṇām muni puṅgavāḥ kāṣṭhāḥ vā ahnāḥ ca vairiñcyāḥ pakṣāḥ śca eva pṛthagvidhāḥ ājyānām kāṣṭha saṃyogāt agneḥ dhūmaḥ pravartitaḥ dvitīyānām ca saṃbhūtiḥ viriñca ucchvāsa vāyunā vāhneyāḥ tu atha jīmūtāḥ tu āvaha sthāna gāḥ śubhāḥ pakṣajāḥ puṣkara ādyāḥ ca varṣanti ca yadā jalam mūkāḥ sa śabda duṣṭa āśāḥ tu etaiḥ kṛtyam yathākramam kṣāma vṛṣṭi pradāḥ dīrgha kālam śīta samīriṇaḥ jīvakāḥ ca tathā kṣīṇāḥ vidyut dhvani vivarjitāḥ tiṣṭhanti ākrośa mātre tu dharā pṛṣṭhāt itas tatas ardha krośe tu sarve vai jīmūtāḥ giri vāsinaḥ meghāḥ yojana mātram tu sādhya tvāt brahu toya dāḥ dharā pṛṣṭhāt dvijāḥ kṣmāyām vidyut guṇa samanvitāḥ teṣām teṣām vṛṣṭi sargam tredhā kathitam atra tu pakṣajāḥ kalpa jāḥ sarve parvatānām mahattamāḥ kalpa ante te ca varṣanti rātrau nāśāya śāradāḥ pakṣajāḥ puṣkara ādyāḥ ca varṣanti ca yadā jalam tadā arṇavam abhūt sarvam tatra śete niśi īśvaraḥ āgneyānām śvāsa jānām pakṣajānām dvijarṣabhāḥ jaladānām sadā dhūmaḥ hi āpyāḥ yanaḥ iti smṛtaḥ pauṇḍrāḥ tu vṛṣṭayaḥ sarvāḥ vaidyutāḥ śīta śasya dāḥ puṇḍra deśeṣu patitāḥ nāgānām śīkarāḥ himāḥ gāṅgā gaṅgā ambu saṃbhūtā parjanyena parāvahaiḥ nagānām ca nadīnām ca diggajānām samākulam meghānām ca pṛthak bhūtam jalam prāyāt agāt agam parāvahaḥ uyaḥ śvayanaḥ ca ānayati ambikā gurum vṛṣṭayaḥ kathitāḥ hi adya dvidhā vaḥ tu vivṛddhaye sraṣṭā bhānuḥ mahā tejāḥ vṛṣṭīnām viśva dṛś vibhuḥ saḥ api sākṣāt dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ ca īśānaḥ paramaḥ śivaḥ cakṣuḥ śrotram manaḥ mṛtyuḥ ātmā manyuḥ vidiś diśaḥ satyam ṛtam tathā vāyuḥ aṃbaram khacaraḥ ca saḥ lokapālaḥ hariḥ brahmā rudraḥ sākṣāt maheśvaraḥ sahasrakiraṇaḥ śrīmān aṣṭa hastaḥ su maṅgalaḥ ardha nāri vapuḥ sākṣāt tra netraḥ tridaśādhipaḥ asya eva iha prasādāt tu vṛṣṭiḥ nānā bhavat dvijāḥ jalasya nāśaḥ vṛddhiḥ vā na asti eva asya vicārataḥ sūtaḥ uvāca sauram saṃkṣepataḥ vakṣye ratham śaśinaḥ eva ca grahaṇām itareṣām ca yathā gacchati ca aṃbupaḥ saṃvatsarasya avayavaiḥ kalpitaḥ ca dvijarṣabhāḥ triṇā tu cakreṇa pañca āreṇa samanvitaḥ sauvarṇaḥ sarva devānām āvāsaḥ bhāskarasya tu prerakaḥ jyotiṣām dhīmān dhruvaḥ vai vāta raśmibhiḥ devāḥ ca eva tathā nityam munayaḥ ca divāniśam āpyāyayanti ca ādityam tejobhiḥ bhāskaram śivam madhuḥ ca mādhavaḥ ca eva śukraḥ ca śuciḥ eva ca ete dvādaśa māsāḥ tu varṣam vai mānuṣam dvijāḥ vāsaṃtikaḥ tathā graiṣmaḥ śubhaḥ vai vārṣikaḥ tathā śāradaḥ ca himaḥ ca eva śaiśiraḥ ṛtavaḥ smṛtāḥ tvaṣṭā viṣṇuḥ pulastyaḥ ca pulahaḥ ca atriḥ eva ca vasiṣṭhaḥ ca aṅgigaḥ ca eva bhṛguḥ buddhimatām varaḥ jamadagniḥ kauśikaḥ ca vāsukiḥ kaṅkaṇī karaḥ takṣakaḥ ca tathā nāgaḥ elāpatraḥ tathā dvijāḥ śaṅkhapālaḥ tathā ca anyaḥ tu airāvataḥ iti smṛtaḥ hāhā hūhūḥ muni śreṣṭhāḥ viśvāvasuḥ anuttamaḥ citrasenaḥ mahā tejāḥ ca ūrṇāyuḥ ca eva suvratāḥ dhṛtarāṣṭraḥ sūryavarcāḥ devī sākṣātkṛtasthalā śubha ānanā śubha śroṇiḥ divyā vai puñjikasthalā pūrvacittiḥ iti khyātā devī sākṣāt tilottamā raṃbhā ca aṃbhoja vadanā rathakṛt grāmaṇīḥ śubhaḥ varuṇaḥ ca tathā eva anyaḥ suṣeṇaḥ senajit śubhaḥ sarpaḥ vyāghraḥ punar ca apaḥ vātaḥ vidyut divākaraḥ dhātṛ ādi viṣṇu paryantāḥ devāḥ dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ ādityam paramam bhānum bhābhiḥ āpyāyayanti te nāgāḥ ca aśvatara antāḥ tu vāsuki pramukhāḥ śubhāḥ dvādaśa eva mahādevam vahanti evam yata ākramam krameṇa sūrya varcā antāḥ tuṃburu pramukha aṃbupam gītaiḥ enam upāsaṃte gandharvāḥ dvādaśa uttamāḥ kṛtasthalā ādyāḥ raṃbhā antāḥ divyāḥ ca apsarasaḥ ravim tāṇḍavaiḥ sarasaiḥ sarvāḥ ca upāsaṃte yathākramam divyāḥ satyajit antāḥ ca grāmaṇyaḥ rathakṛt mukhāḥ dvādaśā asya krameṇa eva kurvate bhīṣu saṃgraham prayānti yajñopeta antāḥ rakṣoheti mukhāḥ saha sa āyudhāḥ dvādaśa eva ete rākṣasāḥ ca yathākramam subāhu nāmā rathacitraḥ ca tau ubhau dhanañjayaḥ irāvān ca suruciḥ sa parāvasuḥ ghṛtācī ca apsaraḥ śreṣṭhā viśvācī ca ati śobhanā brahmopetaḥ tathā rakṣaḥ yajñopetaḥ ca yaḥ smṛtaḥ saṃkṣepāt vistarāt ca eva yata āvṛttam yathāśrutam sūtaḥ uvāca kramate śukla pakṣa ādau bhāskarāt param āsthitaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca vāri tejaḥ mayaḥ ca atha piśaṅgaiḥ ca eva śobhanaiḥ daśabhiḥ ca akṛśaiḥ aśvaiḥ nānā varṇaiḥ rathaḥ smṛtaḥ śukrasya kṣmā mayaiḥ yuktaḥ daitya ācāryasya dhīmataḥ pāda hīnau vakra saurī tathā ā ayām apramām atas tam viṣṇu lokam paramam jñātvā mucyeta kilbiṣāt ṛtavaḥ ṣaṭ smṛtāḥ sarve samāgacchanti pañcadhā evam saṃkṣipya kathitam gamanam dvijāḥ abhiṣiktaḥ sahasrāṃśuḥ rudreṇa tu yathā guhaḥ tasmāt graha arcanā kāryā agnau codyam yatāvidhi ādityagraha pīḍāyām sadbhiḥ kārya artha siddhaye ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ abhyaṣiñcat katham brahmā ca ādhipatye prajāpatiḥ sūtaḥ uvāca ṛkṣāṇām oṣadhīnām ca somam brahmā prajāpatiḥ apām ca varuṇam deva dhanānām yakṣa puṅgavam dharmam pitṝṇām adhipam nirṛtim piśitāsinām rudram paśūnām bhūtānām nandīnām gaṇanāyakam vīrāṇām varibhadram ca piśācānām bhayaṅkaram vighnānām vyoma jam devam gajasyam tu vināyakam strīṇām devīm umā devīm vacasām ca sarasvatīm viṣṇum māyāvinām ca eva svatmānam jagatām tathā himavantam girīṇām tu nadīnām ca eva jāhnavīm samudrāṇām ca sarveṣām adhipam payasām nidhim vṛkṣāṇām ca eva ca aśvattham plakṣam ca prapitāmahaḥ siṃham mṛgāṇām vṛṣabham gāvām ca mṛga adhipānām śarabham cakāra senā adhipānām guham aprameyam śrutī smṛtīnām lakulīśam īśam abhyaṣiñcat sudharmāṇam tathā śaṅkhāpadam diśām pṛthivyām pṛthum īśānam sarveṣām tu maheśvaram catur mūrtiṣu sarvajñam śaṅkaram vṛṣabhadhvajam prasādāt bhagavān śambhoḥ ca abhyaṣiñcat yathākramam purā abhiṣicya puṇya ātmā rarāja bhuvaneśvaraḥ abhiṣiktāḥ tatas tu ete viśiṣṭāḥ viśvayoninā sūtaḥ uvāca ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ jale ca abjaḥ samāviṣṭaḥ na adbhiḥ praśāmyati saptabhiḥ tapate mitraḥ tvaṣṭā smṛtaḥ nakṣatra adhipatiḥ somaḥ nayanam vāmam īśituḥ nayanam ca eva mīśasya dakṣiṇam bhāskaraḥ svayam teṣām janānām loke asmin nayanam nayate yatas sūtaḥ uvāca jagat pratāpanam ṛte bhāskaram rudra rūpiṇam dṛśyante divi tāḥ sarvāḥ viśvam ca idam punar jagat sūtaḥ uvāca abhimāninaḥ avatiṣṭhante devāḥ sthānam punar punar pūrvam eva samākhyātā gatiḥ teṣām yathākramam pañca ete hetavaḥ jñeyāḥ jyotiḥ māna avinirṇaye ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ katham viṣṇoḥ prasādāt vai dhruvaḥ buddhimatām varaḥ meḍhī bhūtaḥ grāhāṇām vai vaktum arhasi sāṃpratam sūtaḥ uvāca etam artham mayā pṛṣṭaḥ nānā śāstra viśāradaḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ purā prāha mahyam śuśrūṣave dvijāḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca uttānapādaḥ rājā vai pālayāmāsa medinīm agra jāyām abhūt putraḥ sunītyām tu mahā yaśāḥ dhruvaḥ nāma mahā prājñaḥ kula dīpaḥ mahāmatiḥ suruciḥ tam vinirdhūya sva putram prīti mānasā nyaveśayat tam vipra indrāḥ hi aṅkam mānitā a labdhvā sa pituḥ dhīmān aṅkam duḥkhita mānasaḥ mātuḥ samīpam āgamya ruroda sa punar punar rudantam putram āha idam mātā śoka pariplutā suruciḥ dayitā bhartuḥ tasyāḥ putraḥ api tādṛśaḥ mama tvam mandabhāgyāyāḥ jātaḥ putraḥ upi abhāgyavān kim śocasi kimartham tvam rodamānaḥ punar punar santapta hṛdayaḥ bhūtvā mama śokam kariṣyasi svastha sthānam dhruvam putra sva saktyā tvam samāpnuyāḥ viśvāmitram tatas dṛṣṭvā praṇipatya yathāvidhi sarveṣām upari sthānam kena prāpsyāmi sattama pituḥ aṅke samāsīnam mātā mām suruciḥ mune vyadhūnayat sa tam rājā pitā na uvāca kiñcana etasmāt kāraṇāt brahyan trastaḥ uham mātaram gataḥ sunītiḥ āha me mātā mā akṛthāḥ śokam uttamam tasyāḥ hi vacanam śrutvā sthānam tava mahā mune prāptaḥ vanam idam brahman adya tvām dṛṣṭavān prabho rāja putra śṛṇuṣva idam sthānam uttamam āpsyasi dakṣiṇa aṅga bhavam śaṃbhoḥ mahādevasya dhīmataḥ iṣṭa dam paramam śuddham pavitram amalam param dhyāyan sanātanam viṣṇum japa homa parāyaṇaḥ prāc mukhaḥ niyataḥ bhūtvā jajāpa prīta mānasaḥ vetālāḥ rākṣasāḥ ghorāḥ siṃha ādyāḥ ca mahā mṛgāḥ tam abhyayus mahātmānam buddhi mohāya bhīṣaṇāḥ sunīti rasya yā mātā tasyāḥ rūpeṇa saṃvṛtā piśāci samanuprāptā ruroda bhṛśa duḥkha tā mām anāthām apahāya tapaḥ āsthitavān asi evamādīni vākyāni bhāṣamāṇām mahā tapāḥ a nirīkṣya eva hṛṣṭa ātmā hareḥ nāma jajāpa saḥ āyayau bhagavān viṣṇuḥ dhruvantikam arāti hā tatas sa paramam jñānam avāpya kuruṣottamam tuṣṭāva prāñjaliḥ bhūtvā sarva loka īśvaram harim sthānam dhruvam samāsādya dyotiṣām agra bhuj bhava mātrā tvam sahitaḥ tatra jyotiṣām sthānam āpnuhi tapasā ārādhya deveśam purā labdham hi śaṅkarāt viṣṇoḥ ājñām puraskṛtya jyotiṣām sthānam āptavān evam dhruvaḥ mahā tejāḥ dvādaśa akṣara vidyayā sūtaḥ uvāca tasmāt yaḥ vāsudevāya praṇāmam kurute naraḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca kadrūḥ tviṣā danuḥ tadvat tāsām putrān vadāmi vaḥ saudāminīm tathā kanyām sarva loka bhayaṅkarīm ete tu kāśyapeyāḥ ca saṃkṣepāt parikīrtitāḥ mānavasya nariṣyantaḥ putraḥ āsīt damaḥ kila bṛhaspateḥ śubhā kanyā nāmnā vai devavarṇinī puṣpotkaṭā hi ajanayat putrān tasmāt dvijottamāḥ vibhīṣaṇa uti śuddha ātmā dharma jñaḥ parikīrtitaḥ atreḥ patnyaḥ daśa eva āsan suṃdaryaḥ ca pativratāḥ balābalā ca vipra indrāḥ yā ca gopābalā smṛtā śyāvaḥ ca pratvasaḥ ca eva vavalguḥ ca atha gahvaraḥ rakṣasā bhakṣite śaktau rudhireṇa tu vai tadā śvetaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ ca gauraḥ ca śyāmaḥ dhūmraḥ tathā aruṇaḥ nīlaḥ bādarikaḥ ca eva sarve ca ete parāśarāḥ triloka dhāraṇe śaktāḥ deva ṛṣi kula saṃbhavāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ vāsiṣṭhaḥ vadatām śreṣṭha sūta vaktum iha arhasi sūtaḥ uvāca rākṣasaḥ rudhiraḥ nāma vasiṣṭhasya sutam purā śaktim sa bhakṣayāmāsa śakteḥ śāpāt saha anujaiḥ vasiṣṭha yājyam vipra indrāḥ tadā diśya eva bhūpatim kalmāṣapādam rudhiraḥ viśvāmitreṇa coditaḥ bhakṣitaḥ saḥ iti śrutvā vasiṣṭhaḥ tena rakṣasā śaktiḥ śaktimatām śreṣṭhaḥ bhrātṛbhiḥ saha dharma vid naṣṭam kulam iti śrutvā martum cakre matim tadā smaran putra śatam ca eva śakti jyeṣṭham ca śaktimān na tam vinā aham jīviṣye iti niścitya duḥkhitaḥ dharādharasya eva tadā dharāyām papāta patnyā sahasā aśru dṛṣṭiḥ dharādharāt tam patitam dharā tadā dadhāra tatra api vicitra kaṇṭhī kara aṃbujābhyām kari khela gāminī rudantam ādāya ruroda sā ca tadā tasya snuṣā prāha patnī śakteḥ mahā munim vasiṣṭham vadatām śreṣṭham rudantī bhaya vihvalā bhagavan brāhmaṇa śreṣṭha tava devam idam śubham evam uktvā atha dharma jñā karābhyām kamala īkṣaṇā utthāpya śvaśuram natvā netre saṃmṛjya vāriṇā duḥkhitā api paritrātum śvaśuram duḥkhitam tadā arundhatīm ca kalyāṇīm prārthayāmāsa duḥkhitām snuṣā vākyam tatas śrutvā vasiṣṭha utthāya bhū talāt saṃjñām avāpya ca āliṅgya sā papāta su duḥkhitā aṃrudhatī kārabyām tām saṃspṛśya asra ākula īkṣaṇām ruroda muni śārdūlaḥ suta vatsalaḥ atha nābhi aṃbuje viṣṇoḥ yathā tasyāḥ caturmukhaḥ āsīnaḥ garbhaśayyāyām kumāraḥ ṛcam āha saḥ tatas niśamya bhagavān vasiṣṭhaḥ ṛcam ādarāt kena uktam iti saṃcintya tat ātiṣṭhat samāhitaḥ vasiṣṭham āha viśvātmā ghṛṇayā sa ghṛṇā nidhiḥ tava pautra mukha aṃbhoja ādṛś eṣā adya viniḥsṛtā tatas praṇamya śirasā vasiṣṭhaḥ vārija īkṣaṇam adṛśyantyā mahā tejāḥ pasparśa udaram ādarāt lalāpa arundhatī prekṣya tadā asau rudatīm dvijāḥ tava putram imam dṛṣṭvā bho śakte kula dhāraṇam sūtaḥ uvāca evam uktvā rudan vipraḥ āliṅgya arundhatīm tadā papāta tāḍayantī iva svasya kukṣī karema vai adṛsyantī jaghāna atha śakti jasya ālayam śubhā sva udaram duḥkhitā bhūmau lalāpa ca papāta ca arundhati tadā bhītā vasiṣṭhaḥ ca mahāmatiḥ samutthāpya snuṣām bālām ūcatuḥ bhaya vihvalau tava ātmajam śakti sutam ca dṛṣṭvā ca āsvādya vaktra amṛtam ārya sūnoḥ trātum yatas deham imam muni indraḥ su niścitaḥ pāhi tatas śarīram sūtaḥ uvāca evam snuṣām upālabhya munim ca arundhatī sthitā jīvitam rakṣa dehasya dhātrī ca kuru yāt hitam adṛśyanti uvāca mayā yadi muni śreṣṭhaḥ trātum vai niścitam svakam mama aśubham śubham deham kathañcid pālayāmi aham priya duḥkham aham prāpta hi asatī na atra saṃśayaḥ mune duḥkhāt aham dagdhā yatas putrī mune tava ahodbhutam mayā dṛṣṭam duḥkha pātrī hi aham vibho tathā api bhartṛ rahitā dīnā nāri bhavet iha pāhi mām tatas ārya indra paribhūtā bhaviṣyati pitā mātā ca putrāḥ ca pautrāḥ śvaśuraḥ eva ca ete na bāndhavāḥ strīṇām bhartā bandhuḥ parā gatiḥ tat api atra mṛṣā hi āsīt gataḥ śaktiḥ aham sthitā patim prāṇa samam tyaktvā sthitā yatra kṣaṇam yatas vasiṣṭha aśvattham āśritya hi amṛtā tu yathā latā nirmūlā api amṛtā bhartrā atyaktā dīnā sthitā api aham snuṣā vākyam niśamya eva vasiṣṭhaḥ bhāryayā saha tadā cakre matim dhīmān yātum sva āśramam āśramī kṛcchrāt sa bhāryaḥ bhagavān vāsiṣṭhaḥ sva āśramam kṣaṇāt adṛśyantyā ca puṇya ātmā saṃviveśa sa cintayan sā garbham pālayāmāsa kathacid muni puṅgavāḥ kula saṃdhāraṇa arthāya patnī pativratā tatas sā asūta tanayam daśame māsi su prabham śakti patnī yathā śaktim śaktimantam arundhatī asūta sā ditiḥ viṣṇum yatā svāhā guham sutam agnim yathā araṇiḥ patnī śakteḥ sākṣāt parāśaram yadā tadā śaktisūjuḥ avatīrṇaḥ mahī tale śaktiḥ tyaktvā tadā duḥkham pitṝṇām sama tām yayau bhrātṛbhiḥ saha puṇya ātmā ādityaiḥ iva bhāskaraḥ rarāja pitṛ loka sthaḥ vāsiṣṭhaḥ muni puṅgavāḥ jaguḥ tadā ca pitaraḥ nanṛtuḥ ca pitāmahāḥ prapitāmahāḥ ca vipra indrāḥ hi avatīrṇe parāśare puṣkara ādyāḥ ca sasṛjuḥ puṣpa varṣam ca khecarāḥ pureṣu rākṣasānām ca praṇādam viṣamam dvijāḥ āśrama sthāḥ ca munayaḥ samūhuḥ harṣa saṃtatim avatīrṇaḥ yathā hi aṇḍāt bhānuḥ saḥ api parāśaraḥ adṛśyantyāḥ catur vaktraḥ megha jālāt divākaraḥ sukham ca duḥkham abhavat adṛśyantyāḥ tathā dvijāḥ dṛṣṭvā putram patim smṛtvā muneḥ tathā dṛṣṭvā ca tanayam bālā parāśaram ati dyutim lalāpa vihvalā bālā sannakaṇṭhī papāta ca sā parāśaram aho mahāmatim deva dānava gaṇaiḥ ca pūjitam jāta mātram anagham śuci smitā budhya sa aśru nayanā lalāpa ca tyajya dīna vadanām vana antare putra darśana parām imām prabho śakte svam ca sutam paśya bhrātṛbhiḥ saha ṣaṣ mukham yathā maheśvara upaśyat sa gaṇaḥ hṛṣita ānanaḥ śrutvā snuṣām uvāca idam mā arodīḥ iti duḥkhitaḥ ājñayā tasya sā śokam visiṣṭhasya kula aṅganā tyaktvā hi apālayat bālam bālā bāla mṛga īkṣaṇā dṛṣṭvā tām abalām prāha maṅgala ābharaṇaiḥ vinā āsīnām ākulām sādhvīm bāṣpa paryākule kṣaṇām śākteyaḥ uvāca aṃba maṅgala vibhūṣaṇaiḥ vinā deha yaṣṭiḥ anaghe na śobhate mātar mātar katham tyaktvā maṅgala ābharaṇāni vai āsīnā bhartṛ hīnā iva vaktum arhasi śobhane adṛśyantī tadā vākyam śrutvā tasya sutasya sā na kiñcid abravīt putram śubham vā yadi vā itarat adṛśyantīm punar prāha śākteyaḥ bhagavān mama mātar kutra mahā tejāḥ pitā vada vada iti tām śrutvā ruroda sā vākyam putrasya atīva vihvalā śrutvā vasiṣṭhaḥ api papāta bhūmau pautrasya vākyam sa rudan dayāluḥ arundhatī ca āśrama vāsinaḥ tadā muneḥ vasiṣṭhasya muni īśvarāḥ ca śrutvā vasiṣṭhaḥ api papāta bhūmau pautrasya vākyam sa rudan dayāluḥ arundhatī ca āśrama vāsinaḥ tadā muneḥ vasiṣṭhasya muni īśvarāḥ ca bhakṣitaḥ rakṣasā mātuḥ pitā tava mukāt iti parāśaraḥ uvāca kṣaṇena mātar pitaram darśayāmi iti me matiḥ sā niśamya vacanam tadā śubham sa smitā tanayam āha vismitā jñātvā śakti sutasya asya saṃkalpam muni puṅgavaḥ vasiṣṭhaḥ bhagavān prāha pautram dhīmān ghṛṇā nidhiḥ tathā api śṛṇu lokasya kṣayam kartum na ca arhasi rākṣasānām abhāvāya kuru sarvesvara arcanam trailokyam śṛṇu śākteya aparādhyati kim tava rākṣasānām abhāvāya matim cakre mahāmatiḥ adṛśyantīm vasiṣṭham ca praṇamya arundhatīm tatas kṛtvā eka liṅgam kṣaṇikam pāṃsunā muni sānnidhau saṃpūjya śivasūktena tryaṃbakena śubhena ca nīla rudram ca śākteyaḥ tathā rudram ca śobhanam vāmīyam pavamānam ca pañca brahma tathā eva ca hotāram liṅga sūktam ca atharvaśiraḥ eva ca aṣṭāṅgam arghyam rudrāya da tvā abhyarcya yathāvidhi parāśaraḥ uvāca pitā mama mahā tejāḥ bhratṛbhiḥ saha śaṅkara tam dṛṣṭvā bhagavān nudraḥ devīm āha ca śaṅkaraḥ sā ca dṛṣṭvā mahādevī parāśaram aninditā prāha bhartāram īśānam śaṅkaram jagatām umā bhāryām āryām umām prāha tatas hālāhala aśanaḥ rakṣāmi enam dvijam bālam phulla indīvara locanam dadāmi dṛṣṭim mad rūpa darśana kṣamaḥ eṣa vai saḥ api dṛṣṭvā mahādevam ānanda asra āvila īkṣaṇaḥ nipapāta ca hṛṣṭa ātmā pādayoḥ tasya sa ādaram punar bhavānyāḥ pādau ca nandinaḥ ca mahātmanaḥ saphalam jīvitam medya brahma ādyān tān tat āha saḥ rakṣā artham āgataḥ tu adya mama vāla indu bhūṣaṇaḥ pitaram bhrātṛbhiḥ sārdha śāktoyaḥ tu parāśaraḥ bhrātṛbhiḥ sahitam dṛṣṭvā nanāma ca jaharṣa ca tadā vṛṣadhvajaḥ devaḥ sa bhāryaḥ sa gaṇeśvaraḥ śrī devaḥ uvāca adṛśyantīm ca vipra indra vasiṣṭham pitaram tava vasiṣṭham ca tadā śreṣṭham śaktiḥ vai śaṅkara ājñayā mātaram ca mahābhāgām kalyāṇīm pati devatām arundhatīm jagannātha niyogāt prāha śaktimān vāsiṣṭhaḥ uvāca labdham adya ānanam dṛṣṭam tava bāla mama ājñayā adṛśyantīm mahābhāgām rakṣa vatsa mahāmate arundhatīm ca pitaram vasiṣṭham mama sarvadā īpsitam varaya īśānam jagatām prabhavam prabhum gamiṣyāmi abhivandya īśam bhrātṛbhiḥ saha śakaram nirīkṣya bhāryām sadasi jagāma pitaram vaśī gatam dṛṣṭvā atha pitaram tadā abhyarcya eva śaṅkaram dadāha rākṣasānām tu kulam mantreṇa mantra vid tadā āha pautram dharma jñaḥ vasiṣṭhaḥ munibhiḥ vṛtaḥ rākṣasāḥ na aparādhyanti pituḥ te vihitam tathā yaśasaḥ tapasaḥ ca eva krodhaḥ nāśa karaḥ smṛtaḥ vasiṣṭhena tu datta arghyaḥ kṛta āsana parigrahaḥ parāśaram uvāca idam praṇipatya sthitam muniḥ prasādāt vaiṣṇavam cakre purāṇam vai parāśaraḥ ṣaṣ prakāram samasta artha sādhakam jñāna saṃcayam ṣaṣ sāhasra mitam sarvam veda arthena ca saṃyutam caturtham hi purāṇānām saṃhitāsu su śobhanam eṣa vaḥ kathitaḥ sarve vāsiṣṭhānām samāsatas prabhavaḥ śakti sūnoḥ ca prabhāvaḥ muni puṅgavāḥ ṛṣe ūcuḥ āditya vaṃśam somasya vaṃśam vaṃśa vidām vara sūtaḥ uvāca chāyā ca tasmāt suṣuve sāvarṇi bhāskarāt dvijāḥ yam eva tāḍitā sā tu chāyā vai duḥkhitā abhavat pūya śoṇita saṃpūrṇam kṛmīṇām nicaya anvitam saḥ api gokarṇam āśritya phalakena anila aśanaḥ labdhavān devadevasya prabhāvāt śūlapāṇinaḥ rūpam tvāṣṭrī sva dehāt tu chāyā ākhyām sā tu akalpayat vaḍavā rūpam āsthāya tapaḥ tepe tu suvratā kālāt prayatnataḥ jñātvā chāyām chāyāpatiḥ prabhuḥ vaḍavām agamat saṃjñām aśva rūpeṇa bhāskaraḥ vaḍavā ca tadā tvāṣṭrī saṃjñā tasmāt divākarāt suṣuve ca aśvinau devau devānām tu bhiṣaj varau likhitaḥ bhāskaraḥ paścāt saṃjñā pitrā mahātmanā nirmame bhagavān tvaṣṭā pradhānam divyam āyudham rudra prasādāt ca śubham sudarśanam iti smṛtam labdhavān bhagavān cakram kṛṣṇaḥ kālāgni sannabham ilā jyeṣṭhā variṣṭhā ca puṃs tvam prāpa ca yā purā sudyumnaḥ iti vikhyātā puṃs tvam prāptā tu ilā purā mitrāvaruṇayoḥ tu atra prasādāt muni puṅgavāḥ punar śaravaṇam prāpya strī tvam prāptaḥ bhava ājñayā sudyumnaḥ mānavaḥ śrīmān soma vaṃśa pravṛddhaye soma vaṃśa agrajaḥ dhīmān bhava bhaktaḥ pratāpavān ikṣvākoḥ vaṃśa vistāram paścāt vakṣye tapodhanāḥ gayā gayasya ca ākhyātā purī parama śobhanā surāṇām saṃsthitiḥ yasyām pitṝṇām ca sadā sthitiḥ tatas pṛthuḥ muni śreṣṭhāḥ viśvakaḥ pārthivaḥ tathā mucukundaḥ ca puṇya ātmā trayaḥ trailokya viśrutāḥ prasādāt brahya sūnoḥ vai taṇḍinaḥ prāpya siṣyatām dhana hīnaḥ ca dharma ātmā dṛṣṭavān brahmaṇaḥ sutam taṇḍi saṃjñam dvijam tasmāt labdhavān dvijasattamāḥ tatas tasmāt nṛpaḥ labdhvā taṇḍinā kathitam purā nāmnām sahasram japtvā vai gāṇapatyam avāptavān ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ nāmnām sahasram viprāṇām vaktam arhasi śobhanam sūtaḥ uvāca yat japtvā tu muni śreṣṭhāḥ gāṇapatyam avāptavān saṃvatsaraḥ kṛtaḥ mantra prāṇāyāmaḥ parantapaḥ bahu bhūtaḥ bahu dhanaḥ sarva sāraḥ amṛta īśvaraḥ khaṅgī śaṅkhī jaṭī jvālī kha caraḥ dyu caraḥ balī mekhalā ākṛti rūpaḥ ca jala ācāraḥ stutaḥ tathā vīṇī ca paṇavī tālī nālī kalikaṭuḥ tathā vṛkṣaḥ śrī māla karmā ca sarva bandha vimocanaḥ bhukṣuḥ ca bhikṣu rūpī ca raudra rūpaḥ su rūpavān ca bhaga hārī niyantā ca kālaḥ loka pitāmahaḥ itihāsaḥ ca kalpaḥ ca damanaḥ jagat īśvaraḥ nīlaḥ tathā aṅga luptaḥ ca śobhanaḥ nara vigrahaḥ asapatnaḥ prasādaḥ ca pratyayaḥ gītasādhakaḥ āśrama sthaḥ kapota sthaḥ viśvakarmā patiḥ virāj kapilaḥ kalaśaḥ sthūlaḥ āyudhaḥ ca eva romaśaḥ laṃbitaḥ laṃbita uṣṭhaḥ ca laṃba hastaḥ vara pradaḥ prāṇeśaḥ bandhakī vṛkṣaḥ nakulaḥ ca adrikaḥ tathā vasubhṛt sarva bhūta ātmā niścalaḥ su viduḥ budhaḥ citta kāmaḥ jita indriyaḥ gāndhāraḥ ca surāpaḥ ca tāpa karma rataḥ hitaḥ mahāketuḥ dharādhātā na ekatāna rataḥ svaraḥ tāraṇaḥ caraṇaḥ dhātā paridhā paripūjitaḥ girāvāsaḥ visargaḥ ca sarva lakṣaṇa lakṣa vid vāryakṣaḥ kakubhaḥ vajrī dīpta tejāḥ sahasrapād tasmāt labdhvā stavam śaṃbhoḥ nṛpaḥ trailokya viśrutaḥ gaṇa ādhipatyam saṃprāptaḥ taṇḍinaḥ tejasā prabhoḥ saṃvatsaram kramāt japtvā trisaṃdhyam śaṅkara āśrame sūtaḥ uvāca sarva lokeṣu vikhyātaḥ triśaṅkuḥ iti vīryavān vasiṣṭha kopāt puṇya ātmā rājā satyavrataḥ purā viśvāmitraḥ mahā tejāḥ varam dattvā triśaṅkave nābhāgaḥ tasya dāyādaḥ bhava bhaktaḥ pratāpavān rājā daśarathaḥ tasmāt śrīmān ikṣvāku vaṃśa kṛt teṣām śreṣṭhaḥ mahā tejāḥ rāmaḥ parama vīryavān bhārate yaḥ mahā tejāḥ saubhadreṇa nipātitaḥ sarve pāśupate jñānam adhītya parameśvaram samabhyarcya yathājñānam iṣṭvā yajñaiḥ yathāvidhi divam gatāḥ mahātmānaḥ kecid mukta ātma yoginaḥ nṛgaḥ brāhmaṇa śāpena kṛkalāsa tvam āgataḥ ṛtaḥ tasya sutaḥ śrīmān sarva dharma vidām varaḥ kṛtaḥ tasya sudharmā abhūt pṛṣitaḥ nāma viśrutaḥ karūṣasya tu kārūṣāḥ sarve prakhyāta kīrtayaḥ pṛṣitaḥ hiṃsayitvā gām guroḥ prāpa su kalmaṣam diṣṭa putrāḥ tu nābhāgaḥ tasmāt api bhalandanaḥ bhalandanasya rājā āsīt ajavāhanaḥ ikṣvākoḥ putra pautra ādyaḥ elasya atha vadāmi vaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca ailaḥ purūravāḥ nāma rudra bhaktaḥ pratāpavān cakre tu akaṇṭakam rājyam deśe puṇyatame dvijāḥ pratiṣṭhāna adhipaḥ śrīmān pratiṣṭhāne pratiṣṭhitaḥ vijātiḥ ca iti ṣaṭ ime sarve prakhyāta kīrtayaḥ tau ubhau śubha karmāṇau stutau vidyā viśāradau bhava bhaktaḥ tu puṇya ātmā dharma niṣṭhaḥ samañjasaḥ yajña yājī jita krodhaḥ sarva bhūta anukaṃpanaḥ sa loha gandha annar muktaḥ enasā ca mahā yaśāḥ yajñasya avabhṛthe madhye yātaḥ divyaḥ rathaḥ śubhaḥ tasmāt vaṃśāt paribhraṣṭaḥ vasoḥ cedi pateḥ punar sūtaḥ uvāca abhyaṣiñcat purum putram yayātiḥ nāhuṣaḥ prabhuḥ kṛta upakāraḥ tena eva puruṇā dvijasattamāḥ yayātiḥ uvāca śukreṇa me samādiṣṭā devayānyāḥ kṛte jarā prārthitena punar tena jarā saṃcāriṇī kṛtā prakṛtayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca tābhiḥ eva naraḥ śrīmān na anyathā karma koṭi kṛt jīryanti dehinaḥ sarve svabhāvāt eva na anyathā sūtaḥ uvāca śūraḥ ca śūravīraḥ ca śūrasenasya ca anaghāḥ śūrasenāḥ iti khyātāḥ deśāḥ teṣām mahātmanām durjayaḥ kṛṣṇa putraḥ tu babhūva amitra karśanaḥ atha prasūtim icchan vai kuśaṅkuḥ su mahā balaḥ śaśarbaduḥ tu vai rājā anvayāt bratam uttamam tasya putrāṇām anantakam anuttamam uśanāḥ tasya tanayaḥ saṃprāpya tu mahīm imām smṛtaḥ ca uśanasaḥ putraḥ siteṣuḥ nāma pārthivaḥ vīraḥ kaṃbalabarhiḥ tu maruḥ tasya ātmajaḥ smṛtaḥ aśvamedhe tu dharma ātmā ṛtvigbhyaḥ pṛthivīm dadau narmadā tīram ekākī kevalam bhāryayā yutaḥ daśārhe naidhṛtaḥ nāmnā mahā ari gaṇa sūdanaḥ tasmāt karaṃbhaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ devarātaḥ abhavat tatas ekṣvākīm avahat ca aṃśuḥ sattvaḥ tasmāt ajāyata jyāmaghasya mayā proktā sṛṣṭiḥ vai vistareṇa vaḥ yaḥ paṭhet śṛṇuyāt vā api tu sūtaḥ uvāca sāttvataḥ satya saṃpannaḥ prajajñe caturaḥ sutān sṛṃjayyām bhajanāt ca eva bhrājamānāt vijajñire ayutāyuḥ śātayuḥ ca balavān harṣa kṛt smṛtaḥ vadham sahāyaḥ ca siṃhādeva su dāruṇāt gāndinīm nāma kāśyaḥ hi dadau tasmai sva kanyakām varṣa trayam pratidinam gām ekām brāhmaṇāya tu yadi dadyāḥ tatas kukṣeḥ nirgamiṣyāmi aham pitar asyām utpādayāmāsa tanayān tān nibodhata sumitrasya sutaḥ jajñe citrakaḥ ca mahā yaśāḥ pauravī bāhlika sutā saṃpūjyā āsīt suraiḥ api āśritam kaṃsa bhītyā ca sva ātmānam śānta tejasam jāte rāme atha nihate ṣaṅgarbhe ca ati dakṣiṇe puruṣaḥ bhagavān kṛṣṇaḥ dharma mokṣa phala pradaḥ tām kanyām jagṛhe rakṣan kaṃsāt svasya ātmajam tadā rakṣakam jagatām viṣṇum sva icchayā dhṛta vigraham bhū bhāra nigraha artham ca hi avatīrṇam jagadgurum mṛtyuḥ eva na saṃdehaḥ iti vāṇī purātanī tatas tām hantum ārebhe kaṃsaḥ sa ullaṅghya ca aṃbaram devakyāḥ sa bhayāt kaṃsaḥ jaghāna eva aṣṭabham tu iti prabhāvāt muni sārdūlāḥ tayā ca eva jaḍīkṛtaḥ nihatāḥ bahavaḥ ca anye deva brāhmaṇa ghātinaḥ kṛṣṇa putrāḥ samākhyātāḥ kṛṣṇena sadṛśāḥ sutāḥ putreṣu eteṣu sarveṣu cārudeṣṇa ādayaḥ hareḥ viśiṣṭāḥ balavantaḥ ca raukmiṇeya ari sūdanāḥ kṛṣṇasya tāsu sarvāsu priyā jyeṣṭhā ca rukmiṇī uṣyatā vāyubhakṣeṇa putra artham pūjitaḥ haraḥ ete labdhāḥ tu kṛṣṇena śūlapāṇi prasādataḥ ṛṣim dṛṣṭvā tu aṅgirasam praṇipatya janārdanaḥ divyam pāśupatam yogam labdhavān tasya ca ājñayā pralupta śmaśru keśaḥ ca ghṛta aktaḥ muñja mekhalī dīkṣitaḥ bhagavān kṛṣṇaḥ tatāpa ca parantapaḥ tapasā tasya saṃtuṣṭaḥ dadau rudraḥ bahūn varān sa aṃbam jāṃbavatī putram kṛṣṇāya ca mahātmane tathā jāṃbavatī ca eva sāṃbam bhāryā hareḥ sutam bāṇasya ca tadā tena cheditam muni puṅgavāḥ bhujānām ca eva sāhasram śāpāt rudrasya dhīmataḥ tathā duṣṭa kṣitīśānām līlayā eva raṇa ajire saṃhṛtya tad kulam ca eva prabhāse atiṣṭhat acyutaḥ kṛṣṇasya dvārakāyām vai śāpa hāriṇaḥ śāpam piṇḍārake arakṣat vacaḥ durvāsasaḥ tadā tyaktvā ca mānuṣam rūpam jaraka astra chalena tu cauraiḥ ca apahṛtāḥ sarvāḥ tasya māya balena ca mahiṣyaḥ tasya kṛṣṇasya rukmiṇī pramukhāḥ śubhāḥ saha agnim viviśuḥ sarvāḥ kṛṣṇena akliṣṭa karmaṇā revatī ca tathā devī balabhadreṇa dhīmatā praviṣṭā pāvakam viprāḥ sā ca bhartṛ patham gatā pretakāryam hareḥ kṛtvā pārthaḥ parama vīryavān rāmasya ca tathā anyeṣām vṛṣṇīnām api suvrataḥ dravya abhāvāt svaṃyam pārthaḥ bhrātṛbhiḥ ca divam gataḥ evam saṃkṣepataḥ proktaḥ kṛṣṇasya akliṣṭa karmaṇaḥ iti etat soma vaṃśānām nṛpāṇām caritam dvijāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca maheśvaraḥ mahādevaḥ prakṛteḥ puruṣasya ca paratve saṃsthitaḥ devaḥ paramātmā muni īśvarāḥ avyaktam ca īśvarāt tasmāt abhavat kāraṇam param vigrahaḥ sarva bhūtānām īśvara ājñā pracoditam praśānta ghora mūḍha tvāt aviśeṣāḥ tatas punar śarvaḥ ca aṇḍa kapāla sthaḥ bhavaḥ ca aṃbhasi suvratāḥ bhīmaḥ ca avani madhya sthaḥ hi ahaṅkāre maheśvaraḥ aṇḍāt jajñe saḥ eva īśaḥ puruṣa ūrka sama prabhaḥ tasya vāma aṅga jaḥ viṣṇuḥ sarva deva namaskṛtaḥ lakṣmyā devyā hi abhūt devaḥ icchayā parameṣṭhinaḥ dakṣiṇa aṅga bhavaḥ brahmā sarasvatyā jagadguruḥ anupravṛttaḥ tu mahān tat enām ciras tira tvāt viṣayam śriyaḥ svayam brahmā ca bhagavān tasmāt caturvaktraḥ prajāpatiḥ bhagavān bhagavat bhāvāt nirmala tvāt śivaḥ smṛtaḥ āpūrya tābhiḥ ayanam kṛtavān ātmanaḥ yatas te sattvasya ca yogena sṛṣṭāḥ sattva udbhavāḥ smṛtāḥ te ūrdhvasrotasaḥ jñeyāḥ tuṣṭa ātmānaḥ budhaiḥ smṛtāḥ iti eṣa prākṛtaḥ sargaḥ vaikṛtaḥ anavamaḥ smṛtaḥ devān ṛṣīn ca mahataḥ gadataḥ tān nabodhata mahiṣāḥ gavaya akṣāḥ ca plavaṅgāḥ śarabhāḥ vṛkāḥ siṃhaḥ tu saptamaḥ teṣām āraṇyāḥ paśavaḥ smṛtāḥ śata rudrāḥ sama ātmanaḥ yājñikāḥ evam bhavatu bhadram te yathā te vyāhṛtam vibho prāk uktā tu mahā devī strī sā evā iha satī hi abhūt hitāya jagatām devī dakṣeṇa ārādhitā purā rudraḥ paśupatiḥ ca āsīt purā dagdham puratrayam devāḥ ca paśavaḥ sarve babhūvuḥ tasya tejasā sa yāti brahmaṇaḥ lokam śrāvayet vā dvijottamān ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ samāsāt vistarāt ca eva sargaḥ proktaḥ tvayā śubhaḥ katham paśupatiḥ ca āsīt puram dagdhum maheśvaraḥ katham ca paśavaḥ ca āsan devāḥ sa brahmakāḥ prabhoḥ purasya saṃbhavaḥ sarvaḥ vara lābhaḥ purā śrutaḥ yathā śrutam tathā prāha vyāsāt viśva artha sūcakāt sūtaḥ uvāca trailokyasya asya śāpāt hi manaḥ vāc kāya saṃbhavāt daityāḥ ūcuḥ āyasam ca abhavat bhumau puram teṣām mahātmanām mayaḥ ca balavān tatra daitya dānava pūjitaḥ hairaṇye rājate ca eva kṛṣṇāyasa maye tathā ālayam ca ātmanaḥ kṛtvā tava āste balavān tadā evam babhūvuḥ daityānām ati durgāṇi suvratāḥ purāṇi vipra indrāḥ trailokyam iva ca aparam śobhitam tripuram teṣām pṛthak pṛthak anuttamaiḥ kṛtvā api su mahat pāpām apāpaiḥ śaṅkara arcanāt daitya īśvaraiḥ mahābhāgaiḥ sa dāraiḥ sa sutaiḥ dvijāḥ mahādeva itaram tyaktvā devam tasya arcanam sthitaiḥ sarvadā kṣudhitaiḥ ca eva dāva agni sadṛśa īkṣaṇaiḥ nīla adri meru saṃkāśaiḥ nīrada upama niḥsvanaiḥ mayena rakṣitaiḥ sarvaiḥ śikṣitaiḥ yuddha lālasaiḥ sa iṃdrāḥ devāḥ dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ drumāḥ dāva agninā yathā athā evam te tadā dagdhāḥ devāḥ deveśvaram harim kim kāryam deva kāryeṣu bhagavān iti sa prabhuḥ tadā sasmāra vai yajñam yajñamūrtiḥ janārdanaḥ yajvā yajñabhuj īśānaḥ yajvanām phala daḥ prabhuḥ devam te puruṣam ca eva praṇemuḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ tadā śrī viṣṇuḥ uvāca sūtaḥ uvāca siṃha nādam mahat kṛtvā yajñeśam tuṣṭuvuḥ surāḥ punar prāha sa sarvān tān tridaśān tridaśeśvaraḥ hatvā dagdhvā ca bhūtāni bhuktvā ca anyāyataḥ api vā yajet yadi mahādeva na atra saṃśayaḥ apāpāḥ na eva hantavyāḥ pāpāḥ eva na saṃśayaḥ hantavyāḥ sarva yatnena katham vadhyāḥ sura uttamāḥ asurāḥ durmadāḥ pāpāḥ api devaiḥ mahā balaiḥ kaḥ ham brahmā athavā devāḥ daityāḥ deva ari sūdanāḥ yaḥ saptaviṃśakaḥ nityaḥ parātparataraḥ prabhuḥ viśva amara īśvaraḥ vandyaḥ viśvādhāraḥ maheśvaraḥ saḥ eva sarva deva īśaḥ sarveṣām api śaṅkaraḥ dharma niṣṭhāḥ ca te sarve śrauta smārta vidhau sthitāḥ rudram iṣṭvā yathānyāyam jeṣyāmaḥ daitya sattamān sa tārakākṣeṇa mayena guptam svastham ca guptam sphaṭika ābham ekam sūtaḥ uvāca evam uktvā hariḥ ca iṣṭvā yajñena upasadā prabhum upaviṣṭaḥ dadarśa atha bhūta saṃghān sahasraśas prāha devaḥ hariḥ sākṣāt praṇipatya sthitān prabhuḥ viṣṇuḥ uvāca praviśya naṣṭāḥ te sarve śalabhāḥ iva pāvakam tatas tu naṣṭāḥ te sarve bhūtāḥ deveśvara ājñayā tatas parājitāḥ devāḥ dhvasta vīryāḥ kṣaṇena tu sa indrāḥ saṃgamya deveśam upendram dhiṣṭhitāḥ bhayāt kim kṛtyam iti saṃtaptaḥ saṃtaptān sa indrakān kṣaṇam dharmāt aiśvaryam iti eṣā śrutiḥ eṣā sanātanī pūjasā bhoga saṃpattiḥ avaśyam jāyate dvijāḥ sūtaḥ uvāca śāstram ca śāstā sarveṣām akarot kāma rūpadhṛk sarva saṃmohanam māyī dṛṣṭa pratyaya saṃyutam etat sva aṅga bhavāya eva puruṣāya upadiśya tu puratraya vināśāya prāha onam puruṣam hariḥ tatas praṇamya tam māyī māyā śāstra viśāradaḥ praviśya tat puram tūrṇam muniḥ māyām tadā akarot māyayā tasya te daityāḥ puratraya nivāsinaḥ śraitam smārtam ca saṃtyajya tasya śiṣyāḥ tadā abhavan muniḥ śiṣyaiḥ praśiṣyaiḥ ca saṃvṛtaḥ sarvatas svayam strī dharmam ca akarot strīṇām duścāra phala siddhi dam jana āsaktāḥ babhūvuḥ tāḥ vinindya pati devatāḥ nāryaḥ caranti saṃtyajya bhartṝn svairam vṛthā adhamāḥ strīṇām mātā pitā bandhuḥ sakhā mitram ca bāndhavaḥ bhartā eva na saṃdehaḥ tathā api a saha māyayā purā ekāmuni śārdūlāḥ sarva dharmān sadā patim saṃtyajya apūjayan sādhvyaḥ devān anyān jagadgurūn narakam ca jagāma anyā tasmāt bhartā parā gatiḥ tathā api bhartṝn svān tyaktvā babhūvuḥ svaira vṛttayaḥ evam naṣṭe tadā dharme śrauta smārte su śobhane tapasā prāpya sarva jñam tuṣṭāva puruṣottamaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca śāśvatāya hi anantāya avyaktāya ca te namaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca jajāpa rudram bhagavān koṭi vāram jale sthitaḥ sa iṃdrāḥ sa sādhyāḥ sa yamāḥ sa rudrāḥ sa marut gaṇāḥ devāḥ ūcuḥ trātā netā jagati asmin dvijānām dvija vatsala sūtaḥ uvāca sūtaḥ uvāca śrutvā prabhoḥ tadā vākyam praṇemuḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ ca te līlā aṃbujena ca āhatya kalam āha vṛṣadhvajam mukuṭaiḥ kaṭakaiḥ ca eva kuṇḍalaiḥ valayaiḥ śubhaiḥ kalpakadruma jaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śobhitaiḥ alakaiḥ śubhaiḥ hāraiḥ vārīja rāga ādi maṇi citraiḥ tathā aṅgadaiḥ tilakaiḥ ca mahādeva paśya putram su śībhanam vakra vṛndam ca paśya īśa vṛndam kāmalakam yathā netrāṇi ca vibho paśya śubhāni tvam śubhāni ca añjanāni vicitrāṇi maṅgala artham ca mātṛbhiḥ kṛttikā ādyaiḥ svāhayā ca viśeṣataḥ saḥ api līlā alasaḥ bālaḥ nanarta ārti haraḥ prabhuḥ trailokyam akhilam tatra nanarta īśa ājñayā kṣaṇam nāgāḥ ca nanṛtuḥ sarve devāḥ sa iṃdra purogamāḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ gaṇapāḥ skandam mumoda aṃbā ca mātaraḥ avā patuḥ tadā tṛptim nandinā ca gaṇeśvarāḥ viveśa divyam bhavanam bhavaḥ api yathām budaḥ anya aṃbudam aṃbuda ābhaḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ ca mahādevam kiñcid udvigna cetasaḥ bhāgyavantaḥ ca daitya indrāḥ iti ca anye sura īśvarāḥ pūjā phalam imam teṣām iti anye na iti ca apare apatan munayaḥ ca anye devāḥ ca dharaṇī tale dṛṣṭvā api devadeveśam devānām ca asura dviṣām procuḥ namaḥ śivāya iti pūjya ca alpataram hṛdi tatas kapardi nandīśaḥ mahādeva apriyaḥ muniḥ śūlī mālī tathā hālī kuṇḍalī valayī gadī vṛṣam āruhya su śvetam yayau tasya ājñayā tadā tatas vai nandinam dṛṣṭvā gaṇaḥ kuṃbhodaraḥ api saḥ nandī bhāti mahā tejāḥ vṛṣa pṛṣṭe vṛṣadhvajaḥ sa gaṇaḥ gaṇa senā nīḥ megha pṛṣṭhe yathā bhavaḥ sita ātapatram śailādeḥ ākāśam iva bhāti tat tatra antar baddha mālā sā muktāphala mayī śubhā gaṅgā ākāśāt nipatitā bhāti mūrdhni vibhoḥ yathā atha dṛṣṭvā gaṇādhyakṣam deva dundubhayaḥ śubhāḥ niyogāt vajriṇaḥ sarve vineduḥ muni puṅgavāḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ ca gaṇeśānam vāgbhiḥ iṣṭa pradam śubham yathā devāḥ bhavam dṛṣṭvā prīti kaṇṭa kita tvacaḥ niyogāt vajriṇaḥ mūrdhni puṣpa varṣam ca khecarāḥ vṛṣṭyā tuṣṭaḥ tadā reje tuṣṭyā puṣṭyā yathārthayā nandī bhavaḥ cāndrayā tu snātayā gandha vāriṇā puṣpaiḥ nānāvidhaiḥ tatra bhāti pṛṣṭham vṛṣasya tat saṃkīrṇam tu divaḥ pṛṣṭham nakṣatraiḥ iva suvratāḥ kusumaiḥ saṃvṛtaḥ nandī vṛṣa pṛṣṭhe rarāja saḥ divaḥ pṛṣṭhe yathā candraḥ nakṣatraiḥ iva suvratāḥ tam dṛṣṭvā nandinam devāḥ sa indra upendrāḥ tathāvidham tu ṣṭuvuḥ gaṇapa īśānam devadevam iva aparam devāḥ ūcuḥ kūṣmāṇḍa gaṇa nāthāya yoginām pataye namaḥ sarva dāya śaraṇyāya sarvajñāya ārti hāriṇe vajriṇe vajra daṃṣṭrāya vajri vajra nivāriṇe vajra alaṅkṛta dehāya vajriṇā ārādhitāya te raktānām bhava pāda abje rudra loka pradāyine bhūtānām bhuvane śānām pataye pāpa hāriṇe namaḥ śivāya saumyāya rudra bhaktāya te namaḥ sūtaḥ uvacā tatas prītaḥ gaṇādhyakṣaḥ prāha sūtaḥ uvāca nemyaḥ ṣaṣ ṛtavaḥ ca eva tayoḥ vai vipra puṅgavāḥ dharmaḥ virāgaḥ daṇḍaḥ usya daṇḍa āśrayāḥ smṛtāḥ dakṣiṇāḥ saṃdhayaḥ tasya lohāḥ pañcāśat agnayaḥ kṣaddhā ca gatiḥ asya eva vedāḥ tasya hayāḥ smṛtāḥ talāḥ kapotāḥ kāpotāḥ sarve tala nivāsinaḥ meruḥ eva mahā chatram mandaraḥ pārśva ḍiṇḍimaḥ iṣuḥ viṣṇuḥ mahā tejāḥ śalyam somaḥ śarasya ca kālāgniḥ tad śarasya eva sākṣāt tīkṣṇaḥ su dāruṇaḥ evam kṛtvā ratham divyam kārmukam ca śaram tathā sārathim jagatām ca eva brahmāṇam prabhum īśvaram purāṇi uddiśya kha sthāni dānavānām tarasvinām teṣām bhāvam tatas jñātvā devaḥ tān idam abravīt tasmāt vai paśavaḥ sarve deva asura narāḥ prabhoḥ tatas vināyakaḥ sākṣāt bālaḥ abāla parākramaḥ a pūjitaḥ tadā devaiḥ prāha devān nivārayan śrī vināyakaḥ uvāca mām apūjya jagati asmin bhakṣya bhojya ādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ kaḥ pumān siddhim āpnoti devaḥ vā dānavaḥ api vā tatas tasmin kṣaṇāt eva deva kārye sura īśvarāḥ vighnam kariṣye deveśa katham karttum samudyatāḥ tatas sa indrāḥ surāḥ sarve bhītāḥ saṃpūjya tam prabhum abruvan te gaṇeśānam nirvighnam ca astu naḥ sadā āliṅgya ca āghrāya sutam tadānīm apūjayat sarva sura indra mukhyaḥ saṃpūjya pūjyam saha deva saṃghaiḥ vināyakam nāyakam īśvarāṇām tam devadevam sura siddha saṃghāḥ maheśvaram bhūta gaṇāḥ ca sarve gaṇeśvarāḥ nandi mukhāḥ tadānīm sva vāhanaiḥ anvayuḥ īśam īśāḥ agre surāṇām ca gaṇa īśvarāṇām tadā atha nandī girirāja kalpam vimānam āruhya puram prahartum jagāma mṛtyum bhagavān iva īśaḥ yāntam tadānīm tu śilāda putram āruhya nāga indra vṛṣa aśva varyān devāḥ tadānīm gaṇapāḥ ca sarve gaṇāḥ yayuḥ sva āyudha cihna hastāḥ sura asura īśam sahasraraśmiḥ bhagavān su tīkṣṇaḥ rarāja madhye bhagavān surāṇām vivāhanaḥ vārija patra varṇaḥ yathā sumeroḥ sikhara adhirūḍhaḥ sahasraraśmiḥ bhagavān su tīkṣṇaḥ sahasranetraḥ prathamaḥ surāṇām gaja indram āruhya ca dakṣiṇe asya samantataḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ iṣṭa dam te jaya iti śakram vara puṣpa vṛṣṭyā tadā hi ahalyā upapatim sureśam jagatpatim diviṣṭhāḥ apām patiḥ tathā īśānaḥ bhavam ca anusamāgatāḥ vṛṣabha indram samāruhya roma jaiḥ ca samāvṛtaḥ vāyavyām sa gaṇaiḥ sa arvam sevān cakre rathasya tu ṣaṇmukhaḥ api saha siddha cāraṇaiḥ senayā ca girirāja saṃnibhaḥ deva nātha gaṇa vṛnda saṃvṛtaḥ vāraṇena ca tathā agni saṃbhavaḥ vighnam gaṇeśaḥ upi asura īśvarāṇām kṛtvā surāṇām bhagavān avighnam vighna īśvaraḥ vighna gaṇaiḥ ca sārdham tam deśam īśāna padam jagāma kālī tadā kālaniśā prakāśam śūlam kapāla ābharaṇā kareṇa prakaṃpayantī ca tadā suredrān mahā asura asṛj madhu pāna mattā matta ibha gāmī mada lola netrā mattaiḥ piśācaiḥ ca gaṇaiḥ ca mattaiḥ matta ibha carma aṃbara voṣṭita aṅgī yayau purastāt ca gaṇeśvarasya tām siddha gandharva piśāca yakṣa vidyādhara ahi indra sura indra mukhyāḥ mātaraḥ sura vari sūdanāḥ sādaram sura gaṇaiḥ surūjitāḥ durga ārūḍha mṛga adhipā duratigā dos daṇḍa vṛndaiḥ śivā bibhrāṇā aṅkuśa śula pāśa paraśum cakra asi śaṅkha āyudham gajaiḥ hayaiḥ siṃha varaiḥ rathaiḥ ca vṛṣaiḥ yayuḥ te gaṇa rāja mukhyāḥ halaiḥ ca phaālaiḥ musalaiḥ bhuśurṃbhuśuṇḍaiḥ girīndra kūṭaiḥ giri sannibhāḥ te yayuḥ purastāt hi maheśvarasya sura īśvarāḥ bhūta gaṇa īśvarāḥ ca tathā indra padmodbhava viṣṇu mukhyāḥ surāḥ gaṇa īśāḥ ca gaṇeśam īśam jaya iti vāgbhiḥ bhagavantam ūcuḥ kirīṭa datta añjalayaḥ samantāt yamajihvaḥ śatāśvaḥ ca kaṇṭhanaḥ kaṇṭhapūjanaḥ muṇḍa ūrdha muṇḍaḥ dīrghaḥ ca piśācāsyaḥ pinākadhṛk pippalāyatanaḥ ca eva tathā hi aṅgārakāśanaḥ śithilaḥ śithilāsyaḥ ca akṣapādaḥ hi ajaḥ kujaḥ vṛndaśas tam samāvṛtya jagmuḥ somam gaṇaiḥ vṛtāḥ dagdhum puratrayam jagmuḥ koṭi koṭi gaṇaiḥ vṛtāḥ trayastrim śat surāḥ ca eva trayaḥ ca tri śatāḥ tathā mātaraḥ sarva lokānām gaṇānām ca eva mātaraḥ bhūtānām mātaraḥ ca eva jagmuḥ devasya pṛṣṭhatas bhāti madhye gaṇānām ca ratha madhye gaṇeśvaraḥ nabhasi amala nakṣatre tārā madhye iva uḍurāj rarāja devī devasya girijā pārśva saṃsthitā śubhāvatī tadā devī pārśva saṃsthā vibhāti sā cāmara āsakta hasta agrā sā hema aṃbuja varṇikā bhāti indradhanuṣā ākāśam meruṇā ca yathā jagat hiraṇya dhanuṣā saumyam vapuḥ śaṃbhoḥ śaśi dyuti sita ātapatram ratna aṃśu miśritam parameṣṭhinaḥ yathā udaye śaśāṅkasya bhāti akhaṇḍam hi maṇḍalam sa dukūlā śive raktā laṃbitā bhāti mālikā chatra antā ratna jā ākāśāt patantī iva saridvarā kim atra dagdhum tripuram pinākī svayam gataḥ ca atra gaṇaiḥ ca sārdham manvāma nūnam bhagavān pinākī līlā artham etat sakalam pravartum vyavasthitaḥ ca iti tathā anyathā ced āḍaṃbareṇa asya phalam kim anyat gaṇaiḥ gaṇeśaḥ tu rarāja devyā jagat rathaḥ meruḥ iva aṣṭa śṛṅgaiḥ atha nirikṣya sureśvaram īśvaram sa gaṇam adrisutā sahitam tadā puṣya yogaḥ upi saṃprāpte līlā vaśam umāpatim sthāne tava mahādeva ceṣṭeyam parameśvara tathā api devāḥ dharmiṣṭhāḥ pūrvadevāḥ ca pāpinaḥ iṣuṇā bhūta saṃghaiḥ ca viṣṇunā ca mayā prabho dagdhum arhasi śīghram tvam trīṇi etāni purāṇi vai somaḥ ca bhagavān viṣṇuḥ kālāgniḥ vāyuḥ eva ca mumoca bāṇam vipra indrāḥ vyākṛṣya ākarṇam īśvaraḥ tad kṣaṇāt tripuram dagdhvā tripura anta karaḥ śaraḥ devadevam samāsādya namaskṛtvā vyavasthitaḥ dṛṣṭvā bhītam tadā anīkam devānām deva puṅgavaḥ kim ca iti āha tadā devān praṇemuḥ tam samantataḥ viṣṇunā ca bhavam devam tripura arātim īśvaram śrī pitāmaḥ uvāca aghora aṣṭaka tattvāya dvādaśa ātma svarūpiṇe agni varṇāya raudrāya aṃbikā ardha śarīriṇe dhavala śyāma raktānām mukti dāya amarāya ca jyeṣṭhāya rudra rūpāya somāya vara dāya ca tri lokāya tri devāya vaṣaṭkārāya vai namaḥ aṣṭa kṣetra aṣṭa rūpāya aṣṭa tattvāya te namaḥ pañcadhā pañcadhā ca eva pañca mantra śarīriṇe catuḥṣaṣṭi prakārāya akārāya namaḥ namaḥ dvātriṃśat tatva rūpāya ukārāya namaḥ namaḥ aṣṭadhā ātma svarūpāya ardha mātrā ātmane namaḥ sapta lokāya pātāla naraka īśāya vai namaḥ aṣṭa kṣetra aṣṭa rūpāya parātparatarāya ca sahasra pāda yuktāya śarvāya parameṣṭhine nava ātma tattva rūpāya nava aṣṭa ātma ātma śaktaye punar aṣṭa prakāśāya tathā aṣṭa aṣṭaka mūrtaye catuḥṣaṣṭi ātma tattvāya punar aṣṭavidhāya te guṇa aṣṭaka vṛtāya eva guṇine nirguṇāya te namaḥ tubhyam śāsvata sthāna vāsine nābhi maṇḍala saṃsthāya hṛdi niḥsvana kāriṇe śivāya śiva rūpiṇe vahni soma arka rūpāya ṣaṭtriṃśat śakti rūpiṇe tri prakāram stitāya eva tretāgni maya rūpiṇe tatpuruṣāya namaḥ astu īśānāya namaḥ namaḥ namaḥ triṃśat prakāśāya śānta atītāya vai namaḥ ananta īśāya sūkṣmāya uttamāya namaḥ astu te namaḥ trimūrtaye tubhyam śrīkaṇṭhāya śikhaṇḍine ananta āsana saṃsthāya anantāya anta kāriṇe vimalāya viśālāya vimala aṅgāya te namaḥ vimala āsana saṃsthāya vimala artha artha rūpiṇe yoginām hṛdi saṃsthāya sadā nīvāra śūka vat pratyāhārāya te nityam pratyāhāra ratāya te pratyāhāra ratānām ca prati sthāna sthitāya ca dhāraṇāyai namaḥ tubhyam dhāraṇa abhiratāya te dhāraṇā abhyāsa yuktānām purastāt saṃsthitāya ca samādhāna abhigamyāya samādhānāya te namaḥ samādhāna ratānām tu nirvikalpa artha rūpiṇe kaḥ totum icchet katham īdṛśam tvām steṣya iha tuṣṭāya śivāya tubhyam ete gaṇāḥ siddha gaṇaiḥ praṇāmam kurvāti deveśa gaṇeśa tubhyam līlā alasena aṃbikayā kṣaṇena dagdham kila iṣuḥ ca tadā atha muktaḥ kṛtaḥ rathaḥ ca eṣu varaḥ ca śubhram śarasanam te tripura kṣayāya aneka yatnaiḥ ca mayā atha tubhyam na dṛṣṭam phalam na dṛṣṭam sura siddha saṃghaiḥ ananta mūrtiḥ katham īdṛśan tvam toṣyam katham īdṛśam tvām stūlāya sūkṣmāya su sūkṣma sūkṣma sūkṣmāya sūkṣma artha vide vidhātre sraṣṭre namaḥ sarva sura asurāṇām bhartre ca hartre jagatām vidhātre netre surāṇām asura īśvarāṇām dātre praśāstre mama sarva śāstre ādi anta śūnyāya ca saṃsthitāya tathā tva śūnyāya ca liṅgine ca aliṅgine liṅga mayāya tubhyam ligāya veda ādi mayāya sākṣāt rudrāya mūrdhāna nikṛntanāya mama ādi devasya ca yajñamūrteḥ vidhvānta bhaṅgam mama kartum īśa dṛṣṭvā eva bhūmau karaja agra koṭyā dehi iva devaiḥ saha deva kāryam kariṣyase nirgumīśa dṛṣṭvā eva bhūmau karaja agra koṭyā ekam sthūlam sūkṣmam ekam su sūkṣmam sūrta amūrtam mūrtam ekam hi amūrtam ekam dṛṣṭam vāc mayam ca ekam īśam dhyeyam ca ekam tattvam atra adbhutam te svapne dṛṣṭam yat padārtham hi alakṣyam dṛṣṭam nūnam bhāti manye na ca api tathā api bhaktyā vilapantam īśa pitāmaham mām bhagavan kṣamasva sūtaḥ uvāca yaḥ imam śṛṇuyāt dvijottamāḥ bhuvi devam praṇipatya paṭhet śrutvā ca bhaktyā caturānanena stutaḥ hasan śailasutām nirīkṣya stavam tadā prāha mahā anubhāvam mahā bhujaḥ mandara śṛṅga vāsī śivaḥ uvāca sūtaḥ uvāca tatas praṇamya deveśam bhagavān padmasaṃbhavaḥ śrī pitāmahaḥ uvāca devānām ca eva sarveṣām tvayī sarva artha da īśvara vāhana tvam tava īśāna nityam īhe prasīda me sāmarthyam ca sadā mahyam bhavantam voḍhum īśvaram sūtaḥ uvāca tayoḥ śrutvā mahādevaḥ vijñaptim parameśvaraḥ sārdham devyāḥ nandinā bhū saṃghaiḥ antardhānam kārayāmāsa śarvaḥ tatas tadā maheśvare gate raṇāt gaṇaiḥ saha sura īśvarāḥ su vismitāḥ bhavam praṇamya pārvatīm yayuḥ ca duḥkha varjitāḥ sva vāhanaiḥ divam tatas sura īśvarāḥ muni īśvarāḥ gaṇa īśvarāḥ ca bhāskarāḥ tripurāreḥ imam puṇyam nirmitam brahmaṇā purā mānasaiḥ vācikaiḥ kāpaiḥ tathā vai kāyikaiḥ punar sthūlaiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ su sūkṣmaiḥ ca mahāpātaka saṃbhavaiḥ pātakaiḥ ca dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ upapātaka saṃbhavaiḥ pāpaiḥ ca mucyate jantuḥ śrutvā adhyāyam imam śubham sūtaḥ uvāca gate maheśvare deve dagdhvā ca tripuram kṣaṇāt sadasi āha suredrāṇām bhagavān padmasaṃbhavaḥ pitāmahaḥ uvāca tārakākṣaḥ api ditijaḥ kamalākṣaḥ ca vīryavān vidyunmālī ca daitya īśaḥ anye ca api sa bāndhavāḥ tyaktvā devam mahādevam māyayā ca hareḥ prabhoḥ sarve vinaṣṭāḥ pradhvastāḥ sva puraiḥ pura saṃbhavaiḥ yāvat pūjā sura īśānām tāvat eva sthitiḥ yatas pūjanīyaḥ śivaḥ nityam śraddhayā deva puṅgavaiḥ tasmāt saṃpūjayet liṅgam yaḥ icchet siddhim ātmanaḥ pitaraḥ munayaḥ ca api piśācāḥ kinnara ādayaḥ arcayitvā liṅga mūrti saṃsiddhāḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ tasmāt liṅgam yajet nityam yena kena api vā surāḥ viśodhya ca eva bhūtāni pañcabhiḥ praṇavaiḥ samam prāṇāyāmaiḥ samāyuktaiḥ pañcabhiḥ sura puṅgavāḥ caturbhiḥ praṇavaiḥ ca eva prāṇāyāma parāyaṇaiḥ tribhiḥ ca praṇavaiḥ devāḥ prāṇāyāmaiḥ tathāvidhaiḥ tatas ca oṅkāram uccārya prāṇa apānau niyamya ca jñāna amṛtena sarva aṅgāni āpūrya praṇavena ca guṇatrayam caturdhā ākhyam ahaṅkāram ca suvratāḥ karmendriyāṇi saṃśodhya puruṣam yugalam tathā cit ātmānam tanum kṛtvā ca agniḥ bhasma iti saṃspṛśet triyāyuṣam tri saṃdhyam ca dhūlayet bhasitena yaḥ bhavena pāśa mokṣa artham kathitam deva sattamāḥ liṅge purā mayā dṛṣṭe viṣṇunā ca mahātmanā paśavaḥ na eva jāyante varṣa mātreṇa devatāḥ asmābhiḥ sarva kāryāṇām devam abhyarcya yatnataḥ pratijñā mama viṣṇoḥ ca divyā eṣā sura sattamāḥ munīnām ca na saṃdehaḥ tasmāt saṃpūjayet śivam teḥ acayantu sadā kālam liṅga mūrtim maheśvaram tadā prabhṛti śakra ādyāḥ pūjayāmāsuḥ īśvaram sūtaḥ uvāca viśvakarmā dadau teṣām niyogāt brahmaṇaḥ prabhoḥ padmarāga mayam śakraḥ haimam viśravasaḥ sutaḥ viśvedevāḥ tathā raupyam vasavaḥ kāntikam śubham ārakūṭa mayam vāyuḥ aśvinau pārthivam sadā mauktikam somarāj dhīmān tathā liṅgam anuttamam ananta ādyāḥ mahā nāgāḥ pravālaka mayam śubham daitya hyoya mayam ligam rākṣasāḥ ca mahātmanaḥ trailohikam guhyakāḥ ca sarva loha mayam gaṇāḥ cāmuṇḍā saikatam sākṣāt mātaraḥ ca dvijottamāḥ dārujam nairṛtiḥ bhaktyā yamaḥ mārakatam śubham nīla ādyāḥ ca tathā rudrāḥ śuddham bhasma mayam śubham lakṣmī vṛkṣa mayam lakṣmīḥ guhaḥ vai gomaya ātmakam munayaḥ muni sārdūlāḥ kuśa agra mayam uttamam vāmā ādyāḥ puṣpa liṅgam tu gandha liṅgam manonmanī ugrā piṣṭa mayam sarve mantrāḥ hi ājya mayam śubham vedāḥ sarve dadhi mayam piśācāḥ sīsa nirmitam ṣaḍvidham liṅgam iti āhuḥ dravyāṇām ca prabhedataḥ teṣām bhedāḥ catur yukta catvāriṃśat iti smṛtāḥ tṛtīyam dhātu jam liṅgam aṣṭadhā parameṣṭhinaḥ turīyam dāru jam liṅgam tat tu ṣoḍaśadhā ucyate mṛd mayam pañcamam liṅgam dvidhā bhinnam dvijottamāḥ ṣaṣṭham tu kṣaṇikam liṅgam saptadhā parikīrtitam śrī prada ratna jam liṅgam śaila jam sarva siddhi dam dhātujam dhana dam sākṣāt dārujam bhoga siddhi dam bahudhā liṅga bhejāḥ ca nava ca eva samāsatas mūle brahmā tathā madhye viṣṇuḥ tribhuvana īśvaraḥ rudra upari mahādevaḥ pramava ākhyaḥ sadāśivaḥ liṅga vedī mahādevī triguṇā tri mayā aṃbikā śaila jam ratna jam vā api dhātu jam vā api dāru jam mṛd mayam kṣaṇikam vā api bhaktyā sthāpya phalam śubham sura idra aṃbhojagarbha agni yama aṃbupa dhaneśvaraiḥ stūyamānaḥ su puṇya ātmā deva dundubhi niḥsvanaiḥ liṅga sthāpana sat mārga nihita su āyata asinā āśu brahmāṇḍam udbhidya nirgacchan nirviśaṅkayā śaila jam ratna jam vā api dhātu jam vā api dāru jam mṛd mayam kṣaṇikam tyaktvā sthāpayet sakalam vapuḥ vidhinā ca eva kṛtvā tu skanda umā sahitam śubham liṅgam yaḥ sthāpayet naraḥ nṛṇām tanum samāsthāya sthitaḥ rudraḥ na saṃśayaḥ sarveṣām eva martyānām vibhoḥ divyam vapuḥ śubham sakalam bhāvanā yogyam yoginām eva niṣkalam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca parama artha vidaḥ kecid ūcuḥ praṇava rūpiṇam vijñānam iti vipra indrāḥ śrutvā śrutiśirasi ajam guru prakāśakam jñānam iti anye munayaḥ dvijāḥ dyauḥ mūrdhā tu vibhoḥ tasya kham nābhiḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ soma sūrya agnayaḥ netre diśaḥ śrotram mahātmanaḥ caraṇau ca eva pātālam samudraḥ tasya ca aṃbaram devāḥ tasya bhujāḥ sarve nakṣatrāṇi ca bhūṣaṇam vattrāt vai brāhmaṇāḥ sarve brahmā ca bhagavān prabhuḥ indra upendrau bhujābhyām tu kṣatriyāḥ ca mahātmanaḥ vaiśyāḥ ca ūru pradeśāt tu śūdrāḥ pādāt pinākinaḥ puṣkarāvartaka ādyāḥ tu keśāḥ tasya prakīrtitāḥ vāyavaḥ ghrāṇa jāḥ tasya gatiḥ śrautam smṛtiḥ tathā puṃsām tu puruṣaḥ śrīmān jñāna gamyaḥ na ca anyathā viśuddhāḥ vidyayā sarve brahma vidyā vidaḥ janāḥ dharma adharmau japaḥ homaḥ dhyāninām saṃnidhiḥ sadā niṣkalaḥ sakalaḥ ca iti sarvam śiva mayam tatas vyoma ekam api dṛṣṭam hi śarāvam prati suvratāḥ pṛthaktvam ca apṛthaktvam ca śaṅkarasya iti ca apare jantavaḥ divi bhūmā ca sarve vai pāñcabhautikāḥ tathā api bahulā dṛṣṭā jāti vyakti vibhedataḥ duḥkhī ca bhegam duḥkham ca na anubhūtam vicārataḥ yoginām niṣkalaḥ devaḥ jñāninām ca jagat mayaḥ tṛtīyam sakalam ca eva na anyathā iti dvijottamāḥ sakalam munayaḥ kecid sadā saṃsāra vartinaḥ evam abhyarcayanti eva sa dārāḥ sa suta narāḥ daśa are dvādaśa are ca ṣoḍaśa are tris asrake sa sva icchayā śivaḥ sākṣāt devyā sārdham sthitaḥ prabhuḥ saṃtāraṇa artham ca śivaḥ sat asat vyakti varjitaḥ ūcuḥ tathā tam ca śivam tathā anye saṃsāriṇam veda vidaḥ vadanti bhaktyā ca yogena śubhena yuktāḥ viprāḥ sadā dharma ratāḥ viśiṣṭāḥ te yānti ca enam na ca yoginaḥ anye tayā ca devyā puruṣam purāṇam sūtaḥ uvāca pratiṣṭhāyāḥ phalam sarvam sarva loka hitāya vai aumam kaumāram aiśānam vaiṣṇavam brāhmam eva ca prājāpatyam mahā tejāḥ jana lokam mahar tathā āsādya caidra tvam kṛtvā varṣa ayutam punar merum āsādya devānām bhavaneṣu pramodate eka pādam catur bāhum tri netram śūla saṃyutam sṛṣṭvā sthitam harīm vāme dakṣiṇe caturānanam koṭiḥ sarva aṅga su prabham pañcaviṃśatikam sākṣāt puruṣam hṛdayāt tathā prakṛtim vāmatas ca eva buddhim vai buddhi deśataḥ ahaṅkāram ahaṅkārāt tanmātrāṇi tu tatra vai indriyāṇi indriyāt eva līlayā parameśvaram pṛthivīm pāda mūlāt tu guhya deśāt jakam tathā nābi deśāt tathā vahnim hṛdayāt bhāskaram tathā kaṇṭhāt somam tathā ātmānam bhrū madhyāt mastakāt divam sṛṣṭa evam saṃsthitam sākṣāt jagat sarvam carācaram sarvajñam sarva gam devam kṛtvā vidyā vidhānataḥ vṛṣa ārūḍham tu yaḥ kuryāt somam soma ardha bhūṣaṇam hayamedha ayutam kṛtvā yat puṇyam tat ā avāpya saḥ nṛtya adbhiḥ apsaraḥ saṃghaiḥ sarvatas sarva śaubhitaiḥ nṛtyantam devadeveśam śailajā sahitam prabhum sahasra bāhum sarvajñam catur bāhum atha api vā bhṛgu ādyaiḥ bhūta saṃghaiḥ ca saṃvṛtam parameśvaram śailajā sahitam sākṣāt vṛṣabhadhvajam īśvaram kṛtvā bhaktyā pratiṣṭhāpya yat phalam tat vadāmi aham sṛṣṭi antare punar prāpte mānavam padam āpnuyāt kapāla hastam deveśam kṛṣṇa kuñcita mūrdhajam ibha indra dārakam devam sa aṃbam siddhārtha dam prabhum su dhūmra varṇam rakta akṣam tri netram candra bhūṣaṇam kākapakṣa dharam mūrdhnā nāga ṭaṅka dharam haram siṃha ajina uttarīyam ca mṛga carma aṃbaram prabhum tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭram gadā hastam kapāla udyata pāṇinam huṃ phaṭkāre mahā śabda śabdita akhila diṅmukham puṇḍarīka ajinam dorbhyām bibhrantam kaṃbukam tathā hasaṃtam ca nadantam ca pibantam kṛṣṇa sāgaram nṛtyantam bhūta saṃghaiḥ ca gaṇa saṃghaiḥ tu alaṅkṛtam ācandratārakam jñānam tatas labdhvā vimucyate kṛtvā bhaktyā pratiṣṭhāpya śiva lokam sa gacchati pūrvadeva amarāṇām ca yat sthānam sakala īpsitam tripuṇḍra dhāriṇaḥ teṣām śiraḥ mālā dharasya ca brahmaṇaḥ keśakena ekam upavītam ca bibhrataḥ viṣṇoḥ kalevaram ca eva bibhrataḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ kṛtvā bhaktyā pratiṣṭhāpya mucyate bhava sāgarāt om namaḥ nīlakaṇṭhāya iti puṇya akṣara aṣṭakam mantram āha sakṛt vā yaḥ pātakaiḥ sa vimucyate mantreṇa anenam gandha ādyaiḥ bhaktyā vitta anusārataḥ saṃpūjya devadeveśam śiva loke mahīyate kṛtvā bhaktyā pratiṣṭhāpya dvidhā bhūtam jalandharam sudarśana pradam devam sākṣāt pūrva ukta lakṣaṇam tiṣṭhataḥ utha nikuṃbhasya pṛṣṭhatas caraṇa aṃbujam vāmetaram su vinyasya vāme ca āliṅgya ca adrijām śūla agre kūrparam sthāpya kiṅkiṇī kṛta pannagam saṃprekṣya ca andhakam pārśve kṛta añjali puṭam sthitam dhanuḥ bāṇa samāyuktam sa umam soma ardha bhūṣaṇam tad ākāra tayā saḥ api gatvā śiva puram sukhī krīḍate na atra saṃdehaḥ dvitīyaḥ iva śaṅkaraḥ gaṅgā dharam sukha āsīnam candraśekharam eva ca gaṅgayā sahitam ca eva vāma utsaṃga iṃbikā anvitam vināyakam tathā skandam jyeṣṭham durgām su śobhanām bhāskaram ca tathā somam brahmāṇīm ca maheśvarīm kaumārīm vaiṣṇavaīm devaīm vārāhīm vāradām tathā indrāṇīm ca eva cāmuṇḍām vīrabhadra samanvitām vighneśena ca yaḥ dhīmān śiva sāyujyam āpnuyāt liṅga mūrtim mahā jvāla mālā saṃvṛtam avyayam vyomni kuryāt tathā liṅgam brahmāṇam haṃsa rūpiṇam viṣṇum varāha rūpeṇa liṅgasya adhas tu adhomukham kṣetra saṃrakṣakam devam tathā pāśupatam prabhum ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ liṅga pratiṣṭhā puṇyam ca liṅga sthāpanam eva ca liṅgānām ca eva bhedāḥ ca śrutam tava mukhāt iha sūtaḥ uvāca tathā api bhaktāḥ parameśvarasya kṛtvā iṣṭa loṣṭaiḥ api rudra lokam bālyāt tu loṣṭena ca kṛtvā mṛdā api vā pāṃsubhiḥ ādidevam gṛham ca tādṛgvidham asya śaṃbhoḥ saṃpūjya rudra tvam avāpnuvanti kesaram nāgaram vā api drāvīḍam vā tathā aparam mandaram vā prakurvīta śivāya vidhi pūrvakam jñāna yogam samāsādya gāṇapatyam labhet naraḥ yaḥ kuryāt meru nāmānam prāsādam parameṣṭhinaḥ niṣadham nāma yaḥ kuryāt prāsādam bhaktitaḥ sudhīḥ kuryāt vā yaḥ śubham viprāḥ himaśailam anuttamam nīla adri śikhara ākhyam vā prāsādam yaḥ su śobhanam mahendra śaila nāmānam prāsādam rudra saṃmatam jñānam vicāritam rudraiḥ saṃprāpya muni puṅgavāḥ hemnā yaḥ tu prakṛrvīta prāsādam ratna śobhitam dravīḍam nāgaram vā api kesaram vā vidhānataḥ kūṭam vā maṇḍapam vā api samam vā dīrgham eva ca pūrvavat kārayet yaḥ tu dvāra ādyaiḥ su śubham dvijāḥ yaḥ sa yāti na saṃdehaḥ svarga lokam sa bāndhavaḥ tasmāt āyatanam bhaktyā yaḥ kuryāt muni sattamāḥ prasāda artha maheśasya prāsāde muni puṅgavāḥ aśaktaḥ ced muni śreṣṭhāḥ prāsādam kartum uttamam yaḥ kuryāt vastra pūtena gandha gomaya vāriṇā ālepanam yathānyāyam varṣa cāndrāyaṇam labhet ardha krośam śiva kṣetram śiva liṅgāt samantataḥ yaḥ tyajet dustyajān prāṇān śiva sāyujyam āpnuyāt svāyaṃbhuvasya mānam hi tathā bāṇasya suvratāḥ svāyaṃbhuve tad ardham syāt syāt ārṣe ca tad ardhakam mānuṣe ca tad ardham syāt kṣetra mānam dvijottamāḥ evam yatīnām āvāse kṣetra mānam dvijottamāḥ rudra avatāre ca ādyam yat śiṣye ca eva praśiṣyake nara avatāre tad ciṣye cat śiṣye ca praśīṣyake śrīparvate mahā puṇye tasya prānte ca vā dvijāḥ tasmin vā yaḥ tyajet prāṇān śiva sāyujyam āpnuyāt vārāṇasyām tathā api evam avimukte viśeṣataḥ kedāre ca mahā kṣetre prayāge ca viśeṣataḥ kurukṣetra ca yaḥ prāṇān saṃtyajet yāti nirvṛtim prabhāse puṣkare avantyām tathā ca eva amareśvare vaṇīśelākule ca eva mṛtaḥ yāti śiva ātma tām vārāṇasyām mṛtaḥ jantuḥ na jātu jantu tām vrajet śālake vā tyajet prāṇān tathā vai jaṃbukeśvare śukreśvare vā gokarṇe bhāskareśe guheśvare sa yāti śiva tām yogī mānuṣe vā ārṣe vā api muni śreṣṭhāḥ tathā svāyaṃbhuve api vā ādhāya agnim śiva kṣetre saṃpūjya parameśvaram śiva kṣetre muni śreṣṭhāḥ śiva sāyujyam āpnuyāt chittvā pāda dvayam ca api śiva kṣetre vaset tu yaḥ kṣetrasya darśanam puṇyam praveśaḥ tat śa ta adhikaḥ kṣīra snānam tatas viprāḥ śata adhikam anuttamam ghṛta snānena ca anantam śārkare tat śata adhikam śiva kṣetra samīpa sthām nadīm prāpya avagāhya ca śiva kṣetra samīpa sthāḥ nadyaḥ sarvāḥ su śobhanāḥ snātvā teṣu naraḥ bhaktyā tīrtheṣu dvijasattamāḥ prātar snātvā muni śreṣṭhāḥ śiva tīrtheṣu mānavaḥ madhyāhne śiva tīrtheṣu snātvā bhaktyā sakṛt naraḥ dvijāḥ triṣavaṇam snātvā śiva tīrthe sakṛt naraḥ purā atha sūkaraḥ kaścid śvānam dṛṣṭvā bhayāt pathi prasaṃgāt vāram ekam tu śiva tīrthe avagāhya ca mṛtaḥ svayam dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ gāṇapatyam avāptavān yaḥ prātar devadeveśam śivam liṅga svarūpiṇam sāyāhne sarva yajñānām phalam prāpya vimucyate mānasaiḥ vācikaiḥ pāpaiḥ kāyikaiḥ ca mahattaraiḥ tathā upapātakaiḥ ca eva pāpaiḥ ca eva anupātakaiḥ saṃkrame devam īśānam dṛṣṭvā liṅga ākṛtim prabhum māsena yat kṛtam pāpam tyaktvā yāti śivam padam ayane ca ardha māsena dakṣiṇe ca uttarāyaṇe viṣuve ca eva saṃpūjya prayāti paramām gatim pradakṣiṇā trayam kuryāt yaḥ prāsādam samantataḥ savya apasavya nyāyena mṛdu gatyā śuciḥ naraḥ vācā yaḥ tu śivam nityam saṃrauti parameśvaram padmarāga mayaiḥ ca eva sphaāṭikaiḥ ca su śobhanaiḥ ālikhya ā kamalam bhadran daśa hasta pramāṇataḥ pañcabhiḥ ca tathā ṣaḍbhiḥ aṣṭābhiḥ ca iṣṭa dam param punar aṣṭābhiḥ īśānam daśa are daśabhiḥ tathā punar bāhye ca daśabhiḥ saṃpūjya praṇipatya ca śāli piṣṭa ādibhiḥ vā api padmam ālikhya nirdhanaḥ dvādaśa aram tathā ālikhya maṇḍalam ratna cūrṇa ādibhiḥ cūrṇaiḥ tathā dvādaśa mūrtibhiḥ grahaiḥ ca saṃvṛtam vā api sūrya sāyujyam uttamam evam prākṛtam api āryām ṣaṣ asram parikalpya ca madhyadeśe ca deveśīm prakṛtim brahma rūpiṇīm dakṣiṇe sattva mūrtim ca vāmatas ca rajaḥ guṇam agratas tu tamaḥ mūrtim madhye devīm tathā aṃbikām ātmānam ca antarātmānam yugalam buddhim eva ca evam vaḥ kathitam sarvam prākṛtam maṇḍalam param ante ca devadevāya dāpayet cūrṇa maṇḍalam yena kena api vā martyaḥ pralipya āyatana agratas sargaḥ ca bhuvana adhīśaḥ śarva vyāpī sadāśivaḥ śiva brahma amṛtam grāhyam mokṣa sādhana muttamam sūtaḥ uvāca śivakṣetra muni śreṣṭhāḥ na anyathā siddhiḥ iṣyate jantubhiḥ miśritāḥ hi āpaḥ sūkṣmābhiḥ tān nihatya tu yat pāpam sakalam ca adbhiḥ a pūtābhiḥ ciram labhet saṃmārjane tathā nṝṇām mārjane ca viśeṣataḥ hiṃsā sadā sṛhasthānām tasmāt hiṃsām vivarjayet kāryam abhyukṣaṇam nityam snapanam ca viśeṣataḥ vihita avihitam na asti yoginām brahma vādinām yatas tasmāt na hantavyāḥ niṣiddhānām niṣevaṇāt na hantavyāḥ sadā pūjyāḥ pāpa karma ratāḥ api striyaḥ sarvāḥ na hantavyāḥ pāpa karma ratāḥ api na yajña artham striyaḥ grāhyāḥ sarvaiḥ sarvatra sarvadā sarva varṇeṣu vipra indrāḥ pāpa karma ratāḥ api malināḥ rūpavatyaḥ ca virupāḥ malina aṃbarāḥ na hantavyāḥ sadā martyaiḥ śiva vat caṅkayā tathā veda bāhya vrata ācārāḥ śrauta smārta bahiṣkṛtāḥ pāṣaṇḍinaḥ iti khyātāḥ na saṃbhāṣyāḥ dvijātibhiḥ na spṛṣṭavyā na draṣṭavyā dṛṣṭvā bhānum samīkṣate prasaṃgāt vā api yaḥ martyaḥ satām sakṛt aho dvijāḥ bhavānti duḥkhitāḥ sarve nirdayāḥ muni sattamāḥ putreṣu dāreṣu gṛheṣu nṝṇām bhaktam yathā cittam atha ādideve sakṛt prasaṃga ādi ati tāpasānām teṣām na dūraḥ parameśa lokaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ saṃvatsara sahasraiḥ ca tapasā pūjya śaṅkaram na paśyanti surāḥ ca api katham devam yajanti te sūtaḥ uvāca kathitam tathya mā evā atra yuṣmābhiḥ muni puṅgavāḥ tathā api śraddhayā dṛśyaḥ pūjyaḥ saṃbhāṣyaḥ eva ca prasaṃgāt ca eva saṃpūjya bhakti hīnaiḥ api dvijāḥ bhāva anurūpa phala daḥ bhagavān iti kīrtitaḥ saṃkruddhaḥ rākṣasam sthānam prāpnuyāt mūḍha dhīḥ dvijāḥ abhakṣya bhakṣī saṃpūjya yā akṣam prāpnoti durjanaḥ khyāti śīlaḥ tathā cāndram strīṣu saktaḥ nara adhamaḥ mada ārtaḥ pūjayan rudram soma sthānam avāpnuyāt brāhmam hi praṇavena eva vaiṣṇavam ca abhinandya ca saṃśodhya ca śubham liṅgam amara asura pūjitam jalaiḥ pūtaiḥ tatā pīṭhe devam āvāhya bhaktitaḥ dṛṣṭvā devam yathānyāyam praṇipatya ca śaṅkaram pādyam ācamanam ca arghyam dattvā rudrāya śaṃbhave rocanā ādyaiḥ ca saṃpūjya divya puṣpaiḥ ca pūjayet nīlotpalaiḥ ca rājīvaiḥ nadyāvartaiḥ ca mallikaiḥ caṅkapaiḥ jātipuṣpaiḥ cavakulaiḥ karavīrakaiḥ apāmārga kadaṃbaiḥ ca bhūṣaṇaiḥ api śobhanaiḥ dattvā pañcavidham dhūpam pāyasam ca nivedayet śuddha annam ca eva mudga annam ṣaḍvidham ca nivedayet kevalam ca api śuddha annam āḍhakam taṇḍulam pacet stutvā ca devam īśānam punar saṃpūjya śaṅkaram sadyojātam japan ca api pañcabhiḥ pūjayet śivam pūjayet yaḥ śivam rudram śarvam bhavam ajam sakṛt arcitam parameśānam bhavam śarvam umāpatim pūjitam vā mahādevam pūjyamānam atha api vā dṛṣṭvā prayāti vai martyaḥ brahma lokam na saṃśayaḥ yaḥ dadyāt hṛta dīpam ca sakṛt liṅgasya ca agratas sa tām gatim avāpnoti sva āśramaiḥ durlabhām sthirām dīpavṛkṣam pārthivam vā dāravam vā śiva ālaye āyasam tāmra jam vā api raupyam sauvarṇikam tathā śivāya dīpam yaḥ dadyāt vidhinā vā api bhaktitaḥ sūrya ayuta samaiḥ ślakṣṇaiḥ yānaiḥ śiva puram vrajet kārtike māsi yaḥ dadyat hṛta dīpam śiva agratas saṃpūjyamānam vā paśyet vidhinā parameśvaram sa yāti brahmaṇaḥ lokam śraddhayā muni sāttamāḥ āvāhanam su sānnidhyam sthāpanam pūjanam tathā saṃproktam rudra gāyatryā āsanam praṇavena vai pañcabhiḥ snapanam proktam rudra ādyaiḥ ca viśeṣataḥ brahmāṇam dakṣiṇe tasya praṇavena samarcayet uttare devadeveśam viṣṇum gāyatriyā yajet vahnau hutvā yathānyāyam pañcabhiḥ praṇavena ca vyāsena kathitaḥ pūrvam śrutvā rudra mukhāt svayam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ katham paśupatim dṛṣṭvā paśu pāśa vimokṣaṇam sūtaḥ uvāca hitāya sarva devānām brahmaṇā ca janārdanaḥ sa iṃdrāḥ sa sādhyāḥ sa yamāḥ praṇemuḥ girim uttamam bhagavān vāsudevaḥ asau garuḍāt garuḍadhvajaḥ sakala durita hītam sarva dam bhoga mukhyam mudita kurara vṛndam nāditam nāga vṛndaiḥ madhura raṇita gītam sa anukūla andhakāram pada racita vanāntam kānta vāta anta toyam gireḥ pṛṣṭhe param śārvam kalpitam viśvakarmaṇā apaśyan tat puram devāḥ sa iṃdra upendrāḥ samāhitāḥ praṇemuḥ dūratas ca eva prabhāvāt eva śūlinaḥ jagāma kailāsa girim mahātmā meru prabhāge puram ādidevaḥ gaṇaiḥ gaṇeśaiḥ ca giri indra sannibham mahā pura dvāram ajaḥ hariḥ ca atha jāṃbūnada mayaiḥ bhavanaiḥ maṇi bhūṣitaiḥ vimānaiḥ vividha ākāraiḥ prākāraiḥ ca samāvṛtam dṛṣṭvā śaṃbhoḥ puram bāhyam devaiḥ sa brahmakaiḥ hariḥ harmya prāsāda saṃbādham mahā aṭṭāla samanvitam vajra vaiḍūrya māṇikya maṇi jālaiḥ samāvṛtam nṛtyadbhiḥ apsaraḥ saṃghaiḥ bhūta saṃghaiḥ ca saṃvṛtam prāsāda śṛṅgeṣu atha paura nāryaḥ sahasraśas puṣpa phala akṣata ādyaiḥ dṛṣṭvā nāryaḥ tadā viṣṇum mada āghūrṇita locanāḥ viśāla jaghanāḥ sadyas nanṛtuḥ mumuduḥ jaguḥ kāścid dṛṣṭvā harim nāryaḥ kiñcid prahasita ānanāḥ ca srasta kāñcī guṇāḥ jaguḥ caturtham pañcamam ca eva ṣaṣṭham ca saptamam tathā aṣṭamam navamam ca eva daśamam ca pura uttamam atītya āsādya devasya puram śaṃbhoḥ su śobhanam su vṛttam sutarām śubhram kailāsa śikhare śubhe sphaāṭikaiḥ maḍapaiḥ śubhraiḥ jāṃbūnada mayaiḥ tathā nānā ratna mayaiḥ ca eva diś vidikṣu vibhūṣitam gopuraiḥ gopateḥ śaṃbhoḥ nānā bhūṣaṇa bhūṣitaiḥ prākāraiḥ vividha ākāraiḥ aṣṭāviṃśatibhiḥ vṛtam upadvāraiḥ mahādvāraiḥ vidikṣu vividhaiḥ dṛḍhaiḥ grāmyaiḥ anyaiḥ mahābhāgāḥ mauktikaiḥ dṛṣṭi mohanaiḥ candanaiḥ vividha ākāraiḥ puṣpa udyānaiḥ ca śaubhanaiḥ taḍāgaiḥ dirghikābhiḥ ca hema sopānapaṅktibhiḥ strīṇām aṅgati jitaiḥ haṃsaiḥ sevitābhiḥ samantataḥ mayūraiḥ ca eva kāraṇḍaiḥ kokilaiḥ cakravākakaiḥ śobhitābhiḥ ca vāpībhiḥ divya amṛta jalaiḥ tathā rudra strī gama saṃkīrṇaiḥ jala krīḍā rataiḥ tathā tatra eva dadṛśuḥ devāḥ vṛndam rudra gaṇasya ca gaṇeśvarāṇām vīrāṇām api vṛndam sahasraśas suvarṇa kṛta sopānān vajra vaiḍūrya bhūṣitān sphaāṭikān devadevasya dadṛśuḥ te vimānakān teṣām śṛṅgeṣu hṛṣṭāḥ ca nāryaḥ kamala locanāḥ kinnaryaḥ kiṃnarāḥ ca eva bhujaṅgāḥ siddha kanyakāḥ padma kiñjalka saṃkāśaiḥ aṃśukaiḥ ati śobhanāḥ valayaiḥ nūpuraiḥ hāraiḥ chatraiḥ citraiḥ tathā aṃśukaiḥ bhūṣitāḥ bhūṣitaiḥ ca anyaiḥ māṇḍitāḥ maṇḍana priyāḥ dṛṣṭvā atha vṛndam sura suṃdarīṇām gaṇeśvarāṇām sura suṃdarīṇām jagmuḥ gaṇeśasya puram sura īśāḥ puradviṣaḥ śakra purogamāḥ ca dṛṣṭvā ca tasthuḥ sura siddha saṃghāḥ purasya madhye puruhūta pūrvāḥ tam dṛṣṭvā nandinam sarve pramamya āhuḥ gaṇeśvaram jaya iti devāḥ tam dṛṣṭvā saḥ upi āha ca gaṇaiśvaraḥ saṃprāptāḥ sarva loke sāḥ vaktum arhatha suvratāḥ paśu pāśa vimokṣa artham darśaya asmān maheśvaram purā puratrayam dagdhum paśu tvam paribhāṣitam śaṅkitāḥ ca vayam tatra paśu tvam prati suvrata dina dvādaśakam vā api kṛtvā tat bratam uttamam praṇemuḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ vā eva prīti kaṇṭakita tvacaḥ viśodhya teṣām devānām paśu tvam parameśvaraḥ upadiśya munīnām ca saha āste ca aṃbayā bhavaḥ paśūnām ca patiḥ yasmāt teṣām sākṣāt hi devatāḥ tasmāt pāśupatāḥ proktāḥ tapaḥ tepuḥ ca te punar tatas dvādaśa varṣa ante mukta pāśāḥ sura uttamāḥ yayuḥ yathāgatam sarve brahmaṇā saha viṣṇunā yaḥ śrāvayet śruciḥ viprān śṛṇuyāt vā śuciḥ naraḥ sa dehabhedam āsādya paśu pāśaiḥ pramucyate ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ vratam pāśupatam laiṅgam purā devaiḥ anuṣṭhitam vaktum arhasi ca asmākam yathāpūrvam tvayā śrutam sūtaḥ uvāca purā sanatkumāreṇa pṛṣṭaḥ śailādiḥ ādarāt aviyoga karam puṇyam bhaktānām bhaya nāśanam ṣaḍaṅga sahitān vedān mathitvā tena nirmitam sarva dāna uttamam puṇyam aśvamedha ayuta adhikam kṛtvā ākanīyasam liṅgam snāpya candana vāriṇā caitra māsa ādi vipra indrāḥ śiva liṅga vratam caret kṛtvā haimam śubham padmam karṇikā kesara anvitam karṇikāyām nyaset liṅgam sphaāṭikam pīṭha saṃyutam śveta arka karṇikāraiḥ ca karavīraiḥ bakaiḥ api etaiḥ anyaiḥ yathālābham gāyatryā tasya suvratāḥ saṃpūjya ca eva gandha ādyaiḥ dhūpaiḥ dīpaiḥ ca maṅgalaiḥ nīrājana ādyaiḥ ca anyaiḥ ca liṅga mūrti maheśvaram agarum dakṣiṇe dadyāt aghoreṇa dvijottamāḥ paścime sadya mantreṇa divyām ca eva manaḥśilām puruṣeṇa muni śreṣṭhāḥ haritālam ca pūrvatas sita agaru udbhavam viprāḥ tathā kṛṣṇa agaru udbhavam sitāram nāma dhūpam ca dadyat īśāya bhaktitaḥ mahā caruḥ nivedyaḥ syāt āḍhaka annam atha api vā etat vāḥ kathitam puṇyam śiva liṅga mahā vratam sarva māseṣu sāmānyam viśeṣaḥ api ca kīrtyate māsi bhādrapade liṅgam padmarāga mayam śubham āśvine ca eva vipra indrāḥ gomedaka mayam śubham pravalena eva kārtikyām tathā vai mārgaśīrṣake vaiḍūlya nirmitam liṅgam puṣparāgeṇa puṣyake alābhe rājatam vā api ke valam kamalam tu vā ratnānām paya lābhe tu hemnā vā rājatena vā śailam vā dāru jam vā api mṛd mayam vā sa vedikam sarva gandha mayam vā api kṣāṇikam parikalpayet haimantike mahādevam śrī patreṇa eva pūjayet tad ardha ardhena vā rudram aṣṭottaraśatena vā bilva patre sthitā lakṣmīḥ devī lakṣaṇa saṃyutā nīlotpala iṃbikā sākṣāt utpale ṣaṇmukhaḥ svayam padma āśritā mahādevaḥ sarva deva patiḥ śivaḥ nīlotpalam ca utpalam ca kamalam ca viśeṣataḥ sarva vaśyā karam padmam śilā sarva artha siddhi dā kṛṣṇa agaru samudbhutam sarva pāpa nikṛntanam guggulu prabhṛtīnām ca eva dīpānām ca nivedanam śvetagaru udbhavam ca eva tathā kṛṣṇāgaru udbhavam saumyam sītāri dhūpam ca sākṣāt nirvāṇa siddhi dam śveta arka kusume sākṣāt caturvattraḥ prajāpatiḥ karṇikārasya kusume medhā sākṣāt vyavasthitā karavīre gaṇādhyakṣaḥ bake nārāyaṇaḥ svayam tasmāt etaiḥ yathālābham puṣpa dhūpa ādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ śuddha annam vā api mudga annam āḍhakam ca ardhakam tu vā upahārāṇi puṇyāni nyāyena eva arjitāni api nānāvidhāni ca arhāṇi prokṣitāni aṃbhasā punar kṣīrāt vai sarva devānām sthiti artham amṛtam dhruvam viṣṇunā jiṣṇunā sākṣāt anne sarvam pratiṣṭhitam bhūtānām anna dānena prītiḥ bavati śaṅkare upahāre tathā tuṣṭiḥ vyañjane pavanaḥ svayam pīṭhe vai prakṛtiḥ sākṣāt mahat ādyaiḥ vyavasthitā saṃvatsara ante godānam vṛṣotsargam viśeṣataḥ sthāpayet vā śiva kṣetre dāpayet brāhmaṇāya vā yaḥ evam sarva māseṣu śiva liṅga mahāvratam kuryāt bhaktyā muni śreṣṭhāḥ saḥ eva tapatām varaḥ gatvā śiva puram divyam na iha āyāti kadācana varṣam ekam caret evam tān tān prāpya śivam vrajet deva tvam vā pitṛ tvam vā deva rāja tvam eva ca dravya arthī ca nidhim paśyet āyuḥ kāmaḥ cira āyuṣam idam pavitram paramam rahasyam vrata uttamam viśvasṛjā api sṛṣṭam hitāya deva asura siddha martya vidyādharāṇām paramam śivena saṃpūjya pūjyam vidhinā evam īśam praṇamya mūrdhnā saha bhṛtya putraiḥ vyapohanam nāma japet stavam ca pradakṣiṇam kṛtya śivam prayatnāt purā kṛtam viśvasṛjā stavam ca hitāya devena jagattrayasya sūtaḥ uvāca nandinaḥ ca mukhāt śrutvā kumāreṇa mahātmanā vyāsāya kathitam tasmāt bahu mānena vai mayā duṣṭa antakāya sarvāya bhavāya paramātmane padmāsana sthaḥ soma īśaḥ pāpam āśu vyapohatu vāmadevaḥ ca bhagavān pāpam āśu vyapohatu śiva dhyāna eka saṃpannaḥ sa me pāpam vyapohatu sūkṣmaḥ sura asura īśānaḥ viśveśaḥ gaṇa pūjitaḥ śiva dhyāna eka saṃpannaḥ sa me pāpam vyapohatu ekāśraḥ bhagavān īśaḥ śiva arcana parāyaṇaḥ śiva dhyāna eka saṃpannaḥ sa me pāpam vyapohatu śiva dhyāna eka saṃpannaḥ sa me pāpam vyapohatu śrīkaṇṭhaḥ śrīpatiḥ śrīmān śiva dhyāna rataḥ sadā śiva arcana rataḥ śrīmān sa me pāpam vyapohatu trailokya namitā devī sa ulkā ākārā purātanī śiva arcana rataḥ śrīmān sa me pāpam vyapohatu trailokya nam iti devī sa ulkā ākārā purātanī dākṣāyaṇī mahā devī bhaurī haimavatī śubhā aparṇā avara dā devī vara dāna eka tatparā umā sura harā sākṣāt kauśikī vā kapardinī khaṭvāṅga dhāriṇī divyā kara agra taru pallavā menāyāḥ nandinī devī vāri jā vārija īkṣaṇā aṃbāyāḥ vīta śokasya nandinaḥ ca mahātmanaḥ śubhāvatyāḥ sakhī śāntā pañcacūḍā vara pradā sṛṣṭi artham sarva bhūtānām prakṛti tvam gatā avyayā kṣobhiṇī mohinī nityam yoginām hṛdi saṃsthitā eka aneka sthitā loke indīvara nibha īkṣaṇā saṃstutā jananī teṣām sarva upadrava nāśinī sā me sākṣāt mahādevī pāpām āśu vyapohatu caṇḍaḥ sarva gaṇa īśānaḥ mukhāt śaṃbhoḥ vinirgataḥ śiva arcana rataḥ śrīmān sa me pāpam vyapohatu jāmātā marutām devaḥ sarva bhūta mahā īśvaraḥ sarvagaḥ sarva dṛś śarvaḥ sarva īśa sadṛśaḥ prabhuḥ sa nārāyaṇakaiḥ devaiḥ sa iṃdra candra divākaraiḥ śiva arcana rataḥ śrīmān sa me pāpam vyapohatu meru mandāra kailāsa taṭa kūṭa prabhedanaḥ sapta pātāla pādaḥ ca sapta dvīpa ūru jaṅghakaḥ sapta arṇava aṅkuśaḥ ca eva sarva tīrtha udaraḥ śivaḥ hata asura mahā vṛkṣaḥ brahma vidyā mahā utkaṭaḥ baddhaḥ hṛd puṇḍarīka ākhyeḥ taṃbhe vṛttim nirudhya ca nāga indra vattraḥ yaḥ sākṣāt gaṇa koṭi śataiḥ vṛtaḥ śiva dhyāna eka saṃpannaḥ sa me pāpam vyapohatu bhṛṅgīśaḥ piṅgalākṣa usau bhasita āśaḥ tu dehu yuj śiva arcana rataḥ śrīmān sa me pāpam vyapohatu deva senāpatiḥ śrīmān sa me pāpam vyapohatu ugraḥ bhīmaḥ mahādevaḥ śiva arcana rataḥ sadā etāḥ pāpam vyapohantu mūrtayaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ kapālī īśaḥ ca vijñeyaḥ rudrāḥ rudra aṃśa saṃbhavāḥ śiva praṇāma saṃpannāḥ vyapohantu malam mama loka prakāśakaḥ ca eva loka sākṣī trivikramaḥ ādityaḥ ca tathā sūryaḥ ca aṃsumān ca divākaraḥ gaganam sparśanam tejaḥ rasaḥ ca pṛthivī tathā candraḥ sūryaḥ tathā ātmā ca tanavaḥ śiva bhāṣitāḥ varuṇaḥ vāyu somau ca īśānaḥ bhagavān hariḥ prāṇaḥ prāṇeśa jīveśau mārutaḥ śiva bhāṣitāḥ suṣeṇaḥ śāśvataḥ pṛṣṭaḥ supuṣṭaḥ ca mahābalaḥ ete vai cāraṇāḥ śaṃbhoḥ pūjayā atīva bhāvitāḥ vyapohantu malam sarvam pāpam ca eva mayā kṛtam mantra jñaḥ mantra vid prājñaḥ mantra rāj siddha pūjitaḥ vyapohantu malam sarve siddhāḥ śiva pada arcakāḥ yakṣaḥ yakṣeśa dhanadaḥ jṛṃbhakaḥ maṇibhadrakaḥ pūrṇabhadra īśvaraḥ mālī śitikuṇḍaliḥ eva ca karkoṭakaḥ mahāpadmaḥ śaṅkhapālaḥ mahābalaḥ śiva praṇāma saṃpannāḥ śiva deha prabhūṣaṇāḥ mama pāpam vyapohantu viṣam sthāvara jaṅgamam vīṇā jñaḥ kinnaraḥ ca eva surasenaḥ pramardanaḥ atiśayaḥ sa prayogī gīta jñaḥ ca eva kinnarāḥ śiva praṇāma saṃpannāḥ vyopohantu malam mama vidyādharaḥ ca vibudhaḥ vidyārāśiḥ vidām varaḥ vibuddhaḥ vibudhaḥ śrīmān kṛtajñaḥ ca mahā yaśāḥ vāmadevī mahājaṃbhaḥ kālanemiḥ mahā balaḥ jaṃbhaḥ kuṃbhaḥ ca māyavī kārtavīryaḥ kṛtañjayaḥ garutmān kha gatiḥ ca eva pakṣirāj nāga mardanaḥ nāgaśatruḥ hiraṇyāṅgaḥ vainateyaḥ prabhañjanaḥ nāga āśīrviṣa nāśaḥ ca viṣṇuvāhanaḥ eva ca ete hiraṇya varṇa ābhāḥ garuḍāḥ viṣṇu vāhanāḥ upamanyuḥ tathā anye ca ṛṣayaḥ śiva bhāvitāḥ lakṣmīḥ ca dharaṇī ca eva gāyatrī ca sarasvatī durgāḥ uṣā śacī jyeṣṭhā mātaraḥ sura pūjitāḥ sumukhī durmukhī ca eva kāmukhī kāma vardhanī tathā anyāḥ sarva lokeṣu divyāḥ ca apsarasaḥ tathā śivāya tāṇḍavam nityam kurvantyā utīva bhāvitāḥ śukraḥ śanaiścaraḥ ca eva rāhuḥ ketuḥ tathā eva ca vyapohantu bhayam ghoram grahapīḍām śiva arcakāḥ meṣaḥ vṛṣaḥ utha mithunaḥ tathā karkaṭakaḥ śubhaḥ siṃhaḥ ca kanyā vipulā tulā vai vṛścikaḥ tathā aśvinī bharaṇī ca eva kṛttikā rohiṇī tathā śrīmat mṛgaśiraḥ ca ārdrā punarvasu puṣya sārpakāḥ maghāḥ vai pūrva phaālgunyaḥ uttarā phaālgunī tathā hastaḥ citrā tathā svātī viśākhā ca anurādhikā jyeṣṭhā mūlam mahābhāgā pūrvāṣāḍhā tathā eva ca uttara āṣāḍhikā ca eva śravaṇam ca śraviṣṭhikā śatabhiṣaj pūrvabhadrā tathā proṣṭhapadā tathā mahākarṇaḥ prabhātaḥ ca mahā bhūta pramardanaḥ śyenajit śiva dūtaḥ ca pramathāḥ prīti vardhanāḥ koṭi koṭi śataiḥ ca eva bhūtānām mātaraḥ sadā vyapohantu bhayam pāpam mahādeva prasādataḥ śiva dhyāna eka saṃpannaḥ himarāj aṃbu sannibhaḥ kunda indu sadṛśa ākāraḥ kuṃbha kunda indu bhūṣaṇaḥ catuṣpāda samāyuktaḥ kṣīrodaḥ iva pāṇḍuraḥ rudra loke sthitaḥ nityam rudraiḥ sārdham gaṇa īśvaraiḥ vṛṣā indraḥ viśvadhṛk devaḥ viśvasya jagataḥ pitā gaṅgā mātā jaganmātā rudra loke vyavasthitā śiva bhaktā tu yā nandā sā me pāpam vyapohatu mātā gavām mahābhāgā sā me pāpam vyapohatu rudra pūjā ratā nityam sā me pāpam vyapohatu su śīlā śīla saṃpannā śrī pradā śiva bhāvitā śiva loke sthitā nityam sā me pāpam vyapohatu veda sa astra artha tattva jñaḥ sarva kārya abhicintakaḥ samasta guṇa saṃpannaḥ sarva deva śvara ātmajaḥ jyeṣṭhaḥ sarva īśvaraḥ saumyaḥ mahā viṣṇu tanuḥ svayam āryaḥ senāpatiḥ sākṣāt gahanaḥ makha mardanaḥ airāvata gaja ārūḍhaḥ kṛṣṇa kuñcita mūrdhajaḥ bhūtaiḥ pretaiḥ piśācaiḥ ca kūṣmāṇḍaiḥ ca samāvṛtaḥ śiva arcana rataḥ sākṣāt sa me pāpam vyapohatu yoginī bhīḥ mahā pāpam vyapohantu samāhitāḥ rudrasya tanayaḥ raudraḥ śūla āsakta mahā karaḥ mātṝṇām rakṣakaḥ nityam mahā vṛṣabha vāhanaḥ yajñasya ca śiraḥ chettā pūṣṇaḥ danta vināśanaḥ pādāṅguṣṭhena soma aṅga peṣakaḥ prabhu saṃjñakaḥ sarasvatya mahādevyāḥ nāsikā uṣṭha avakartanaḥ mahā lakṣmīḥ jaganmātā sā me pāpam vyapohatu mahāmohā mahābhāgā mahā bhūta gaṇaiḥ vṛtā sarvadā sarva gā devī sā me pāpam vyapohatu siṃha ārūḍhā mahā devī pārvatyāḥ tanayā avyayā viṣṇoḥ nidrā mahāmāyā vaiṣṇavī sura pūjitā śiva arcana ratā durgā sā me pāpam brahmāṇḍa dhārakāḥ rudrāḥ sarva loka prapūjitāḥ satyāḥ ca mānasāḥ sarve vyapohantu bhayam mama kūṣmāṇḍakāḥ ca te pāpam vyapohantu samāhitāḥ praṇamya śirasā bhūmau pratimāse dvijottamāḥ yat puṇyam ca eva tīrthānām yajñānām ca eva yat phalam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ vyapohana stavam puṇyam śrutam asmābhiḥ ādarāt sūtaḥ uvāca tāni vyāsāt upaśrutya yuṣmākam pravadāmi aham varṣam ekam tu bhuñjānaḥ naktam yaḥ pūjayet śivam snāpya rudram mahādevam saṃpūjya vidhi pūrvakam yāvakam ca odanam dattvā sa kṣīram sa ghṛtam dvijāḥ bhījayet brāhmaṇān śiṣṭān japet śāntim viśeṣataḥ kṛṣṇam go mithunam dadyāt pūjayet ca eva saṃkaram paurṇamāsyām mahādevam snāpya saṃpūjya śaṅkaram dadyāt go mithunam vā api tāmra ābham śūlapāṇaye śāli annam payasā yuktam ghṛtena ca yathāsukham goṣṭha śāyī muni śreṣṭhāḥ kṣitau niśi bhavam smaret paurṇamāsyām śivam snāpya dadyāt go mithunam sitam jyeṣṭhe māse ca deveśam bhavam śarvam umāpatim saṃpūjya śraddhayā bhaktyā kṛtvā vai naktabhojanam raktaśāli anna madhvā ca ā adbhiḥ pūtam ghṛta ādibhiḥ vīrāsanī niśārdham ca gavām śuśrūpaṇe rataḥ snāpya śaktyā yathānyāyam carum dadyāt ca śūline dhūmram go mithunam dattvā vāyu loke mahīyate āṣāḍhe māsi ca api evam naktabhojana tatparaḥ bhūri khaṇḍa ājya saṃmiśram saktubhiḥ ca eva gorasam paurṇamāsyām ghṛta ādyaiḥ tu snāpya pūjya yathāvidhi brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā ca śrotriyān vedapāragān śrāvaṇe ca dvijāḥ māse kṛtvā vai naktabhojanam paurṇamāsyām ghṛta ādyaiḥ tu snāpya pūjya yathāvidhi brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā ca śrotriyān vedapāragān paurṇamāsyām tu deveśam snāpya saṃpūjya śaṅkaram nīla skandham vṛṣam gām ca dattvā bhaktyā yathāvidhi tatas ca āśvayuje māsi kṛtvā evam naktabhojanam gām ca da tvā yathānyāya maiśānam lokam āpnuyāt paurṇamāsyām ca vidhivat snāpya dattvā carum punar dattvā go mithunam ca eva kāpilam mārgasīrṣe ca kṛtvā evam naktabhojanam yava annena yathānyāyam ājya kṣīra ādibhiḥ samam paurṇamāsyām ca pūrva uktam kṛtvā śarvāya śaṃbhave dattvā go mithunam ca eva pāṇḍuram vidhi pūrvakam kuryāt varṣam krameṇa eva vyutkrameṇa api vā dvijāḥ sa yāti śiva sāyujyam jñāna yogam avāpnuyāt sūtaḥ uvāca nara nārī ādi jantūnām hitāya muni sattamāḥ ratha ādyaiḥ vā api devaśam nītvā rudra ālayam prati sa yāti śivā sāyujyam nārī devyāḥ yadi prabho varṣam ekam na bhuñjati kanyā vā vidhavā api vā pratiṣṭhāpya yathānyāyam dattvā rudra ālaye punar yā nārī evam caret abdam kṛṣṇām ekām caturdaśīm varṣa ante pratimām kṛtvā yena kena api vā dvijāḥ snāpya īśānam yajet bhaktyā sahasraiḥ kamalaiḥ sitaiḥ rājatam kamalam ca eva jāṃbūnada su karṇikam dattvā bhavāya viprebhyaḥ pradadyāt dakṣiṇām api sāyujyam ca evam āpnoti bhavānyāḥ dvijasattamāḥ kuryāt yat vā naraḥ saḥ api rudra sāyujyam āpnuyāt niyogāt eva tat kāryam bhartṝṇām dvijasattamāḥ japam dānam tapaḥ sarvam asvatantrāḥ yatas striyaḥ sā bhavānyāḥ ca sāyujyam sārūyam ca api suvratā kārtikyām vā tu yā nārī eka bhaktena vartate dadyāt kṛṣṇa tilānām ca bhāram ekam atandriyā sa ghṛtam sa guḍam ca eva odanam parameṣṭhine bhavānyā modate sārdham sārūpyam prāpya suvratā mārgaśīrṣakam āsādi kārtikya antam yata ākramam vratam su vipulam puṇyam nandinā paribhāṣitam vṛṣam pūrṇa aṅgam uttamam sā ca sārdham bhavānyā vai modate na atra saṃśayaḥ puṣya māse tu vai śūlam pratiṣṭhāpya nivedayet dadyāt saṃpūjya deveśam brāhmaṇān ca eva bhojayet phaālgune pratimām kṛtvā hiraṇyena yathāvidhi caitre bhavam kumāram ca bhavānīm ca yathāvidhi bhavānyā modate sārdham dattvā rudrāya śaṃbhave kṛtvā ālayam hi kauberam rājatam rajatena vai īśvara umā samāyuktam gaṇeśaiḥ ca samantataḥ jyeṣṭhe māsi mahādevam liṅga mūrtim umāpatim haṃsena ca varāheṇa kṛtvā tāmra ādibhiḥ śubhām śivāya śivam āsādya śiva sthāne yathāvidhi brāhmaṇaiḥ sahitām sthāpya devyāḥ sāyujyam āpnuyāt āṣāḍhe ca śubhe māse gṛham kṛtvā su śobhanam dāsī dāsa ādibhiḥ ca eva śayanaiḥ asana ādibhiḥ saṃpūrṇaiḥ ca gṛham vastraiḥ ācchādya ca samantataḥ devam ghṛta ādibhiḥ snāpya mahādevam umāpatim prathama āśramiṇam bhaktyā saṃpūjya ca yathāvidhi kanyām sumadhyamām yāvat kāla jīvana saṃyutām bhavānyā sadṛśībhūtvā sarva kalpeṣu sā avyayā brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā ca dāpayet ca yathāvidhi sā ca sūrya aṃśu saṃkāśā bhavānyā saha modate suvarṇa vastra yaṃyuktam dattvā saṃpūjya śaṅkaram sarva dhānya samāyuktam sarva bīja rasa ādibhiḥ dakṣiṇe ca yathānyāyam brahmāṇam ca catur mukham indra ādi lokapālān ca kṛtvā bhaktyā yathāvidhi vāme pāśam bhavānyāḥ ca kamalam hema bhūṣitam viṣṇoḥ ca śaṅkham cakram ca gadām abjam prayatnataḥ brahmaṇaḥ ca akṣa sūtram ca kamaṇḍalum anuttamam yamasya daṇḍam nirṛteḥ khaḍgam niśicarasya tu varuṇasya mahā pāśam nāga ākhyam rudram adbhutam vāyoḥ yā aṣṭim kuberasya gadām loka prapūjitām ṭaṅkam ca īśāna devasya nivedya evam krameṇa ca mahā meru vratam kṛtvā mahādevāya dāpayet kārtikyām api yā nārī kṛtvā devīm umām śubhām hema tāmra ādibhiḥ ca eva pratiṣṭhāpya vidhānataḥ lokapālaiḥ tathā siddhaiḥ saṃvṛtam sthāpya yatnataḥ mārgaśīrṣaka māsa ādi kārtika antam pravartitam nara nārī ādi jantūnām hitāya muni sattamāḥ nārī devyā na saṃdehaḥ śivena paribhāṣitam sūtaḥ uvāca japāt eva na saṃdehaḥ vratānām vai viśeṣataḥ samāptiḥ na anyathā tasmāt japet pañcākṣarīm śubhām ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ katham pañcākṣarī vidyā prabhāvaḥ vā katham vada sūtaḥ uvāca pārvatyāḥ kathitam puṇyam pravadāmi samāsatas śrī devī uvāca śrī bhagavān uvāca vācyaḥ pañca akṣaraiḥ devi śivaḥ trailokya pūjitaḥ vācakaḥ paramaḥ mantraḥ tasya pañca akṣaraḥ sthitaḥ jñātvā prayogam vidhinā ca siddhim labdhvā tathā pañcamukhaḥ mahātmā tam ārādhayitum devam parātparataram śivam tatas tutoṣa bhagavān trimūrtīnām paraḥ śivaḥ te api labdhvā varān viprāḥ tad ārādhana kāṅkṣiṇaḥ tiṣṭhantaḥ anugraha arthāya devi te ṛṣayaḥ purā pañcākṣaram ṛṣicchandau daivatam śakti bījavat tat śrutvā mantra māhātmyam ṛṣayaḥ te tapodhanāḥ mantrasya viniyogam ca kṛtvā sarvam anuṣṭhitāḥ varṇān varṇa vibhāgān ca sarva dharmān ca śobhanān yasya evam hṛdi saṃsthaḥ yam mantraḥ syāt pārameśvaraḥ pañca akṣaraiḥ sa praṇavaḥ mantraḥ yam hṛdayam mama guhyāt guhyataram sākṣāt mokṣa jñānam anuktamam bījam śaktim svaram varṇam sthānam ca eva akṣaram prati nakāra ādīni bījāni pañcabhūta ātmakāni ca tvadīyam praṇavam kiñcid madīyam praṇavam tathā tvadīyam devim mantrāṇām śakti bhūtam na saṃśayaḥ akāra ukāra makārāḥ madīye praṇave sthitāḥ ukāram ca makāram ca akāram ca krameṇa vai oṅkārasya svara udāttaḥ ṛṣiḥ brahma sitam vapuḥ nakāraḥ pīta varṇaḥ ca sthānam pūrva mukham smṛtam indraḥ adhidaivatam chandaḥ gāyatrī gautamaḥ ṛṣiḥ makāraḥ kṛṣma varṇaḥ usya sthānam vai dākṣiṇāmukham chandaḥ anuṣṭubh ṛṣiḥ ca atrī rudraḥ daivatam ucyate śikāraḥ dhūmra varṇaḥ usya sthānam vai paścimam mukham viśvāmitraḥ ṛṣiḥ triṣṭubh chandaḥ viṣṇuḥ tu daivatam brahma adhidaivatam chandaḥ bṛhatī ca aṅgirāḥ ṛṣiḥ yakāraḥ rakta varṇaḥ ca sthānum ūrdhvam mukham virāj utpattiḥ brahmacārīṇām gṛhasthānām sthitiḥ sadā yatīnām saṃhṛtiḥ nyāsaḥ siddhiḥ bhavati na anyathā aṅganyāsaḥ karanyāsaḥ dehanyāsaḥ iti tridhā aṅganyāsam tatas paścāt akṣarāṇām vidhi kramāt pāda ādi mūrdha paryantam saṃhāraḥ bhavati priye hṛdaya āsya gala nyāsaḥ sthiti nyāsaḥ udāhṛtaḥ sa śiraskam tatas deham sarvamantreṇa saṃspṛśet atīva bhoga daḥ devī sthiti nyāsaḥ kuṭuṃbinām kara nyāsam purā kṛttvā taram aṅganyāsam nyaset paścāt eṣa sādhāraṇaḥ vidhiḥ oṅkāram saṃpuṭīkṛtya sarva aṅgeṣu ca vinyaset karayoḥ ubhayoḥ ca eva daśa agrām guliṣu kramāt
