smaret pūrvam ṛṣin chandaḥ daivatam bījam eva ca śaktim ca paramātmānam gurum ca eva varānane mantreṇa pāṇī saṃmṛjya talayoḥ praṇavam nyaset aṅgulīnām ca sarveṣām tathā ca ādi anta parvasu ubhābhyām eva pāṇibhyām āpādatalamastakam mantreṇa saṃspṛśet deham praṇavena eva saṃpuṭam kṛtvā aṅganyāsam evam hi mukhāni parikalpayet ṣaḍaṅgāni nyaset paścāt yathāsthānam ca śobhanam praṇavam hṛdayam vidyāt nakāraḥ śiraḥ ucyate śikhā makāraḥ ākhyātaḥ śikāraḥ kavacam tathā vākāraḥ netram astram tu yakāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ vighneśaḥ mātaraḥ durgā kṣetrajñaḥ devatāḥ diśaḥ aṅguṣṭha tarjanī agrābhyām saṃsthāpya su mukham śubham gale madhye tathā aṅguṣṭhe tarjanī ādyā aṅgulīṣu ca evam vinyasya medhāvī śuddha kāyaḥ dṛḍha vrataḥ japet pañcākṣaram mantram labdhvā ācārya prasādataḥ evam ca dakṣiṇā siddham mantram siddham yatas tatas śuśrūṣum anahaṅkāram upavāsa kṛśam śucim puraścaraṇa jāpī vā api vā nitya jāpakaḥ āsanam ruciram baddhvā maunī ca ekāgra mānasaḥ ādya antayoḥ japasya api kuryāt vai prāṇasaṃyamān tathā ca ante japet bījam śatam aṣṭa uttaram śubham pañca akṣarasya mantrasya prāṇāyāmaḥ udāhṛtaḥ indriyāṇām vaśi tvam ca tasmāt prāṇān ca saṃyamet nadyām śata sahasram tu anantaḥ śiva sannidhau aṅgulī japa saṃkhyānam ekam ekam śubha ānane pañcaviṃśati mokṣa artham saptaviṃśati pauṣṭikam triṃśat ca dhana saṃpattyai pañcāśat ca ābhicārikam śṛṇuṣva sarva yajñebhyaḥ japa yajñaḥ viśiṣyate jāpinam na upasarpanti bhaya bhītāḥ samantataḥ evam labdhvā śivam jñānam jñātvā japa vidhi kramam yasmāt ācāra hīnasya sādhanam niṣphalam bhavet sat ācāravatām puṃsām sarvatra api abhayam bhavet tadvat ācāra hīnānām sarvatra eva bhayam bhavet sat ācāreṇa deva tvam ṛṣi tvam ca varānane durvṛttaḥ śuddhi bhūyiṣṭhaḥ pāpīyān jñāna dūṣakaḥ udaya astamayāt pūrvam āramya vidhinā śuciḥ kāmāt mohāt bhayāt lobhāt saṃdhyām na atikramet dvijaḥ saṃdhyā atikramaṇāt vipraḥ brāhmaṇyāt patate yatas anṛtam paruṣam śāṭhyam paiśunyam pāpa hetukam kvacid ca api na kurvīta vācā ca manasā tathā śūdra annam yātayāma annam naivedyam śrāddham eva ca a snātvā na ca bhuñjīyāt a japa ugnim a pūjya ca parṇa pṛṣṭhe na bhuñjīyāt rātrau dīpam vinā tathā bhinna bhāṇḍe ca rathyāyām patitānām ca saṃnidhau śūdra śeṣam na bhuñjīyāt saha annam śisukaiḥ api śuddha annam snigdham aśrīyāt saṃskṛtam ca abhimantritam bhoktā śivaḥ iti smṛtvā maunī ca ekāgra mānasaḥ āsyena na pibet toyam tiṣṭhan añjalinā api vā vāma hastena śayyāyām tathā eva anyaṃkareṇa vā vibhītaka arka kārañja snuhi chāyām na ca āśrayet staṃbha dīpa manuṣyāṇām anyeṣām praṇinām tathā na avaroheta kūpa ādim na ārohet ucca pādapān sūrya agni jala devānām gurūṇām vimukhaḥ śubhe na kuryāt iha kāryāṇi japa karma śubhāni vā agnau na tāpayet pādau hastam padbhyām na saṃspṛśet na jalam tāḍayet padbhyām na aṃbhasi aṅga malam tyajet malam prakṣālayet tī re prakṣālya snānam ācaret aśrī karam manuṣyāṇām asuddham saṃspṛśet yadi aja aśvān akhara uṣṭrāṇām mārjanāt tupa reṇukān saspṛśīt yādi mūḍha ātmā śriyam hanti hareḥ api sphigvātam śūrpavātam ca vātam prāṇa mukha anilam sukṛtāni haranti ete saṃspṛṣṭāḥ puruṣasya tu śva nīca darśanam nidrā pralāpāḥ te japa dviṣaḥ eteṣām saṃbhave vā api kuryāt sūrya ādi darśanam ācamya vā japet śeṣam kṛtvā vā prāṇasaṃyamam ete jyotīṃṣi proktāni vidvadbhiḥ bāhmaṇaiḥ tathā an āsanaḥ śayānaḥ vā rathyāyām śūdra sannidhau rakta bhūmyām ca khaṭvāyām na japet jāpakaḥ tathā kauśeyam vyāghra carmam vā cailam taulam atha api vā dāravam tāla parṇam vā āsanam parikalpayet trisaṃdhyam tu guroḥ pūjā kartavyā hitam icchatā saguṇaḥ nirguṇaḥ vā api tasya ājñām śirasā vahet yathā prajvalitaḥ vahniḥ viṣṭhā kāṣṭham ca nirdahet guruḥ tuṣṭaḥ dahati evam pāpam tat mantra tejasā kurvanti anugraham tuṣṭāḥ gurau tuṣṭe na saṃśayaḥ evam ācāravān bhaktaḥ nityam japa parāyaṇaḥ yasya yena viyuñjīta kāryeṇa tu viśeṣataḥ rājya aiśvaryam ca vijñānam svargaḥ nirvāṇe eva ca prokṣaṇam ca abhiṣekam ca aghamarṣaṇam eva ca snāne ca saṃdhyayoḥ ca eva kuryāt ekādaśena vai dūrvā aṅkurāḥ tilāḥ vāṇī guḍūcī guṭikā tathā teṣām tu daśa sāhasram homam āyuṣya vardhanam aśvattha vṛkṣam āśritya japet lakṣa dvayam sudhīḥ śanaiścara dine spṛṣṭvā dīrgha yuṣyam labhet naraḥ śanaiścara dine aśvattham pāṇibhyām saṃspṛśet sudhīḥ samasta vyādhi śānti artham palāśa samidaiḥ naraḥ ekādaśena bhuñjīyāt annam ca eva abhimantritam bhakṣyam ca anyat tathā peyam viṣam api amṛtam bhavet japet lakṣam tu pūrvāhṇe hutvā ca aṣṭaśatena vai nadī toyena saṃpūrṇam ghaṭam saṃspṛśya śobhanam japtvā ayutam ca tad snānāt rogāṇām bheṣajam bhavet aṣṭāviṃśat japitvā annam aśrīyāt anvaham śuciḥ hutvā ca tāvat pālaśaiḥ evam vā ārogyam aśnute candra sūrya grahe pūrvam upoṣya vidhinā śuciḥ yāvat grahaṇa mokṣam tu tāvat nadyām samāhitaḥ aṣṭottarasahasreṇa pibet brāhmī rasam dvijāḥ ehikām labhate medhām sarva śāstra dharām śubhām sārasvatī bhavet devī tasya vāc ati mānuṣī graha nakṣatra pīḍāsu japet bhaktyā ayutam naraḥ duḥsvapna darśane snātvā japet vai ca ayutam naraḥ ghṛte na aṣṭaśatam hutvā sadyas śāntiḥ bhaviṣyati gajānām turagāṇām tu go jātīnām viśeṣataḥ vyādhi āgame śuciḥ bhūtvā juhuyāt samidh āhutim utpāte śatru bādhāyām juhuyāt ayutam śuciḥ pālāśa samidhaiḥ devi tasya śāntiḥ bhaviṣyati ābhicārika bādhāyām etat devi samācaret pratyak bhavati tad śaktiḥ śatroḥ pīḍā bhaviṣyati vidveṣaṇa artham juhuyāt vaibhīta samidhā aṣṭakam viṣeṇa rudhira abhyaktaḥ vidveṣāṇa karam nṛṇām prayāścittam pravakṣyāmi sarva pāpa a viśuddhaye pāpa śuddhiḥ na ced puṃsaḥ kriyāḥ sarvāḥ ca niṣphalāḥ jñānam ca hīyate tasmāt kartavyam pāpa śodhanam vidyā alakṣmī viśuddhi artham mām dhyātvā añjalinā śubhe śivena ekā daśena adbhiḥ abhiṣiñcet samantataḥ aṣṭottaraśatena eva snāyāt pāpa viśuddhaye saṃdhyā upāsana vicchede japet aṣṭaśatam naraḥ spṛṣṭam annam na bhuñjīta bhuktvā ca aṣṭaśatam japet upapātaka duṣṭānām tad ardham parikīrtitam śeṣāṇām api pāpānām japet pañca sahasrakam ātma bodha param guhyam śiva bodha prakāśakam japet ca pañca lakṣam tu vigṛhīta indriyaḥ śuciḥ pañca indriyāṇām vijayaḥ bhaviṣyati varānane bhūtānām iha pañcānām vijayam manujaḥ labhet samyak vijayam āpnoti karaṇānām varānane pañcaviṃśati lakṣāṇām japena kamala ānane pañcaviṃśati tattvānām vijayam manujaḥ labhet madhyarātre ca śivayoḥ paśyati eva na saṃśayaḥ andhakāra vināśaḥ ca dīpasya iva prakāśanam sa bīja saṃpuṭam mantram śata lakṣam japet śuciḥ śravayet ca dvijān śuddhān pañcākṣara vidhi kramam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ japāt śreṣṭhātamam prāhuḥ brāhmaṇāḥ dagdha kilbiṣāḥ viraktānām prabuddhānām dhyāna yajñam su śobhanam tasmāt vadasva sūta adya dhyāna yajñam aśeṣatas vistārāt sarva yatnena viraktānām mahātmanām rudreṇa kathitam prāha guhām prāpya mahātmanām saṃhṛtya kālakūṭa ākhyam viṣam vai viśvakarmaṇā sūtaḥ uvāca guhām prāpya sukha āsīnam bhavānyā saha śaṅkaram munayaḥ saṃśita ātmānaḥ preṇamuḥ tam guhā āśrayam astuvan ca tatas sarve nīlakaṇṭham umāpatim ati ugram kālakūṭa ākhyam saṃhṛtam bhagavan tvayā atas pratiṣṭhitam sarvam tvayā deva vṛṣadhvaja kim anena dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ viṣam vakṣye su dāruṇam na viṣam kālakūṭa ākhyam saṃsāraḥ viṣam ucyate saṃsāraḥ dvividhaḥ proktaḥ sva adhikāra anurūpataḥ puṃsām saṃmūḍha cittānām asaṃkṣīṇaḥ su dāruṇaḥ īṣaṇā rāga doṣā imā sargaḥ jñānena suvratāḥ asat nikṛṣṭe tu arthe api śāstram tad śravaṇāt satām buddhim utpādayati eva saṃsāre viduṣām dvijāḥ tasmāt dṛṣṭa ānuśravikam duṣṭam iti ubhaya ātmakam śāstram iti ucyate a bhāgam śruteḥ karmasu tat dvijāḥ mūrdhānam brahmaṇaḥ sāram ṛṣīṇām karmaṇaḥ phalam nanu svabhāvaḥ sarveṣām kāmaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ na ca anyathā śrutuḥ pravartikā teṣām iti karmaṇya tā dvidaḥ nivṛtti lakṣaṇaḥ dharmaḥ samarthānām miha ucyate kalā saṃśoṣam āyāti karmaṇā anya svabhāvataḥ nārakī pāpa kṛt svargī puṇya kṛt puṇya gairavāt vyatimiśreṇa vai jīvaḥ caturdhā saṃvyavasthitaḥ evam vyavasthitaḥ dehī karmaṇā ajñaḥ hi anirvṛtaḥ ṣaṣ kauśikam samudbhūtam bhajati eṣa kalevaram vicārataḥ satām duḥkham strī saṃsarga ādi bhīḥ dvijāḥ duḥkhena ekena vai duḥkham praśāmyanti iha duḥkhinaḥ tasmāt vicārataḥ nā asti saṃyogāt api vai nṛṇām paiśāce rākṣase duḥkham yākṣe ca eva prājāpatye tathā brāhme prākṛte pauruṣe tathā kṣaya sātiśaya ādyaiḥ tu duḥkhaiḥ duḥkhāni suvratāḥ tāni bhāgyāni aśuddhāni saṃtyajet ca dhanāni ca tasmāt aṣṭaguṇam bhogam tathā ṣoḍaśadhā sthitam caturviśat prakāreṇa saṃsthitam ca api suvratāḥ dvātriṃśat bhedam anaghāḥ catvāriṃśadguṇam punar tathā aṣṭacatvāriṃśca ṣaṣ pañcaṭāśat prakārataḥ catuḥṣaṣṭividham ca eva duḥkham eva vivekinaḥ pārthivam ca tathā āpyam ca taijasam ca vicārataḥ ābhimānikam api evam bauddham prākṛtam eva ca duḥkham eva na saṃdehaḥ yoginām brahma vādinām vartamānāni duḥkhāni bhaviṣyāṇi yathātatham doṣa duṣṭeṣu deśeṣu duḥkhāni vividhāni ca kṣudh vyādheḥ parihāra artham na sukhāya annam ucyate yathā itareṣām rogāṇām auṣadham na sukhāya tat śīta uṣṇa vāta varṣa ādyaiḥ tad tad kāleṣu dehinām duḥkham eva na saṃdehaḥ na jānānti hi apaṇḍitāḥ svarge pi evam muni śreṣṭhāḥ hi aviśuddha kṣaya ādibhiḥ rogauḥ nānāvidhaiḥ grastā rāga dveṣa bhaya ādibhiḥ puṇya vṛkṣa kṣayāt tadvat gām patanti divaukasaḥ narake duḥkham eva atra nākāṇām niṣevaṇāt vihita akaraṇāt ca eva varṇinām muni puṅgavāḥ evam yatiḥ dhyāna paraḥ mahātmā saṃsāra bhītaḥ na labheta nidrām kīṭa pakṣi mṛgāṇām ca paśūnām gaja vājinām dṛṣṭam eva asukham tasmāt tyajataḥ sukham uttamam vaimānikānām api evam duḥkham kalpa adhikāriṇām sthāna abhimāninām ca eva manu ādīnām ca suvratāḥ devānām ca eva daityānām anyonya vijigīṣayā duḥkham eva nṛpāṇām ca rākṣasānām jagattraye śrama artham āśramaḥ ca api varṇānām paramārthataḥ āśramaiḥ na ca devaiḥ ca yajñaḥ sāṃkhyaiḥ vrataiḥ tathā bhasma śāyī bhavet nityam vrate pāśupate budhaḥ pañcārtha jñāna saṃpannaḥ śivatattve samāhitaḥ pañcārtha yoga saṃpannaḥ duḥkha antam vrajate sudhīḥ jyotiṣam ca aparā vidyā para akṣaram iti sthitam tat adṛśyam tat agrāhyam agotram tat avarṇakam tat a cakṣuḥ tat a śrotram tat a pāṇi a pādakam tat ajātam abhūtam ca tat aśabdam dvijottamāḥ avyayam ca apratiṣṭham ca tat nityam sarvagam vibhum mahāntam tad gṛham tam ca tat ajam cit mayam dvijāḥ asaṃvṛtam tat ātmā ekam parā vidyā na ca anyathā mattaḥ na anyat iti īkṣeta manaḥ vāc pāṇibhiḥ tathā sarvam ātmani saṃpaśyet sat ca asat ca samāhitaḥ adhas dṛṣṭyā vitastyām tu nābhyām upari tiṣṭhati hṛdayam tat vijānīyāt viśvasya āyatanam mahat chidrāṇi ca diśaḥ yasya prāṇa ādyāḥ ca pratiṣṭhitāḥ prāṇa ādyaiḥ ca eva saṃyuktaḥ paśyate bahudhā kramāt daśa prāṇa vahāḥ nāḍyaḥ pratyekam muni puṅgavāḥ netra sthan jāgratam vidyāt kaṇṭhe svapnam samādiśet suṣuptam hṛdaya stham tu turīyam mūrdhani sthitam jāgre brahmā ca viṣṇuḥ ca svapne ca eva yathākramāt īśvaraḥ tu suṣupte tu turīye ca maheśvaraḥ ṇa naḥ buddhiḥ ahaṅkāram cittam ca uti catuṣṭayam karaṇāni vilīnāni yadā sva ātmani suvratāḥ paraḥ turīya atītaḥ usau śivaḥ parama kāraṇam manaḥ buddiḥ ahaṅkāraḥ cittam ca iti catuṣṭayam adhyātmam pṛthak eva idam caturdaśavidham smṛtam rasitavyam muni śreṣṭhāḥ sparśitavyam tathā eva ca tathā cetayitavyam ca vaktavyam muni puṅgavāḥ ādityaḥ api diśaḥ ca eva pṛthivī varuṇaḥ tathā vāyuḥ candraḥ tathā brahmā rudraḥ kṣetrajñaḥ eva ca rājñī sudarśanā ca eva jitā saumyā yathākramam moghā rudra amṛtā satyā madhyamā ca dvijottamāḥ nāḍī rāśi śukā ca eva asurā ca eva kṛttikā bhāsvatī nāḍayaḥ ca etāḥ caturdaśa nibandhanāḥ vāyavaḥ nāḍi madhya sthāḥ vāhakāḥ ca caturdaśa kūrmakaḥ ca tathā śyenaḥ śvetaḥ kṛṣmaḥ tathā anilaḥ yaḥ cakṣuṣu atha draṣṭavye tathā āditye ca suvratāḥ nāḍyām prāṇe ca vijñāne tu ānande ca yathākramam ātmā ekaḥ ca carati tam upāsīta mām prabhum eṣa sarva adhipaḥ devaḥ tu antaryāmī mahā dyutiḥ upāsyamānaḥ vedaiḥ ca śāstraiḥ nānāvidhaiḥ api na vā eṣa veda śāstrāṇi sarvajñe yāsyati prabhuḥ sva ātmanā rakṣitam ca adyāt anna bhūtam na kutracid sarvatra prāṇinām annam prāṇinām granthiḥ asmi aham praśāstā nayanaḥ ca eva pañca ātmā sa vibhāgaśaḥ prāṇa mayaḥ ca indriya ātmā saṃkalpa ātmā manaḥ mayaḥ kāla ātmā somaḥ eva iha vijñāna mayaḥ ucyate sadānanda mayaḥ bhūtvā maheśaḥ parameśvaraḥ na prajñāna ghanaḥ tu evam na prājñaḥ jñāna pūrvakaḥ viditam na asti vedyam ca nirvāṇam paramārthataḥ nirvāṇam ca eva kaivalyam niḥśreyasam anāmayam prasannam ca yat ekāgram tadā jñānam iti smṛtam ajñāna mala pūrva tvāt puruṣaḥ malinaḥ smṛtaḥ jñānam ekam vinā nā asti puṇya pāpa parikṣayaḥ jñāna abhyāsa rataḥ nityam jñāna tattva artha vid svayam varṇa āśrama abhimānī yaḥ tyakta krodhaḥ dvijottamāḥ anyatra ramate mūḍhaḥ saḥ ajñānī na atra saṃśayaḥ saṃsāra hetuḥ ajñānam saṃsāraḥ tanu saṃgrahaḥ mokṣa hetuḥ tathā jñānam muktaḥ sva ātmani avasthitaḥ ajñāne sati vipra indrāḥ krodha ādyāḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ dharma adharmau hi teṣām ca tad vaśāt tanu saṃgrahaḥ śarīre sati vai kleśaḥ saḥ vidyām saṃtyajet budhaḥ krodha ādyāḥ nāśam āyānti dharma adharmau ca vai dvijāḥ saḥ eva muktaḥ saṃsārāt duḥkha traya vivarjitaḥ evam jñānam vinā nā asti dhyānam dhyātuḥ dvijarṣabhāḥ jñānam guroḥ hi saṃparkāt na vācā paramārthataḥ sahaja āgantukam pāpam asthi vāc udbhavam tathā jñāna agniḥ dahate kṣipram suṣka indhanam iva analaḥ abhyaset ca sadā jñānam sarva saṃga vivarjitaḥ jñānam yathā tathā dhyānam tasmāt dhyānam samabhyaset dhyānam nirviṣayam proktam ādau sa viṣayam tathā ṣaṣ prakāram samabhyasya cutaḥ ṣaṣ daśabhiḥ tathā tathā dvādaśadhā ca eva punar ṣoḍaśadhā kramāt śuddha jāṃbūnada ākāram vadhūma aṅgāra sannibham ahiṃsakaḥ satya vādī asteyī sarva yatnataḥ saṃtuṣṭaḥ śauca saṃpannaḥ svādhyāya nirataḥ sadā na budhyati tathā dhyātā sthāpya cittam dvijottamāḥ na ghrāti na śṛṇoti eva līnaḥ sva ātmani yaḥ svayam kālarudra ākhyaḥ vāyu tattve maheśvaraḥ suṣire sa śivaḥ sākṣāt kramāt evam vicintayet kṣitau śarvaḥ smṛtaḥ devaḥ hi apām bhave iti smṛtaḥ bhīmaḥ suṣira nāke asau bhāskare maṇḍale sthitaḥ īśānaḥ somabiṃve ca mahādevaḥ iti smṛtaḥ puṃsā paśupatiḥ devaḥ ca aṣṭadhā aham vyavasthitaḥ kāṭhinyam yat tanau sarvam pārthivam parigīyate yat saṃcarati tat vāyuḥ suṣiram yat dvijottamāḥ tat ākāśam ca vijñānam śabda jam vyoma saṃbhavam tathā eva viprāḥ vijñānam sparśa ākhyam vāyu saṃbhavam rūpam vāhneyam iti uktam āpyam rasa mayam dvijāḥ gandha ākhyam pārthivam bhūyas cintayet bhāskaram kramāt netre ca dakṣiṇe vāme somam hṛdī vibhum dvijāḥ ājānu pṛthivī tattvam ānābheḥ vāri maṇḍalam ākaṇṭham vahni tattvam syāt lalāṭa antam dvijottamāḥ vāyavyam vai lalāṭa ādyam vyoma ākhyam vā śikhā agrakam na jīvaḥ prakṛtiḥ sattvam rajaḥ ca atha tamaḥ punar vyoma ādīni ca bhūtāni na eva iha paramārthataḥ vyāpya tiṣṭhat yatas viśvam sthāṇuḥ iti abhidhīyate dadhāti bhūmiḥ ākāśam avakāśam dadāti ca tad ājñayā tatam sarvam tasmāt vai cintayet dvijāḥ tena eva adhiṣṭhitam tasmāt etat sarvam dvijottamāḥ saṃsāra viṣa taptānām jñāna dhyāna amṛtena vai pratīkāraḥ samākhyātaḥ na anyathā dvijasattamāḥ vairāgyāt paramam jñānam paramārtha prakāśakam yoga siddhyā vimuktiḥ syāt sattva niṣṭhasya na anyathā sattva śaktim samāsthāya śivam abhyarcayet dvijāḥ sarvatas dharma niṣṭhaḥ ca sadā utsāhī samāhitaḥ ṛju svabhāvaḥ satatam svastha cittaḥ mṛduḥ sadā amānī buddhimān śāntaḥ tyakta spardhaḥ dvijottamāḥ sadā mumukṣuḥ dharma jñaḥ sva ātma lakṣaṇa lakṣaṇaḥ ṛṇa traya vinirmuktaḥ pūrva janmani puṇya bhāj jarā yuktaḥ dvijaḥ bhūtvā śraddhayā ca guroḥ kramāt anyathā vā api śuśrūṣām kṛtvā kṛtrima varjitaḥ āsādya bhāratam varṣam brahma vid jāyate dvijāḥ saṃparkāt jñānam āsādya jñāninaḥ yoga vid bhavet kramaḥ yam mala pūrṇasya jñāna prāpteḥ dvijottamāḥ tasmāt anena mārgeṇa tyakta saṃgaḥ dṛḍha vrataḥ saṃsāra kālakūṭa ākhyāt mucyate muni puṅgavāḥ jñānasya eva iha māhātmyam prasaṃgāt iha śobhanam evam pāśupatam yogam kathitam tu īśvareṇa tu na deyam yasya kasya api śiva uktam muni puṅgavāḥ sūtaḥ uvāca niśamya te mahā prājñāḥ kumāra ādyāḥ pinākinam procuḥ praṇamya vai bhītāḥ prasannam parameśvaram sūtaḥ uvāca prāhatām aṃbikām prekṣya praṇipatya sthitān dvijān akārtajñaḥ paśuḥ jīvaḥ vibhuḥ bhoktā hi aṇuḥ pumān māyī ca māyayā baddhaḥ karmabhiḥ yujyate tu saḥ jñānam dhyānam ca bandhaḥ ca mokṣaḥ na asti ātmanaḥ dvijāḥ eṣā vidyā hi aham vedyaḥ prajñā eṣā ca śrutiḥ smṛtiḥ dhṛtiḥ eṣā mayā niṣṭhā jñāna śaktiḥ kriyā tathā icchā ākhyā ca tathā hi ajñā dve vidye na ca saṃśayaḥ na hi eṣā prakṛtiḥ jaivī vikṛtiḥ ca vicārataḥ purā mama ajña mad vaktrāt samutpannā sanātanī tām ājñām saṃpraviśya aham cintayan jagatām hitam saptaviṃśat prakāreṇa sarvam vyāpya anayā śivaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca bhavānī ca tam ālokya māyām aharat avyayā te māyā mala nirmuktāḥ munayaḥ prekṣya pārvatīm prītāḥ babhūvuḥ muktāḥ ca tasmāt eṣā parā gatiḥ dvidhā asau rūpam āsthāya sthitaḥ eva na saṃśayaḥ prasādena kṣaṇāt muktiḥ pratijñā eṣā na saṃśayaḥ eṣaḥ eva jagannāthaḥ bandha mokṣa karaḥ śivaḥ bhūḥ bhūvaḥ svar mahaḥ ca eva janaḥ sākṣāt tapaḥ svayam satya lokaḥ tathā aṇḍānām koṭi koṭi śatāni ca vigraham devadevasya tathā aṇḍa āvaraṇa aṣṭakam tathā anyeṣu ca lokeṣu vasaṃti ca cara acarāḥ sarve bhava aṃśa jāḥ nūnam gatiḥ tu eṣām saḥ eva vai viśvam bhūtam tathā jātam bahudhā rudraḥ eva saḥ rudra ājñā eṣā sthitā devī hi anayā muktiḥ aṃbikā iti evam khecarāḥ siddhāḥ jajalpuḥ prīta mānasāḥ yadā avalokya tān sarvān prasādāt anayā aṃbikā tadā tiṣṭhanti sāyujyam prāptāḥ te khecarāḥ prabhoḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca ṣaḍviṃśat śakti saṃyuktam aṣṭadhā ca dvijottamāḥ tatas ṣoḍaśadhā ca eva punar dvādaśadhā dvijāḥ aṣṭa śakti samāyuktam aṣṭamūrtim ajam prabhum tābhiḥ ca aṣṭavidhāḥ rudrāḥ catuḥṣaṣṭividhāḥ punar śaktayaḥ ca tathā sarvāḥ guṇa aṣṭaka samanvitāḥ evam pāśupatam yogam mokṣa siddhi pradāyakam tasya aṇina ādayaḥ viprāḥ na anyathā karma koṭibhiḥ prākāmyam ca eva sarvatra īśitvam ca eva sarvatas tat ca api trividham jñeyam aiśvaryam sārvakāmikam punar aṣṭaguṇaḥ ca api sūkṣmeṣu eva vidhīyate prākāmān viṣayān bhuṅkte tathā apratihataḥ kvacid aiśvaryam ca apratīghātam prāpya yogam anuttamam evam pāśupatam yogam jñātavyam muni puṅgavāḥ aliṅginam nirguṇam cetanam ca nityam sadā sarva gam sarva sāram paśyanti yuktyā hi acala prakāśam tad bhāvitāḥ tejasā dīpyamānam apāṇi pāda udara pārśva jihvaḥ hi atīndriyaḥ vā api su sūkṣmaḥ ekaḥ paśyati acakṣuḥ sa śṛṇoti akarṇaḥ na ca asti abuddham na ca buddhiḥ āsti ace tanām sarva gatām sūkṣmām prasava dharmiṇīm strī puṃsoḥ saṃprayoge hi jāyate hi tatas prabhuḥ kālena kalalam ca api budvudam saṃprajāyate evam ādhyātmikaiḥ yuktāḥ vāyunā saṃprapūritaḥ yāvat hi vaiṣṇavaḥ vāyuḥ jāta mātram na saṃspṛśet tāvatkālam mahādevam arcayāmi iti cintayet tāḍanam bhakṣaṇam ca eva pūya śoṇita bhakṣaṇam yathā hi āpaḥ tu saṃchinnāḥ saṃśleṣmam upayānti vai anādimān prabandhaḥ syāt pūrva karmaṇi dehinaḥ sarīsṛpa tvāt gacchet vai sthāvara tvam na saṃśayaḥ vivṛtyāḥ hi eṣa saṃbheda adbhutānām ca eva śāśvataḥ tathā antar saṃsthitam devam sva śaktyā parimaṇḍitam aṣṭadhā ca aṣṭadhā ca eva tathā ca aṣṭavidhena ca sṛṣṭi artham saṃsthitam vahnim saṃkṣipya ca hṛdi sthitam dhyātvā yathāvat deveśam rudram bhuvana nāyakam prāṇe niviṣṭaḥ vai rudraḥ tasmāt prāṇa mayaḥ svayam śiva āviśa iha mām īśa svāhā brahma ātmane svayam evam pañca āhutīḥ ca eva prabhuḥ prīṇātu śāśvataḥ savasya jagataḥ ca eva prabhuḥ prīṇātu śāśvataḥ yoga ācāraḥ svayam tena brahmaṇā kathitaḥ purā sūtaḥ uvāca saṃkṣepāt sarva veda artham saṃcayam brahma vādinām matsya gṛhyasya yat pāpam ṣaṣ māsa abhyantare bhavet eka aham tad samam jñeyam a pūtam yat jalam bhavet aghora lakṣaṇam mantram tatas śuddhim avāpnuyāt athavā pūjayet śaṃbhum ghṛta srāna ādi vistaraiḥ tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya śuddhyate na atra saṃśayaḥ dadhi bhakṣāḥ payaḥ bhakṣāḥ ye ca anye jīva kṣīṇakāḥ bhasma śāyī bhavet nityam bhikṣā cārī jita indriyaḥ yaḥ icchet paramam sthānam vratam pāśupatam caret samāpnuyāt yogam imam mahātmā mahā ṛṣayaḥ ca evam anindita amalāḥ sarva loka upakāra artham śṛṇudhvam pravadāmi vaḥ guru upadeśa yuktānām vṛddhānām krama varttinām aṣṭāṅga praṇipātena tridhā nyastena suvratāḥ jyeṣṭha anye api ca te sarve vandanīyāḥ vijānatā ājñā bhaṅgam na kurvīta yadi icchat siddhim uttamām viṣa graha viḍaṃba ādīn varjayet sarva yatnataḥ kaitavam vitta śāṭhyam ca paiśunyam varjayet sadā ati hāsam avaṣṭaṃbham līlā sva icchā pravartanam yatīnām āsanam vastram daṇḍa ādyam pāduke tathā mālyam ca śayana sthānam pātram chāyām ca yatnataḥ yajña upakaraṇa aṅgam ca na spṛśet vai padena ca kṛtvā pramādataḥ viprāḥ praṇavasya ayutam japet saṃdhyā lope kṛte vipraḥ tris āvṛttya eva śuddhi ati kāka ulūka kapotānām pakṣiṇām api ghātane na evam ātma vidām asti prāyaścittāni codanā yoga dhyāna eka niṣṭhāḥ ca nirlepāḥ kāñcanam yathā śuddhānām śodhanam na asti viśuddhāḥ brahma vidyayā paṅka aśma dūṣitam ca eva sāmudram palvala udakam sa śaivālam tathā anyaiḥ vā doṣaiḥ duṣṭam vivarjayet vastra śauca anvitaḥ kuryāt sarva kāryāṇi vai dvijāḥ namaskāra ādikam sarvam guru śuśrūṣaṇa ādikam itareṣām hi vastrāṇām śaucam kāryam mala āgame carmaṇām vidalānām ca vetrāṇām vastra vat matam valkalānām tu sarveṣām chatra cāmarayoḥ api bhasmanā kāṃsyam kṣāreṇa āyasam ucyate haimam adbhiḥ śubham pātram raupya pātram dvijottamāḥ tṛṇa kāṣṭha ādi vastūnām śubhena abhyukṣaṇam smṛtam uṣṇena vāriṇā śuddhiḥ tathā sruc sruvayoḥ api saṃhatānām mahābhāgāḥ dravyāṇām prokṣaṇam smṛtam asaṃhatānām dravyāṇam pratyekam śaucam ucyate go nivāsena vai śuddhā secanena dharā smṛtā svādārāsyam gṛhasthānām ratau bhāryā abhikāṅkṣayā hastābhyām kṣālitam vastram kāruṇā ca yathāvidhi kuśa aṃbunā su saṃprokṣya gṛhṇīyāt dharma vittamaḥ śuciḥ ākara jam teṣām śvā mṛga grahaṇe śuciḥ chāyā ca vipluṣaḥ viprāḥ makṣikā ādyāḥ dvijottamāḥ suptvā bhuktvā ca vai viprāḥ kṣuttvā pītvā ca vai tathā kṛtvā ca maithunam spṛṣṭvā patitam kukkuṭa ādikam sūkaram ca eva kāka ādi śvānam uṣṭram kharam tathā yūpam cāṇḍālaka ādyān ca spṛṣṭvā snānena śudhyati rajasvalām sūtikām ca na spṛśet antyajām api sūtikā śauca saṃyuktaḥ śāva aśauca samanvitaḥ saṃspṛśet na rajaḥ tāsām spṛṣṭvā snātvā eva śudhyati na eva aśaucam yatīnām ca vanastha brahmacāriṇām naiṣṭhikānām nṛpāṇām ca maṇḍalīnām ca suvratāḥ tatas kārya virodhāt hi nṛpāṇām na anyathā bhavet vaikhānasānām viprāṇām patitānām asaṃbhavāt a saṃcaya dvijānām ca snāna mātreṇa na anyathā tathā saṃnihitānām ca yajña artham dīkṣitasya ca ekāha ādi ajña yājīnām śuddhiḥ uktā svayaṃbhuvā tatas tu adhīta śākhānām caturbhiḥ sarva dehinām avārk ekādaśa aha antam bāndhavānām dvijottamāḥ tatas ṛtu trayāt arvāk eka ahaḥ parigīyate sapta varṣāt tatas ca arvāk tri rātram hi tatas param daśa aham brāhmaṇānām vai prathame ahani vā pituḥ daśa aham sūtikā aśaucam mātuḥ api evam avyayāḥ arvāk tri varṣāt snānena bāndhavānām pituḥ sadā aṣṭa abdāt eka rātreṇa śuddhiḥ syāt brāndhavasya tu dvādaśa abdāt tatas ca arvāk tri rātram strīṣu suvratāḥ daśa ahe tu tri rātram aśuciḥ bhavet tatas saṃnihitaḥ vipraḥ ca arvāk pūrvam tat eva vai spṛṣṭvā pretan tri rātreṇa dharma artham snānam ucyate dāhakānām ca netṝṇām snāna mātram abāndhave ācārya maraṇe ca eva tri rātram śrotriye mṛte pakṣiṇī mātulānām ca sodarāṇām ca vā dvijāḥ bhūpānām maṇḍalīnām ca sadyas nīrāṣṭra vāsinām na abhiṣiktasya ca aśaucam saṃpramādeṣu vai raṇe aśaucam ca ānupūrvyeṇa yatīnām na eva vidyate tretā prabhṛti nārīṇām māsi māsi ārtavam dvijāḥ prayānti ca mahābhāgāḥ bhāryābhiḥ kuravaḥ yathā bhārate dakṣiṇe varṣe vyavasthā na itareṣu atha mahāvīte suvīte ca jaṃbūdvīpe tathā aṣṭasu śākadvīpa ādiṣu proktaḥ dharmaḥ vai bhārate yathā rasollāsā kṛte vṛttiḥ tretāyām gṛha vṛkṣa jā sā eva ārtava kṛtāt doṣāt rāga dveṣa ādibhiḥ nṛṇām oṣadhyaḥ ca rajaḥ doṣāḥ strīṇām rāga ādibhiḥ nṛṇām akāla kṛṣṭāḥ vidhvastāḥ punar utpāditāḥ tathā prathame ahani cāṇḍālī yathā avajyā tathā aṅganā snātvā ardha māsāt saṃśuddhā tatas śuddhiḥ bhaviṣyati ā ṣoḍaśāt tatas strīṇām mūtra vat śaucam iṣyate pañca rātram tathā aspṛśyā rajasā vartate yadi sā viṃśat divasāt ūrdhvam rajasā pūrvavat tathā snāne śaucam tathā gānam rodanam hasanam tathā yānam abhyañjanam nārī dyūtam ca eva anulepanam divāsvapnam viśeṣeṇa tathā vai dantadhāvanam maithunam mānasam vā api vācikam devatā arcanam rajasvalā aṅganā sparśa saṃbhāṣe ca rajasvalā saṃtyāgam ca eva vastrāṇām varjayet sarva yatnataḥ snātvā anya puruṣam nārī na spṛśet tu rajasvalā īkṣayet bhāskaram devam brahmakūrcam tatas vibet kevalam pañcagavyam vā kṣīram vā ca ātma śuddhaye caturthyām strī na gamyā tu gata ulpa āyuḥ prasūyate vidyā hīnam vrata bhraṣṭam patitam pāradārikam dāridrya arṇava magnam ca tanayam sā prasūyate rakta ādhikyāt bhavet nārī śukra ādhikye bhavet pumān ṣaṣṭhyām gamyā mahābhāgā sat putra jananī bhavet puṃsaḥ trāṇa anvitam putram tathābhūtam prasūyate aṣṭamyām sarva saṃpannam tanayam saṃprasūyate navamyām dārikā daśamyām paṇḍitā bhavet ekādaśyām tathā nārīm janayet sā eva pūrvavat dvādaśyām dharma tattva jñam śrauta smārta pravartakam trayodaśyām jaḍām nārīm sarva saṃkara kāriṇīm caturdaśyām yadā gaccet sā putra jananī bhavet pañcadaśyām ca dharmiṣṭhām ṣoḍaśyām jñāna pāragam strīṇām vai mathune kāle vāma pārśve prabhañjanaḥ caret yadi bhavet nārī pumāṃsam dakṣiṇe labhet strīṇām maithuna kāle tu pāpa graha vivarjite ukta kāle śuciḥ bhūtvā śuddhām gacchet śuci smitām iti evam saṃprasaṃgena yatīnām dharma saṃgrahe śrāvayet vā yathānyāyam brāhmaṇān dagdha kilbiṣān sūtaḥ uvāca satatam hi divā rātrau yena idam veṣṭyate jagat punar āśramam āgatya caret bhikṣuḥ atandritaḥ vyatikramāḥ ca ye kecid vāc manaḥ kāya saṃbhavāḥ sūtaḥ uvāca paśyet pratorpa śācān ca nava māsān sa jīvati dhūmam vā mastakāt paśyet daśa ahān na sa jīvati chidram vā svasya kaṇṭhasya svapne yaḥ vīkṣate naraḥ gaṇḍā iva piṇḍikā rakte tasya mṛtyuḥ upasthitaḥ kāmam vitarkam prītim ca sukha duḥkhe ubhe tathā kālakarmāṇi vijñāya samūheṣu eva nityaśas tatra ca eṣā tu yā mātrā plutā nāma upadiśyate ātmānam vidyate yaḥ tu sa sarvam vindate dvijāḥ tasmāt pāśupataiḥ yogaiḥ ātmānam cintayet budhaḥ ātmānam jānate ye tu śucayaḥ te na saṃśayaḥ avimukteśvaram gatvā vārāṇasyām tu śodhanam śrīparvate vā vipra indrāḥ saṃtyajet sva tanum naraḥ avimuktam param kṣetram jantūnām mukti dam sadā seveta satatam dhīmān viśeṣāt maraṇa antike ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ evam vārāṇasī puṇyā yadi sūta mahāmate sūtaḥ uvāca vakṣye saṃkṣepataḥ samyak vārāṇasyāḥ su śobhanam avimukteśvaram liṅgam vāsam tatra cakāra saḥ vārāṇasī kurukṣetra śrīparvata mahālaye uktavān parameśānaḥ pārvatyāḥ prīyate bhavaḥ kvacid ca kekā ruta nāditam śubham kvacid ca kāraṇḍava nāda nāditam kvacid ca matta ali kula ākulīkṛtam mada ākulābhiḥ bhramara aṅganā ādi bhīḥ niṣevitam cāru su gāndhi puṣpakaiḥ kvacid su paṣpaiḥ sahakāra vṛkṣaiḥ latā upagūḍhaiḥ tilakaiḥ ca gūḍham pragīta vidyādhara siddha cāraṇam pravṛtta nṛtta anugata apsaraḥ gaṇam prahṛṣṭa nānāvidha pakṣi sevitam pranṛtta hārīta kula upanāditam mṛgendra nāda ākula matta mānasaiḥ puṣpaiḥ vanyaiḥ śubha śubhatamaiḥ kalpitaiḥ divya bhūṣaiḥ devīm divyām upavana gatām bhūṣayāmāsa śarvaḥ sā ca api enam tuhināgiri sutā śaṅkaram devadevam puṣpaiḥ divyaiḥ śubhataratamaiḥ bhūṣayāmāsa bhaktyā saṃpūjya pūjyam saṃprekṣya ca udyāna ramyam gaṇa īśvaraiḥ nādi mukhaiḥ ca sārdham uvāca devam praṇipatya devī śrī devī uvāca sūtaḥ uvāca āghrāya vadana aṃbhojam tadā āha girijām hasan śrī bhagavān uvāca kāmam hi atra mṛtaḥ devī jantuḥ mokṣāya kalpate atas param idam kṣetram parā ca iyam gatiḥ mama kāmam bhuñjan svapan krīḍan kurvan hi vividhāḥ kriyāḥ goprekṣakam idam kṣetram brahmaṇā sthāpitam purā kailāsa bhavanam ca atra paśya divyam varānane kapila ahraham iti evam tathā vai brahmaṇā kṛtam svarlīneśvaraḥ iti evam atra aham svayam āgataḥ devaiḥ saṃmatāt etāni liṅgāni stāpitāni atas mṛtaḥ ca na punar jantuḥ saṃsārī tu bhavet naraḥ kedāram madhyamam kṣetram sthānam ca eva mahālayam sūtaḥ uvāca pañcākṣarasya vai bījam saṃsmarantaḥ su śobhanam śrī bhagavān uvāca yaiḥ yaiḥ yogāḥ iha abhyastāḥ steṣām ekena janmanā tasmāt etat mahat kṣetram brahma ādyaiḥ sevitam tathā puṇya sthāna sthitām puṇyām puṇya diś pravahām sadā kṣetrāṇi sarvatas devī devatāḥ ṛṣayaḥ tathā kālabhairavam āsādya dhūta pāpāni sarvaśas amareśam mahākālam jyotiṣam bhasmagātrakam aṣṭaṣaṣṭi samākhyāni ruḍhāni anyāni kṛtsnaśas su dṛṣṭam kuru deveśi avimuktam gṛham mama darśayāmāsa bhagavān śrīparvatam anuttamam śrīparvatam anuprāpya devyāḥ deveśvaraḥ haraḥ kṣetrāṇi darśayāmāsa sarva bhūta patiḥ bhavaḥ kuṇḍiprabhe ca paramam divyam vaiśravaṇeśvaram āśāliṅgam ca deveśam divyam yat ca bileśvaram rāmeśvaram ca paramam viṣṇunā yat pratiṣṭhitam dakṣiṇa dvāra pārśve tu kuṇḍaleśvaram īśvaram vivṛddham giriṇā sārdham devadeva namaskṛtam karmeśvaram ca vipulam kārya artham brahmaṇā kṛtam śrīmat siddhavaṭam ca eva sadā āvāsaḥ mama avyaye ajena nirmitam divyam sākṣāt ajabilam śubham tatra śṛṅgāṭaka ākāram śṛṅgāṭa acala madhyame śṛṅgāṭakeśvaram nāma śrī devyā tu pratiṣṭhitam rajeśvaram ca paryāye rajasā su pratiṣṭhitam gajeśvaram ca vaiśākham kapoteśvaram avyayam koṭīśvaram mahā tīrtham rudra koṭi gaṇaiḥ purā dvidevakula saṃjñam ca brahmaṇā dakṣiṇe śubham uttare sthāpitam ca eva viṣṇunā ca eva śailajam mahā pramāṇa liṅgam ca mayā pūrvam pratiṣṭhitam iti uktvā tad gṛhe tiṣṭhat alaṅgṛham iti smṛtam tatra api tīrtham tīrtha jñe vyomaliṅgam ca paśya me gomaṇḍaleśvaram ca eva nanda ādyaiḥ su pratiṣṭhitam devaiḥ sarvaiḥ tu śakra ādyaiḥ stāpitāni varānane śrīmat devahrada prānte sthānāni imāni pasya me tvayā hitāya jagatām hārakuṇḍam idam kṛtam alaṅkṛtam mayā brahma purastāt munibhaḥ siha caṇḍikeśvarakam devi caṇḍikeśā tava ātmajā caṇḍikā nirmitam sthānam aṃbikātīrtham uttamam rucikeśvarakam ca eva dhārā eṣā kapilā śubhā eteṣu devi sthāneṣu tīrtheṣu vividheṣu ca pūjayet mām sadā bhaktyā mayā sārdham hi modate śrīśaile saṃtyajet daṃham brāhmaṇaḥ dagdha kilbiṣaḥ mahāsnānam ca yaḥ kuryāt ghṛtena vidhinā eva tu sa yāti mama sāyujyam sthāneṣu eteṣu suvrate snāpya liṅgam madīyam tu gavyena eva ghṛtena ca saṃmārjya śata yajñānām snānena prayutam tathā mahāsnāne prasaktam tu snānam aṣṭaguṇam smṛtam śami puṣpam ca vidhinā bilva patram ca paṅkajam anyāni api ca puṣpāṇi bilva patram na saṃtyajet catur droṇaiḥ mahādevam aṣṭa droṇaiḥ atha api vā daśa droṇaiḥ tu naivedyam aṣṭa droṇaiḥ atha api vā śata droṇa samam puṇyam āḍhake pi vidhīyate sa bhṛtya putra dāraiḥ ca tathā saṃbandhi bāndhavaiḥ sārdham pradakṣiṇam kṛtvā pārthayet liṅgam uttamam kṛtam vā na kṛtam vā api kṣantum arhasi śaṅkara japitvā evam mahābījam tathā pañcākṣarasya vai sūtaḥ uvāca niśamya vacanam devī gatvā vārāṇasīm purīm tatra andhakam mahā daityam hiraṇyākṣa sutam prabhuḥ anugṛhya gaṇa tvam ca prāpayāmāsa līlayā śrāvayet vā dvijān sarvān kṛta śaucān jita indriyān ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ damitaḥ tu katham lebhe gāṇapatyam maheśvarāt sūtaḥ uvāca andaka anugraham ca eva mandare śoṣaṇam tathā vara lābham aśeṣam ca pravadāmi samāsatas prasādāt brahmaṇaḥ sākṣāt avadhya tvam avāpya ca līlayā ca aprayatnena trāsayāmāsa vāsavan bādhitāḥ tāḍitāḥ baddhāḥ pātitāḥ tena te surāḥ viviśuḥ mandaram bhītāḥ nārāyaṇa purogamāḥ evam saṃpīḍya vai devān andhakaḥ upi mahā asuraḥ tatas te samāḥ tāḥ suredrāḥ sa sādhyāḥ sureśa maheśam purā iti āhuḥ evam drutam ca alpa vīrya prabhinna aṅga bhinnāḥ vayam daitya rājasya śastraiḥ nikṛttāḥ jaya iti vācā bhagavan damūcuḥ kirīṭa baddha añjalayaḥ samantāt atha aśeṣa asurān tasya koṭi koṭi sataiḥ tatas bhasmīkṛtya mahādevaḥ nirbibheda andhakam tadā śūlena śūlinā protam dagdha kalmaṣa kañcukam tad nāda śravaṇāt neduḥ devāḥ devam praṇamya tam nanṛtuḥ munayaḥ sarve mumuduḥ gaṇa puṅgavāḥ dagdha ugninā ca śūlena protaḥ pretaḥ iva andhakaḥ ārādhitaḥ mayā śaṃbhuḥ purā sākṣāt maheśvaraḥ yaḥ smaret manasā rudram prāṇānte sakṛt eva vā śaraṇam prāpya tiṣṭhanti tam eva śaraṇam vrajet evam saṃcintya tuṣṭa ātmā saḥ uṃdhakaḥ ca andhaka ardanam sa gaṇam śivam īśānam astuvat puṇya gauravāt hiraṇyanetra tanayam śūla agra stham sureśvaraḥ provāca dānavam prekṣya ghṛṇayā nīlalohitaḥ śrutvā vākyam tadā śaṃbhoḥ hiraṇyanayana ātmajaḥ pradadau durlabhām śraddhām daitya indrāya mahā dyutiḥ praṇemuḥ tam suredra ādyāḥ gām apatye pratiṣṭhitam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ viṣṇunā sūditaḥ viṣṇuḥ vārāha tvam katham gataḥ tasya śṛṅgam maheśasya bhūṣaṇa tvam katham gatam sūtaḥ uvāca purā andhaka asura īśasya pitā kāla antaka upamaḥ bādhitāḥ tāḍitāḥ baddhva hiraṇyākṣeṇa tena vai sarve vijñāpayāmāsuḥ dharaṇī bandhanam hareḥ śrutvā etat bhagavān viṣṇuḥ dharaṇī bandhanam hariḥ bhūtvā yajñavarāhā usau yathā liṅga udbhave tathā daṃṣṭra agra koṭyā hatvā enam reje daitya anta kṛt prabhuḥ kalpa ādiṣu yathāpūrvam praviśya ca rasātalam ānīya vasudhām devīm aṅka sthām akarot bahis śāśvatāya varāhāya daṃṣṭriṇe daṇḍine namaḥ kartre netre suredrāṇām śāstre ca sakalasya ca tavyā kṛtam sarvam idam prasīda sureśa lokeśa varāha viṣṇo tathā eka daṃṣṭra agra mukha agra koṭi bhāga eka bhāga ardhatamena viṣṇo hatāḥ kṣaṇāt kāma da daitya mukhyāḥ sva daṃṣṭra koṭyā saha putra bhṛtyaiḥ tvayā uddhṛtā deva dharā dharā īśa dharādhara ākāra dhṛtā agra daṃṣṭre tvayā eva deveśa vibho kṛtaḥ ca jayaḥ surāṇām asura īśvaṇām aho vasuṃdharā tava pṛṣṭhatas sakala tārakā ādayaḥ nihitā rasātala gatā vasuṃdharā tava pṛṣṭhatas sakala tārakā ādayaḥ jagatām hitāya bhavatā vasuṃdharā bhagavān rasa atasa puṭaṅga tā tadā atha tām uddhṛtām tena dharām devāḥ muni īśvarāḥ mūrdhni āropya namaścakruḥ cakriṇaḥ saṃnidhau tadā kṛṣṇena akliṣṭa kāryeṇa śata hastena viṣṇunā lokānām dhāriṇī tvam hi mṛttike hara pātakam tvayā hatena pāpena jīvāmaḥ tvad prasādataḥ varāha daṃṣṭra bhinnāyām dharāyām mṛttikām dvijāḥ vārāha rūpam anagham cacāla ca dharā punar yadṛcchayā bhavaḥ paśyan jagāma jagat īśvaraḥ daṃṣṭrām jagrāha dṛṣṭvā tām bhūṣaṇa artham atha ātmanaḥ devāḥ ca tuṣṭuvuḥ sa iṃdrāḥ devadevasya vaibhavam dharā pratiṣṭhitā hi evam devadevena līlayā bhūtānām saṃplave ca api viṣṇoḥ ca eva kalevaram brahmaṇaḥ ca tathā anyeṣām devānām api līlayā katham vimuktiḥ viprāṇām tasmāt daṃṣṭrī maheśvaraḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ nṛsiṃhena hataḥ pūrvam hiraṇyākṣa agrajaḥ śrutam sūtaḥ uvāca dharma jñaḥ satya saṃpannaḥ tapasvī ca abhavat sudhīḥ janma prabhṛti deveśam pūjayāmāsa ca avyayam brahmaṇā adhipatim sṛṣṭi sthiti saṃhāra kāraṇam saḥ upi viṣṇoḥ tathābhūtam dṛṣṭvā putram samāhitam stuvantam prāha deva arīḥ pradahan iva pāpa dhīḥ prahrāda vīra duṣputra dvija deva ārti kāraṇam kaḥ viṣṇuḥ padmajaḥ vā api śakraḥ ca vāyuḥ somaḥ tathā īśānaḥ pāvakaḥ mama yaḥ samaḥ mām eva arcaya bhaktyā ca svalpam nārāyaṇam sadā prahrāda jīvite vāñchā tava eṣā śṛṇu ca asti ced śrutvā api tasya vacanam hiraṇyakaśipoḥ sudhīḥ prahrādaḥ pūjayāmāsa namaḥ nārāyaṇa iti ca adhyāpayāmāsa ca tām brahma vidyām su śobhanām durlaṅghyām ca ātmanaḥ dṛṣṭvā śakra ādibhiḥ api svayam putreṇa laṅghitām ājñām hiraṇyaḥ prāha dānavān kṣīra vārinidhi śāyinaḥ prabhoḥ niṣphalam tu atha babhūva tejasā tatra eva āvirabhūt hantum nṛsiṃha ākṛtim āsthitaḥ jaghāna ca sutam prekṣya pitaram dānava adhamam bibheda tad kṣaṇāt eva karajauḥ niśitaiḥ śataiḥ pīḍayāmāsa daitya indram yuga anta agniḥ iva aparaḥ ā brahma bhuvanāt viprāḥ pracacāla ca suvratāḥ dṛṣṭvā sura asura mahā uraga siddha sādhyāḥ tasmin kṣaṇe hari viriñci mukhāḥ nṛsiṃham dhairyam balam ca samavāpya yayuḥ visṛjya ādiṅmukha antam asura akṣaṇa tatparāḥ ca tatas taiḥ gataiḥ sa eṣa devaḥ nṛsiṃhaḥ sahasra ākṛtiḥ sarva pād sarva bāhuḥ sahasra īkṣaṇaḥ soma sūrya agni netraḥ tadā saṃsthitaḥ sarvam āvṛtya māyī tam tuṣṭuvuḥ sura śreṣṭhāḥ lokāḥ loka acale stitāḥ sa brahmakāḥ sa sādhyāḥ ca sa yamāḥ sa marūt gaṇāḥ jyotiṣām tu paran jyotiḥ paramātmā jagat mayaḥ sthūlam sūkṣmam su sūkṣmam ca śabdabrahma mayaḥ śubhaḥ yajñabhuj yajñamūrtiḥ tvam yajñinām phala daḥ prabhuḥ bhavān matsya ākṛtiḥ kaurmam āsthāya jagati sthitaḥ vārāhim ca eva tām saiṃhīm āsthāya iha vyavasthitaḥ devānām deva rakṣā artham nihatya ditija īśvaram dvija śāpa chalena evam avatīrṇaḥ asi līlayā na dṛṣṭam yat tvad anyam hi bhavān sarvan carācaram bhavān viṣṇuḥ bhavān rudraḥ bhavān eva pitāmahaḥ māyayā bahudhā saṃstham advitīya mayam prabho stoṣyāmaḥ tvām katham bhāsi devadeva mṛgādhipa na jagāma dvijāḥ śāntim mānayan yonim ātmanaḥ yaḥ nṛsiṃha stavam bhaktyā paṭhet vā artham vicārayet tad antare śivam devāḥ sa iṃdrāḥ sa brahmakāḥ prabhum saṃprāpya tuṣṭavuḥ sarvam vijñāpya mṛga rūpiṇaḥ ātma trāṇāya śaraṇam jagmuḥ parama kāraṇam mandara stham mahādevam krīḍamānam saha umayā namaḥ śivāya śarvāya śaṅkarāya ārtti hariṇe kālāya kāla pūpāya namaḥ kāla aṅga hāriṇe śaṃbhave haimavatyāḥ ca manyave rudra rūpiṇe aghora rūpāya vikaṭāya vikaṭa śarīrāya te nāmaḥ puruṣa rūpāya puruṣa eka tatpuruṣāya vai namaḥ sarva viṣṇuḥ nṛsiṃhasya rūpam āsthāya viśva kṛt daitya indraiḥ bahubhiḥ sārdham hita artham jagatām prabhuḥ saiṃhīm samānayan yonim bādhate nikhilam jagat kālakūṭa ādi vapuṣā trāhi naḥ śaraṇa āgatān śukram tu vṛttam viśveśa krīḍāḥ vai kevalam vayam saṃtaptāsmaḥ u ayam deva hariṇā amita tejasā sūtaḥ uvāca vijñāpitaḥ tathā devaḥ prahasan prāha tān surān abhayam ca dadau teṣām haniṣyām iti tam prabhuḥ saḥ upi śakraḥ suraiḥ sārdham praṇipatya yathāgatam jagāma bhagavān brahmā tathā anye ca sura uttamāḥ yayau prānte nṛsiṃhasya garvitasya mṛga āśinaḥ apahṛtya tadā prāṇān śarabhaḥ sura pūjitaḥ siṃhāt tatas naraḥ bhūtvā jagāma ca yathākramam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ kiṅkim dhairyam kṛtam tena brūhi sarvam aseṣataḥ sūtaḥ uvāca vīrabhadraḥ upi bhagavān vīra śakti vijṛṃbhitaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca śrī vīrabhadraḥ uvāca jantu cakram bhagavatā rakṣitam matsya rūpiṇā sūtaḥ uvāca śrī nṛsiṃhaḥ uvāca rajasā adhiṣṭhitaḥ sraṣṭā rudraḥ tāmasaḥ ucyate sūtaḥ uvāca śrī vīrabhadraḥ uvāca sṛṣṭi arthena jagat pūrvam śaṅkaram nīlalohitam evam rakṣaḥ vidārya eva tvam śakti kalayā yutaḥ chinnam tad aṃśam tasya tad valam tvad ādiḥ taṃvaṣarī antam rudra śakti vijṛṃbhitam sūtaḥ uvāca na tāt hiraṇmayam saumyam na sauram na agni saṃbhavam śrī nṛsiṃhaḥ uvāca kāla kālāya kālāya mahākālāya mṛtyave ekasmai nīlakaṇṭhāya śrīkaṇṭhāya pinākine namaḥ viṣṇu kalatrāya viṣṇu kṣetrāya bhānave kaivartāya kirātāya mahā vyādhāya śāśvate namaḥ nṛsiṃha saṃhartre kāma kāla purāraye mahā pāśa ogha saṃhartre viṣṇu māyā anta kāriṇe tryaṃbakāya tri akṣarāya śipiviṣṭāya mīḍhuṣe mṛtyuñjayāya śarvāya sarvajñāya makha araye makha īśāya vareṇyāya namaḥ te vahni rūpiṇe mahā ghrāṇāya jihvāya prāṇa apāna pravarttine triguṇāya triśūlāya guṇa atītāya yogine saṃsārāya pravāhāya mahā yantra pravartine amoghāya agni netrāya lakulīśāya śaṃbhave bhiṣaktamāya muṇḍāya daṇḍine yoga rūpiṇe meghavāhāya devāya pārvatīpataye namaḥ sthāṇave kṛtti vāsāya namaḥ pañcārtha hetave sūtaḥ uvāca devāḥ ūcuḥ atha brahma ādayaḥ sarve varībhadra tvayā dṛśā jīvitāḥ smaḥ vayam devāḥ parjanyena iva pādapāḥ vātaḥ vāti ca sosi tvam mṛtyuḥ dhāvati pañcamaḥ yat avyaktam param vyoma kalā atītam sadāśivam na viddhi paramam dhāma rūpa lāvaṇya varṇane upasargeṣu sarveṣu trāyasva asmān gaṇādhipa ekādasa ātman bhagavān vartate rūpavān haraḥ īdṛśān te avatārāṇi dṛṣṭvā śiva bahūn tamaḥ kadācid saṃdihet na asmān tvad cintā astamayā tathā guñja giri vara taṭa amita rūpāṇi sarvaśas gamyam te na nītavyam parāparā ghorā api anyā śivā api anyā te pratyekam anekadhā iha asmān pāhi bhagavan iti ahata mahā balaḥ atas smān pāhi bhagavan surā dānaiḥ abhīpsitaiḥ jagat saṃhāra kāreṇa pravṛttaḥ narakesarī tatas duḥsvapna śamanam sarva bhūta nivāraṇam śeṣa lokasya sopānam vāñchita artha eka sādhanam vāñcha siddhi pradam ca eva ṛddhi prajñā ādi sādhanam bhakteṣu ca ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca āsīt antaka saṃkāśaḥ tapasā labdha vikramaḥ jitvā eva deva saṃghātam brahmāṇam vai jalandharaḥ jagāma deva devaśam viṣṇum viśva haram gurum provāca idam diteḥ putrān nyāya dhīḥ jetum īśvaram tam jitvā sarvam īśānam gaṇapaiḥ nadinā kṣaṇāt vāsava tvam ca yuṣmākam dāsye dānava puṅgavāḥ jagarjuḥ uccais pāpiṣṭhāḥ mṛtyu darśana tatparāḥ sa aṃbaḥ sa nandī sa gamaḥ provāca prahasan iva kim kṛtyam asura īśāna yuddhena anena sāṃpratam jalandharaḥ upi tat vākyam śrutvā śrotra vidāraṇam vākyena alam mahā bāho devadeva vṛṣadhvaja candra aṃśu sannibhaiḥ śastraiḥ hara yoddhum iha āgataḥ niśamya āsya vacaḥ śūlī pādāṅguṣṭhena līlayā kathañcid labdhavān śakraḥ śacīm ekām praṇamya mām mām na jānāsi daitya indram jalandharam umāpate sūtaḥ uvāca nāgāt vaiśasam anusaṃvṛtaḥ ca nāgaiḥ deveśam vacanam uvāca ca alpa buddhiḥ yat tasmāt bhayam iha nā asti vāñchā eṣā vipulatarā na durmadena avinīta ātmā dorbhyām āsphoṭya dos balāt durdhareṇa rathāṅgena kucchreṇa api dvijottamāḥ papāta daityaḥ balavān añjana adriḥ iva aparaḥ tasya raktena raudreṇa saṃpūrṇam abhavat kṣaṇāt siṃhanādam mahat kṛtvā sādhu deva iti ca bruvan ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sudarśana ākhyam vai labdham vaktum arhasi viṣṇunā sūtaḥ uvāca te devāḥ śakti muśalaiḥ sāyakaiḥ nata parvabhiḥ parājitāḥ tadā devāḥ devadeva īśvaram harim praṇemuḥ tam sureśānam śoka saṃvigna mānasāḥ tān samīkṣya atha bhagavān devadeva īśvaraḥ hariḥ vatsāḥ kim iti vai devāḥ cyuta alaṅkāra vikramāḥ samāgatāḥ sa saṃtāpāḥ vaktum arhatha suvratāḥ praṇamya āhuḥ yathāvṛttam devadevāya viṣṇave dānavaiḥ pīḍitāḥ sarve vayam śaraṇam āgatāḥ tvam eva bhartā hartā ca bhoktā dātā janārdana daityāḥ ca vaiṣṇavaiḥ brāhmaiḥ raudraiḥ yāmyaiḥ su dāruṇaiḥ kauberaiḥ ca eva saumyaiḥ ca nairṛtyaiḥ vāruṇaiḥ dṛḍhaiḥ sauraiḥ raudrauḥ tathā bhīmaiḥ kaṃpanaiḥ jūṃbhaṇaiḥ dṛḍhaiḥ sūrya maṇḍala saṃbhūtam tvadīyam cakram udyutam daṇḍam śārṅgam tava astram ca labdham daityaiḥ prasādataḥ purā jalandharam hantum nirmitam tripurāriṇā rathāṅgam su śitam ghoram tena tān hantum arhasi tatas niśamya teṣām vai vacanam vārīja īkṣaṇaḥ śri viṣṇuḥ uvāca saṃprāpya sāṃpratam sarvam kariṣyāmi divaukasām devāḥ jalandharam hantum nirmitam hi purāriṇā sarvān dhundhu mukhān daityān aṣṭaṣaṣṭi śatān surān sa bāndhavān kṣaṇāt eva yuṣmān saṃtārayāmi aham sūtaḥ uvāca evam uktā sura śreṣṭhān sura śreṣṭhān suraśreṣṭham anusmaran su śreṣṭhaḥ tadā śreṣṭham pūjayāmāsa śaṅkaram snāpya saṃpūjya gandha ādyaiḥ jvālā ākāram manoramam tuṣṭāva ca tadā rudram saṃpūjya agnau praṇamya ca pūjayāmāsa ca śivam praṇava ādyam namaḥ ntakam devam nāmnām sahasreṇa bhava ādyena maheśvaram tuṣṭāva ca punar śaṃbhum bhava ādyaiḥ bhavam īśvaram śrī viṣṇuḥ uvāca bhavaḥ śivaḥ haraḥ rudraḥ puruṣaḥ pada mlocanaḥ tamoharaḥ mahāyogī goptā brahma aṅga hṛt jaṭi unmattaveṣaḥ caśruṣyaḥ durvāsāḥ smaraśāsanaḥ caturmukhaḥ caturbāhuḥ durāvāsaḥ durāsadaḥ parama arthaḥ para mayaḥ śaṃbaraḥ vyāghrakaḥ analaḥ ātmayoniḥ anādyantaḥ ṣaḍviṃśat sapta lokadhṛk bhagaḥ vivasvān ādityaḥ yoga ācāryaḥ bṛhaspatiḥ hiraṇya varṇaḥ jyotiṣmān nānā bhūta dharaḥ dhvaniḥ bṛhajjyotiḥ sudhāmā ca mahājyotiḥ anuttamaḥ aṅgirāḥ muniḥ ātreyaḥ vimalaḥ viśva vāhanaḥ sahasra mūrdhā deva indrāḥ sarva śastra prabhañjanaḥ svavaśaḥ sa vaśaḥ svargaḥ svaraḥ svara maya svanaḥ daṃbhaḥ adaṃbhaḥ mahā daṃbhaḥ sarva bhūta maheśvaraḥ triloka ātmā triloka īśaḥ śuddhaḥ śuddhiḥ ratha akṣa jaḥ arthaḥ anarthaḥ mahā kośaḥ para kārya eka paṇḍitaḥ su prītaḥ su mukhaḥ sūkṣmaḥ su karaḥ dakṣiṇaḥ analaḥ varāha śṛṅga dhṛk vāyuḥ balavān eka nāyakaḥ śruti prakāśaḥ śrutimān eka bandhuḥ anekadhṛk vedyaḥ veda artha vid goptā sarva ācāraḥ muni īśvaraḥ sūtaḥ uvāca snāpayāmāsa ca vibhuḥ pūjayāmāsa paṅkajaiḥ parīkṣā artham hareḥ pūjā kamaleṣu maheśvaraḥ gopayāmāsa kamalam tadā ekam bhuvaneśvaraḥ pūjayāmāsa bhāvena nāmnā tena jagadgurum tatas tatra vibhuḥ dṛṣṭvā tathābhūtam haraḥ harim tasmāt avatatāra āśu maṇḍalāt pāvakasya ca koṭi bhāskara saṃkāśam jaṭā mukuṭa maṇḍitam jvālā mālā vṛtam divyam tīkṣṇan daṃṣṭram bhayaṅkaram itthaṃbhūtam tadā dṛṣṭvā bhavam bhasma vibhūṣitam hṛṣṭaḥ namaścakāra āśu devadevam janārdanaḥ sudarśana ākhyam cakram ca dadāmi tava śobhanam śāntam raṇa ajire viṣṇo devānām duḥkha sādhanam śāntasya ca astram śāntasya a chāntena astra ima kim phalam śāntasya samare ca astram śāntiḥ eva tapasvinam yoddhuḥ śāntyā bala chedaḥ parasya bala vṛddhi daḥ devaiḥ aśāntaiḥ yat rūpam madīyam bhāvaya avyayam kim āyudhena kāryam vai yoddhum deva ari sūdana kṣamā yudhi na kāryam vai yoddhum deva ari sūdana tadā prabhṛti tam prāhuḥ padmākṣam iti suvratam dattvā enam nayanam cakram viṣṇave nīlalohitaḥ pasparśa ca karābhyām vai su śubhābhyām uvāca ha vara doham vara śreṣṭha varān varaya ca īpsitān bhaktyā vaśīkṛtaḥ nūnam tvayā aham puruṣottama pasparśa ca dadau tasmai śraddhām śītāṃśubhūṣaṇaḥ mātaram pitaram dakṣam bhaviṣyati sureśvarī bhaginīm tava kalyāṇīm devīm haimavatīm umām mad saṃbandhī ca lokānām madhye pūjyaḥ bhaviṣyasi mām divyena ca bhāvena tadā prabhṛti śaṅkaram ayācata mahādevam munibhiḥ samam ghṛta ādyau snāpayet rudram stālyā vai kalaśaiḥ śubhaiḥ nāmnām sahasreṇa anena śraddhayā śivam īśvaram tasmāt nāmnām sahasreṇa dvijāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ saṃbhavaḥ sūcitaḥ devyāḥ tvayā sūta mahāmate menā ja tvam mahādevyāḥ dakṣa yajña vimardanam viṣṇunā ca katham dattā devadevāya śaṃbhave kalyāṇam vā katham tasya vaktum arhasi sāṃpratam saṃbhavam ca mahādevyāḥ prāha teṣām mahātmanām sūtaḥ uvāca yuṣmābhiḥ vai kumārāya tena vyāsāya dhīmate vacanāt vaḥ mahābhāgāḥ praṇamya umām tathā bhavam sā bhaga ākhyā jagaddhātrī liṅga mūrteḥ tri vedikā liṅgaḥ tu bhagavān dvābhyām jagat sṛṣṭiḥ dvijottamāḥ liṅga vedi samāyogāt ardhanārīśvaraḥ bhavat prāhiṇoti sma tasya eva jñānam jñāna mayaḥ haraḥ viśva adhikaḥ usau bhagavān ardhanārīśvaraḥ vibhuḥ hiraṇyagarbham tam devaḥ jāyamānam apaśyata tam dṛṣṭvā saṃsthitam devam ardhanārīśvaram prabhum vibhajasva iti viśveśam viśvātmānam ajaḥ vibhuḥ sasarja devīm vāma aṅgāt patnīm ca eva ātmanaḥ samām śraddhā hi asya śubhā patnī tatas puṃsaḥ surātanī sā eva ājñayā vibhoḥ devī dakṣa putrī babhūva ha satī saṃjñā tadā sā vai rudram eva āśritā patim dakṣam vinindya kālena devī hi abhūt punar nāradasya eva dakṣaḥ upi śāpāt evam vinindya ca an ādṛtya kṛtim jñātvā satī dakṣeṇa tad kṣaṇāt bhasmīkṛtvā ātmanaḥ deham yoga mārgeṇasā punar babhūva pārvatī devī tapasā ca gireḥ prabhoḥ jñātvā etat bhagavān bhargaḥ dadāha ruṣitaḥ prabhuḥ viṣṇunā lokapālan ca śaśāpa ca muni īśvaraḥ vināśaḥ vai kṣaṇāt eva māyayā śaṅkarasya vai ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ vijitya viṣṇunā sārdham bhagavān parameśvaraḥ sūtaḥ uvacā dakṣa yajñe su vipule devān viṣṇu purogamān dadāha bhagavān rudraḥ sarvān muni gaṇān api saḥ sṛjat vīrabhadraḥ ca gaṇeśān roma jān śubhān gaṇeśvaraiḥ samāruhya ratham bhadraḥ pratāpavān gaṇeśvarāḥ ca te sarve vividha āyudha pāṇayaḥ yajña vāṭaḥ tathā tasya gaṅgādvāra samīpatas tad deśe ca eva vikhyātam śubham kanakhalam dvijāḥ dagdhum vai preṣitaḥ ca asau bhagavān parameṣṭhinā tatas kṣaṇāt praviśya eva yajña vāṭam mahātmanaḥ roma jaiḥ sahitaḥ bhadraḥ kālāgniḥ iva ca aparaḥ saṃparkāt eva dakṣa ādya munīn devān pinākinā dagdhum saṃproṣitaḥ ca aham bhavantam sa muni īśvaraiḥ trayaḥ ca tri śatam teṣām tri sāhasram ca līlayā trayam ca eva suredrāṇām jaghāna ca muni īśvarān jaghāna bhagavān nudraḥ khaḍga muṣṭi ādi sāyakaiḥ tān sarvān api nihatya gadayā viṣṇum tāḍayāmāsa mūrdhani tribhiḥ ca dharṣitam śārṅgam tridhā bhūtam prabhoḥ tadā śārṅgaṅkoṭi prasaṃgāt vai ciccheda ca śiraḥ prabhoḥ vāyunā preritam ca eva prāṇa jena pinākinā bhadram āha mahā tejāḥ prārthayan praṇataḥ prabhuḥ śamam jagāma śanakais śāntaḥ tasthau tad ājñayā hatānām ca tadā teṣām pradadau pūrvavat tanum dakṣasya ca muni indrasya tathā anyeṣām maheśvaraḥ vāgīśyāḥ ca eva nāsā agram devamātuḥ tathā eva ca naṣṭānām jīvitam ca eva varāṇi vividhāni ca kalpayāmāsa vai vaktram līlayā ca mahān bhavaḥ dakṣaḥ upi labdha saṃjñaḥ ca samutthāya kṛtāñjaliḥ stutaḥ tena mahā tejāḥ pradāya vividhān varān nārāyaṇaḥ ca bhagavān tuṣṭāva ca kṛtāñjaliḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ devaḥ deveśam nīlakaṇṭham vṛṣadhvajam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ katham vā devadeveśam avāpa patim īśvaram sūtaḥ uvāca sā menā tanum āśritya sva icchayā eva vara aṅganā tadā haimavatī jajñe tapasā ca dvijottamāḥ dvādaśe ca tadā varṣe pūrṇe haimavatī śubhā tapaḥ tepe tayā sārdham anujā ca śubha ānanā ṛṣayaḥ ca tadā sarve sarva loka maheśvarīm tuṣṭuvuḥ tapasā devīm samāvṛtya samantataḥ jyeṣṭhā hi aparṇā hi anujā ca ekaparṇā śabhānanā tṛtīyā ca vārārohā tathā ca eva ekapāṭalā tapasā ca mahādevyāḥ parvātyāḥ parameśvaraḥ tārā ātmajaḥ mahā tejāḥ babhūva diti nandanaḥ tasya putrāḥ trayaḥ ca api tārakākṣaḥ mahā asuraḥ pitāmahaḥ tathā ca eṣām tāraḥ nāma mahā balaḥ tapasā labdha vīryaḥ ca prasādāt brahmaṇaḥ prabhoḥ vijitya samare pūrvam viṣṇum ca jitavān asau tāreṇa vijitaḥ saṃkhye dudrāva garuḍadhvajaḥ tāraḥ varān śataguṇam labdhvā śataguṇam balam deva indra pramukhān jitvā devān deva īśvara īśvaraḥ vārayāmāsa taiḥ devan sarva lokeṣu māyayā devatāḥ ca saha indreṇa tārakāt bhaya pīḍitāḥ na śāntim lebhire śūrāḥ śaraṇam vā bhaya arditāḥ uvāca aṅgirasam devaḥ devānām api saṃnidhau bhagavan tārakaḥ nāma tārajaḥ dānava uttamaḥ asamākam yāni amoghāni āyudhāni aṅgiraḥ vara tāni moghāni jāyante prabhāvāt amara dviṣaḥ viṣṇunā yodhitaḥ yuddhe tena api na ca sūditaḥ evam uktaḥ tu śakreṇa jīvaḥ sārdham sura adhipaiḥ sahasrākṣeṇa ca vibhum saṃprāpya āha kuśadhvajam saḥ upi tasya mukhāt śrutvā praṇayāt praṇata ārti hā devaiḥ aśeṣaiḥ sa iṃdraiḥ tu jīvam āha pitāmahaḥ jāne vā ūrtim suredrāṇām tathā api śṛṇu sāṃpratam vinindya dakṣa yā devī satī rudra aṅga saṃbhavā tasyāḥ ca eva iha rūpeṇa yūyam devāḥ sura uttamāḥ tayoḥ yogena saṃbhūtaḥ skandaḥ śakti dharaḥ prabhuḥ ṣaṣ āsyaḥ dvādaśa bhujaḥ senā nīḥ pāvakiḥ prabhuḥ svāheyaḥ kārtikeyaḥ ca gāṅgeyaḥ śaradhāmajaḥ līlayā eva mahāsenaḥ prabalam tāraka asuram sa śakram āha tam jīvam jagajjīvaḥ dvijottamāḥ smṛtaḥ yat bhavatā jīva saṃprāptaḥ ham tava antikam śaṅkareṇa aṃbikām adya saṃyojaya yathāsukham tena mārgeṇa mārgasva patnyā ratyā anayā saha saḥ upi tuṣṭaḥ mahādevaḥ pradāsyati śubhām gatim viprayuktaḥ tayā pūrvam tām girijām umām devadeva āśramam gantum matim cakre tayā saha gatvā tad aśraye śaṃbhoḥ saha ratyā mahā balaḥ adahat tad kṣaṇāt eva lalāpa karuṇam ratiḥ kṛpayā parayā prāha kāma patnīm nirībhya ca yadā viṣṇuḥ ca bhavitā vāsudevaḥ mahā yaśāḥ śāpāt bhṛgoḥ mahā tejāḥ sarva loka hitāya vai sā praṇamya tadā rudram kāmapatnī śuci smitā hitāya ca āśramāṇām ca krīḍā artham bhagavān bhavaḥ jagāma sa svayam brahmā marīci ādyaiḥ mahā ṛṣibhiḥ tapovanam mahādevyāḥ pārvatyāḥ padamsaṃbhavaḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya ca tām devīm sa jagatoraṇīm kīm artham tapasā lokān saṃtāpayasi śailaje saḥ evam parameśānaḥ svayam ca varayiṣyati vara de yena sṛṣṭā asi na vinā yaḥ tvayā aṃbike jagāma anugraham karttum dvija rūpeṇa ca āśramam sā ca dṛṣṭvā māhadevam dvija rūpeṇa saṃsthitam pratibhā ādyaiḥ prabhum jñātvā nanāma vṛṣabhadhvajam kula dharma āśrayam rakṣan bhūdharasya mahātmanaḥ krīḍā artham ca saṃtām madhye sarva deva patiḥ bhavaḥ āsthāya rūpam yat saumyam sameṣya iham saha tvayā iti uktvā tām samālokya devaḥ divyena cakṣuṣā jagāma iṣṭam tadā divyam sva puram prayayau ca sā dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭaḥ tadā devīm menayā tuhinācalaḥ āliṅgya āghrāya saṃpūjya putrīm sākṣāt tapasvinīm svayaṃvaram tadā devyāḥ sarva lokeṣu aghoṣayat vāyuḥ somaḥ tathā īśānaḥ rudrāḥ ca munayaḥ tathā aśvinau dvādaśa ādityāḥ gandharvāḥ garuḍaḥ tathā yakṣāḥ sādhyāḥ daityāḥ kiṃpuruṣa uragāḥ samudrāḥ ca nadāḥ vedāḥ mantrāḥ stotra ādayaḥ kṣaṇāḥ nāgāḥ ca parvatāḥ sarve yajñāḥ sūrya ādayaḥ grahāḥ trayastriṃśat ca devānām trayaḥ ca tri śatam tathā vandibhiḥ stūyamānā ca sthitā śailasutā tadā sita ātapatram ratna aṃśu miśritam ca avahat tathā mālinī giriputryāḥ tu saṃdhyā pūrṇa indu maṇḍalam vijayā vyajanam gṛhya sthitā devyāḥ samīpa gā śiśuḥ bhūtvā mahādevaḥ krīḍā artham pṛṣabhadhvajaḥ vahnim śaktim tathā kṣeptam na śaśāka tathā sthitaḥ yamaḥ api daṇḍam khaḍgam ca nirṛtiḥ muni puṅgavāḥ varuṇaḥ nāgapāśam ca dhvaja yaṣṭim samīraṇaḥ somaḥ gadām dhaneśaḥ ca daṇḍam daṇḍa bhṛtām varaḥ īśānaḥ ca tathā śūlam tīvram udyamya saṃsthitaḥ rudrāḥ ca śūlam ādityāḥ muśalam vasavaḥ tathā buddhiḥ tvam sarva lokānām ahaṅkāraḥ tvam īśvaraḥ bhūtānām indriyāṇām ca tvam eva īśa pravarttakaḥ tava aham dakṣiṇāt hastāt sṛṣṭaḥ pūrvam purātanaḥ vāma hastāt mahā bāho devaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ sūtaḥ uvāca saṃstantitān tadā tena bhagavān āha padmajaḥ sa nārāyaṇakāḥ sarve munibhiḥ śaṅkaram prabhum praṇemuḥ manasā sarve sa nārāyaṇakāḥ prabhum yathāpūrvam cakāra āśu vacanāt brahmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ tejasā tasya devāḥ te sa iṃdra candra divākarāḥ sa brahmakāḥ sa sādhyāḥ ca sa nārāyaṇakāḥ tathā sa yamāḥ ca sa rudāḥ ca cakṣura prāthayan vibhum dadau aṃbā patiḥ śarvaḥ bhavānyāḥ ca calasya ca labdhvā cakṣuḥ tadā devāḥ indra viṣṇu purogamāḥ munayaḥ ca mahādevam gaṇeśāḥ śiva saṃmatāḥ mumuhuḥ gaṇapāḥ sarvam mumoda aṃbā ca pārvatī pādayoḥ stāpayāmāsa mālām divyām sugandhinīm sūtaḥ uvāca yathā iṣṭam iti lokeśam prāha bhūtapatiḥ prabhuḥ udvāha artham maheśasya tad kṣaṇāt eva suvratāḥ brahmaṇā kalpitam divyam puram ratna mayam śubham atha aditiḥ ditiḥ sākṣāt danuḥ kadruḥ sukālikā siddhiḥ māyā kriyā durgā devī sākṣāt sudhā svadhā sāvitrī veda mātā ca rajanī dakṣiṇā dyutiḥ svāhā svāhā matiḥ vuddhiḥ ṛddhiḥ vṛddhiḥ sarasvatī dharaṇī dhāraṇī ca ilā śacī nārāyaṇī tathā vedāḥ mantrāḥ tathā yajñāḥ stomāḥ dharmāḥ ca sarvaśas huṅkāraḥ praṇavaḥ ca eva pratihārāḥ sahasraśas koṭiḥ apsarasaḥ divyāḥ tāsām ca paricārikāḥ yāḥ ca sarveṣu dvīpeṣu deva lokeṣu nimnagāḥ tāḥ ca strī vigrahāḥ sarvāḥ saṃjagmuḥ hṛṣṭa mānasāḥ karaṇaḥ ca eva viṃśatyā navatyā kevalaḥ śubhaḥ mṛtyu hṛt kāla hā kālaḥ mṛtyuñjaya karaḥ tathā viṣādaḥ viṣadaḥ ca eva vidyutaḥ kāntakaḥ prabhuḥ pātāla cāriṇaḥ ca eva sarva loka nivāsinaḥ girijām tām alaṅkṛtya svayam eva śuci smitām sadasi āha ca deveśam nārāyaṇam ajaḥ harim vāma aṅgāt asya rudrasya dakṣiṇa aṅgāt aham prabho mad mūrtiḥ tuhinādri īśaḥ yajña artham sṛṣṭaḥ eva hi śrauta smārta pravṛtti artham udvāha artham miha āgataḥ atas asau jagatām dhātrī dhātā tava mama api ca kṣmā ap agni kha indu sūrya ātma pavana ātmā yatas bhavaḥ eṣā hi ajā śukla kṛṣṇā lohitā prakṛtiḥ bhavān śreyaḥ api śailarājena saṃbandhaḥ ayam tava api ca mad aṃśasya asya śailasya mama api ca guruḥ bhavān sūtaḥ uvāca devāḥ ca munayaḥ sarve devadevaḥ ca śaṅkaraḥ tatas ca utthāya vidvān saḥ padmanābhaḥ praṇamya tām pādau prakṣālya devasya karābhyām kamala īkṣaṇaḥ tvadīyā eṣā vivāha artham menajā hi anujā mama iti uktvā sa udakam da tvā devīm deveśvarāya tām atha sarve muni śreṣṭhāḥ sarva veda artha pāragāḥ ūcuḥ dātā gṛhītā ca phalam dravyam vicārataḥ eṣa devaḥ haraḥ nūnam māyayā hi tatas jagat iti uktvā tam praṇemuḥ ca prīti kaṇṭākita tvacaḥ brahmaṇā munibhiḥ sārdham devadevam umāpatim devaḥ upi devīm ālokya sa lajjām himaśailajām na tṛpyati anavadya aṅgī sā ca devam vṛṣadhvajam vara daḥ asmi iti tam prāha harim saḥ upi āha śaṅkaram ānītān viṣṇunā viprāḥ saṃpūjya vividhaiḥ varaiḥ tatas pādyam tayoḥ datvā śaṃbhoḥ ācamanam tathā madhuparkam tathā gām ca praṇamya ca punar śivam bhṛgo adya munayaḥ sarve ca akṣataiḥ tila taṇḍulaiḥ sūrya ādayaḥ samabhyarcya tuṣṭuvuḥ vṛṣabhadhvajam śivaḥ samāpya deva uktam vahnim āropya ca ātmani śrāvayet vā dvijān śuddhān veda vedāṅga pāragān yatra ayam kīrtyate vipraiḥ tāvat āste tadā bhavaḥ tasmāt saṃpūjya vidhivat kīrtayet na ayathā dvijāḥ purīm vārāṇasīm divyām ājagāma mahā dyutiḥ apṛcchat kṣetra māhātmyam bhavānī harṣita ānanā vaktum mayā sureśāni ṛṣi saṃgha abhipūjitam pāpinām yatra muktiḥ syāt mṛtānām eka janmanā anyatra tu kṛtam pāpam vārāṇasyām vyapohati oṅkāra īśaḥ kṛttivāsāḥ mṛtānām na punarbhavaḥ uktvā kṣetrasya māhātmyam saṃkṣepāt śaśiśekharaḥ darśayāmāsa ca udyānam parityajya gaṇeśvarān yathāśrutam mayā sarvam prasādāt vaḥ su śobhanam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ katham vināyakaḥ jātaḥ gajavaktraḥ gaṇeśvaraḥ sūtaḥ uvāca dharma vighnam tadā karttum daityānām abhavan dvijāḥ tāmāsāḥ ca tathā ca anye rājasāḥ ca tathā bhuvi brahmāṇam ca harim viprāḥ labdha īpsita varāḥ yatas tatas asmākam sura śreṣṭhāḥ sadā vijaya saṃbhavaḥ putra artham ca eva nārīṇām narāṇām karma siddhaye vighneśam śaṅkaram sraṣṭum gaṇapam stotum arhatha iti uktvā anyonyam anagham tuṣṭuvuḥ śivam īśvaram anaghāya viriñcāya devyāḥ kārya artha dāyine akāyāya artha kāyāya hareḥ kāya apahāriṇe kāya antastha amṛta ādhāra maṇḍala avasthitāya te kṛta ādi bheda kālāya kālavegāya te namaḥ kālāgni rudra rupāya dharma ādi aṣṭa padāya ca kālī viśuddha dehāya kālikā kāraṇāya te kālakaṇṭhāya mukhyāya vāhanāya varāya te hiraṇyaretase ca eva namaḥ śarvāya śūline kapāla daṇḍa pāśa asi carma aṅkuśa dharāya ca pīta śuklāya rakṣā artham surāṇām kṛṣṇavartmane pañcamāya mahā pañca yajñinām phala dāya ca pañcāsya phaṇi hārāya pañcākṣara mayāya te ṣoḍaśa svara vajra aṅga vaktrāya akṣaya rūpiṇe pādi meṇḍhrāya yadi aṅga dhātu saptaka dhāriṇe śānta tma rūpiṇe sākṣāt kṣa danta krodhine namaḥ lava repha hala aṅgāya niraṅgāya ca te namaḥ sarvaṣām eva bhūtānām hṛdi niḥsvana kāriṇe bhruveḥ ante sadā sadbhiḥ dṛṣṭāya atyanta bhānave guṇatraya upari sthāya tīrtha pādāya te namaḥ tīrtha tattvāya sārāya tasmāt api parāya te ṛk jayuḥ sāmavedāya oṃkārāya namaḥ namaḥ oṅkāre trividham rūpam āsthāya upari vāsine pītāya kṛṣṇa varṇāya raktāya atyanta tejase sthāna pañcaka saṃsthāya pañcadhā aṇḍa bahis kramāt aṃbāyāḥ parameśāya sarva upari cāraya te sarva saṃkalpa śūnyāya sarvasmāt rakṣitāya te ādi madhya anta śūnyāya cit saṃsthāya namaḥ namaḥ yama agni vāyu rudra aṃbu soma śakra niśācaraiḥ diṅmukha udiṅmukhe nityam sa gaṇaiḥ pūjitāya te sarvaṣu sarvadā sarva mārge saṃpūjitāya te maheśvarāya dhīrāya namaḥ sākṣāt śivāya te makha madana yama agni dakṣa yajña kṣapaṇa vicitra vicoṣṭitam kṣamasva sūtaḥ uvāca yaḥ paṭhet tu stavam bhakyā śakra agni pramukhaiḥ suraiḥ sūtaḥ uvāca yadā sthitāḥ sura īśvarāḥ praṇamya ca evam īśvaram tadām bikāpatiḥ bhavaḥ pinākadhṛk maheśvaraḥ dadau nirīkṣaṇam kṣaṇāt bhavaḥ sa tān sura uttamān praṇemuḥ ādarāt haram surāḥ mudā ārdra locanāḥ bhavaḥ sudhā amṛta upamaiḥ nirīkṣaṇaiḥ nirīkṣaṇāt tat āha bhadram astu vaḥ sura īśvarān maheśvaraḥ vara artham īśa vīkṣyate surāḥ gṛham gatāḥ tu ime praṇamya ca āha vākpatiḥ patim nirīkṣya nirbhayaḥ surā pakāra kāriṇām iha eṣaḥ eva naḥ varaḥ tatas tadā niśamya vai pinākadhṛk sureśvaraḥ gaṇeśvaram sureśvaram vapuḥ dadhāra saḥ śivaḥ gaṇeśvarāḥ ca tuṣṭuvuḥ surevarāḥ maheśvaram samasta loka saṃbhavam bhava ārtti hāriṇam śubham ibha ānana āśritam varam triśula pāśa dhāriṇam samasta loka saṃbhavam gajānanam tadā aṃbikā daduḥ puṣpa varṣam hi siddhāḥ muni indrāḥ tathā khecarāḥ deva saṃghāḥ tadānīm tadā tuṣṭuvuḥ ca ekadantam sura īśāḥ praṇemuḥ gaṇeśam maheśam vitandrāḥ vicitra vastra bhūṣaṇaiḥ alaṅkṛtaḥ gajānano jāta mātram sutam dṛṣṭvā cakāra bhagavān bhavaḥ gajānanāya kṛtyān tu sarvān sarveśvaraḥ svayam ādāya ca karābhyām ca su sukhābhyām bhavaḥ svayam āliṅgya āghrāya mūrdhānam mahādevaḥ jagadguruḥ tava avatāraḥ daityānām vināśāya mama ātmaja yajñaḥ ca dakṣiṇā hīnaḥ kṛtaḥ yena mahī tale yaḥ anyāyataḥ karoti asmin tasya prāṇān sadā hara varṇāt cyutānām nārīṇām narāṇām nara puṅgava svadharma rahitānām ca prāṇān apahara prabho yāḥ striyaḥ tvām sadā kālam puruṣāḥ ca vināyaka yajanti tāsām teṣām ca tvad sāmyam dātum arhasi yauvana sthān ca vṛddhān ca iha amutra ca pūjitaḥ saṃpūjyaḥ vandanīyaḥ ca bhaviṣyasi na saśaṃyaḥ mām ca nārāyaṇam vā api putraka tvām an abhyarcya kalyāṇam śrautam smārtam ca laukikam kurute tasya kalyāṇam akalyāṇam bhaviṣyati saṃpūjya sarva siddhi artham bhakṣya bhojya ādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ ajam harim ca mām vā api śakram anyān surān api sasarja ca tadā vighna gaṇam gaṇapatiḥ prabhuḥ gaṇaiḥ sārdham namaskṛtvā api atiṣṭhat tasya ca agratas daityānām dharma vighnam ca cakāra asau gaṇeśvaraḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ nṛtya āraṃbhaḥ katham śaṃbhoḥ kimartham vā yathātatham vaktum arhasi ca asmākam śrutaḥ skanda agraja udbhavaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca sūdayāmāsa kālāgniḥ iva devān dvijottamān dārukeṇa tadā devāḥ tāḍitāḥ pīḍitāḥ bhṛśam brahmāṇam ca tathā īśānam kumāram viṣṇum eva ca bādhitāḥ tena te sarve brahmāṇam prāpya vai dvijāḥ dāruṇaḥ bhagavān dāruḥ pūrvam tena vinirjitāḥ bhavatīm prārthayāmi adya hitāya jagatām śubhe ekena aṃśena deveśam praviṣṭāḥ deva sattamam girijām pūrvavat śaṃbhoḥ dṛṣṭvā pārśva sthitām śubhām māyayā mohitaḥ tasyāḥ sarvajñaḥ api caturmukhaḥ sā praviṣṭā atanum tasya devadevasya pārvatī kaṇṭha sthena viṣeṇa asya tanum cakre tadā ātmanaḥ tām ca jñātvā tathā bhūtām tṛtīyena īkṣaṇena vai sasarja kālīm kāmāriḥ kālakaṇṭhīm kapardinīm jātā yadā kālikā alakaṇṭhī jātā tadānīm vipulā jaya śrīḥ devetarāṇām ajayaḥ tu asiddhyā tuṣṭiḥ bhavānyāḥ parameśvarasya jātām tadānīm sura siddha saṃghāḥ dṛṣṭvā bhayāt dudruvuḥ āgni kalpām kālīm gara alaṅkṛta kāla kaṇṭhīm upedandra padmodbhava śakramukhyāḥ tathā eva jātam nayanam lalāṭe sitāṃśu lekhā ca sirasi udagrā kaṇṭhe karālam niśitam triśūlam kare karālam ca vibhūṣaṇāni siddha indra siddhāḥ ca tathā piśācāḥ jajñire punar ājñayā dārukam tasyāḥ pārvatyāḥ parameśvarī dānavam sūdayāmāsa sūdayantam sura adhipān saṃraṃbha ati prasaṃgāt vai tasyāḥ sarvam idam jagat krodha agninā ca vipra indrāḥ saṃbabhūva tadā āturam bhavaḥ api bāla rūpeṇa śmaśāne preta saṃkule ruroda māyayā tasyāḥ krodha agnim pātum īsvaraḥ tam dṛṣṭvā bālam īśānam māyayā tasya mohitā utthāpya āghrāya vakṣojam stanam sā pradadau dvijāḥ stana jena tadā sārdham kopam asyāḥ papau punar krodhena anena vai bālaḥ kṣetrāṇām rakṣakaḥ abhavat mūrtayaḥ aṣṭau ca tasya api kṣetrapālasya dhīmataḥ evam vai tena bālena kṛtā sā krodha mūrcchitā kṛtam asyāḥ prasāda artham devadevena tāṇḍavam saṃdhyāyām sarva bhūta indraiḥ pretaiḥ prītena śūlinā pītvā nṛtya amṛtam śaṃbhoḥ ākaṇṭham parameśvarī praṇemuḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ kālīm punar devīm ca pārvatīm evam saṃkṣepataḥ proktam tāṇḍavam śūlinaḥ prabhoḥ yoga ānandena ca vibhoḥ tāṇḍavam ca iti ca apare ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ purā upamanyunā sūta gāṇapatyam maheśvarāt kṣīrārṇavaḥ katham labdhaḥ vaktum arhasi sāṃpratam sūtaḥ uvāca kumāraḥ iva tejasvī krīḍamānaḥ yadṛcchayā sūtaḥ uvāca sūtaḥ uvāca tam āha mātā śuśubham kurvi iti sutarām sutam prāptaḥ śakraḥ jagannāthaḥ bhagavān bhānunā prabhuḥ prāha sa vyagram īśānaḥ śakra rūpa dharaḥ svayam mām na jānāsi deva ṛṣe deva rājānam īśvaram tatas śakrasya vacanam śrutvā śrotra vidāraṇam upamanyuḥ idam prāha japan pañcākṣara śubham sva deham tam nihatya āśu śiva lokam sa gacchati yaḥ vācā utpāṭayot jihvām śiva nindā ratasya tu purā mātrā tu kathitam tatthyam eva na saṃśayaḥ bhasma ādhārāt mahā tejāḥ bhasma muṣṭim pragṛhya ca dagdhum svada iha mā āgneyīm dhyātvā vai dhāraṇām tadā atiṣṭhat ca mahā tejāḥ śuṣka indhanam iva avyayaḥ atharva astram tadā tasya saṃhṛtam candrikeṇa tu kāla agni sadṛśam ca idam niyogāt nandinaḥ tathā apūpa girayaḥ ca eva tathā atiṣṭhan samantataḥ girijā avalokya sa smitām sa ghṛṇam prekṣyatu tam tadā ghṛṇī apūpa girayaḥ ca eva bhakṣya bhojya arṇavaḥ punar pitā tava mahādevaḥ pitā vai jagatām mune mātā tava mahābhāgā jaganmātā na saṃśayaḥ saḥ upi labdhvā varam tasyāḥ kumāra tvam ca sarvadā varayāmāsa ca tadā vareṇyam viraja īkṣaṇam prasīda devadeveśa tvayi ca avyabhicāriṇī ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ katham labdham tadā jñānam tasmāt kṛṣṇena dhīmatā vaktum arhasi tām sūta kathām pātaka nāśinīm sūtaḥ uvāca namaścakāra tam dṛṣṭvā dhaumya agrajam aho dvijāḥ divyam pāśupatam jñānam pradadau prīta mānasaḥ muneḥ prasādāt mānyaḥ asau kṛṣṇaḥ pāśupate dvijāḥ divyāḥ pāśupatāḥ sarve tasthuḥ saṃvṛtya sarvadā anyam ca kathayiṣyāmi mukti artham prāṇinām sadā sauvarṇīm mekhalām kṛtvā ādhāram daṇḍa dhāraṇam sauvarṇam piṇḍikam ca api vyajanam daṇḍam eva ca naraiḥ striyā atha vā kāryam maṣī bhājana lekhanīm ca api atha pātram atha api vā ātma vitta anusāreṇa yoginam pūjayet budhaḥ te sarve pāpa nirmuktāḥ samasta kula saṃyutāḥ yoginām saṃpradānena śivaḥ kṣipram prasīdati rājyam putram dhanam bhavyam aśvam yānam atha api vā
